```
Parameter | Value | Unit | Description
Span_Count | 3 | nos | Number of spans
Span_Length_1 | 30.00 | m | First span length
Span_Length_2 | 30.00 | m | Second span length
Carriageway_Width | 12.00 | m | Clear carriageway
Overall_Width | 13.50 | m | Including kerbs
Skew_Angle | 15 | degrees | If applicable
```

#### Sheet 3: SUPERSTRUCTURE
```
Type | Value | Unit
Superstructure_Type | T-Beam | - | Options: T-Beam, Box_Girder, Slab, PSC
Deck_Thickness | 250 | mm
Wearing_Coat | 65 | mm
Parapet_Type | RCC | - | Options: RCC, Metal, Crash_Barrier
```

#### Sheet 4: SUBSTRUCTURE
```
Component | Type | Foundation_Type | Dimensions
Pier | Wall_Type | Pile | 2.0m x 8.0m
Abutment | Cantilever | Well | 6.0m dia
```

#### Sheet 5: FOUNDATION_DETAILS
```
Foundation_Type | Depth | Diameter/Size | Cutoff_Level
Pile | 15.0 | 1.2m | -2.5m
Well | 12.0 | 6.0m | -3.0m
```

#### Sheet 6: APPROACHES
```
Left_Approach_Length | Right_Approach_Length | Retaining_Wall_Type
30.00 | 30.00 | Cantilever_RCC
```


## COMPLETE PYTHON MODULE: Bridge Plan View Generator

### File 1: `bridge_geometry.py` - Core Geometry Engine

```python
"""
Bridge Geometry Calculation Engine
Handles coordinate calculations for bridge plan views including skew bridges
"""

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Tuple, Optional


@dataclass
class BridgeParameters:
    """Bridge geometric parameters from Excel input"""
    bridge_name: str
    chainage: float
    span_lengths: List[float]  # List of span lengths in meters
    carriageway_width: float
    overall_width: float
    skew_angle: float  # Degrees, 0 = square, positive = clockwise
    pier_width: float
    pier_length: float
    abutment_width: float
    abutment_length: float
    bearing_width: float = 0.4  # Default 400mm
    kerb_width: float = 0.75
    
    @property
    def total_length(self) -> float:
        """Calculate total bridge length"""
        return sum(self.span_lengths)
    
    @property
    def span_count(self) -> int:
        return len(self.span_lengths)


@dataclass
class Point:
    """2D Point with x, y coordinates"""
    x: float
    y: float
    
    def offset(self, dx: float, dy: float) -> 'Point':
        return Point(self.x + dx, self.y + dy)
    
    def rotate(self, angle_deg: float, origin: 'Point' = None) -> 'Point':
        """Rotate point around origin by given angle in degrees"""
        if origin is None:
            origin = Point(0, 0)
        
        angle_rad = math.radians(angle_deg)
        dx = self.x - origin.x
        dy = self.y - origin.y
        
        new_x = origin.x + dx * math.cos(angle_rad) - dy * math.sin(angle_rad)
        new_y = origin.y + dx * math.sin(angle_rad) + dy * math.cos(angle_rad)
        
        return Point(new_x, new_y)


class BridgeGeometryCalculator:
    """Calculate all geometric elements for bridge plan view"""
    
    def __init__(self, params: BridgeParameters):
        self.params = params
        self.skew_rad = math.radians(params.skew_angle)
        
    def calculate_pier_positions(self) -> List[Point]:
        """Calculate center points of all piers"""
        positions = []
        current_chainage = 0
        
        # First pier (after first span)
        for i, span in enumerate(self.params.span_lengths[:-1]):
            current_chainage += span
            positions.append(Point(current_chainage, 0))
            
        return positions
    
    def calculate_abutment_positions(self) -> Tuple[Point, Point]:
        """Calculate center points of left and right abutments"""
        left_abut = Point(0, 0)
        right_abut = Point(self.params.total_length, 0)
        return left_abut, right_abut
    
    def get_deck_corners(self, center: Point, width: float, length: float) -> List[Point]:
        """
        Get four corners of a rectangular element (deck, pier, abutment)
        Accounts for skew angle
        """
        half_width = width / 2
        half_length = length / 2
        
        # Square corners relative to center
        corners = [
            Point(-half_length, -half_width),  # Bottom-left
            Point(half_length, -half_width),   # Bottom-right
            Point(half_length, half_width),    # Top-right
            Point(-half_length, half_width),   # Top-left
        ]
        
        # Rotate each corner by skew angle and translate to center
        rotated_corners = []
        for corner in corners:
            rotated = corner.rotate(self.params.skew_angle)
            rotated_corners.append(Point(
                rotated.x + center.x,
                rotated.y + center.y
            ))
        
        return rotated_corners
    
    def get_deck_outline(self) -> List[Point]:
        """Get the four corners of the bridge deck"""
        center = Point(self.params.total_length / 2, 0)
        return self.get_deck_corners(center, self.params.overall_width, self.params.total_length)
    
    def get_carriageway_outline(self) -> List[Point]:
        """Get the four corners of the carriageway"""
        center = Point(self.params.total_length / 2, 0)
        return self.get_deck_corners(center, self.params.carriageway_width, self.params.total_length)
    
    def get_pier_outline(self, pier_center: Point) -> List[Point]:
        """Get corners of a pier cap"""
        return self.get_deck_corners(
            pier_center, 
            self.params.pier_length, 
            self.params.pier_width
        )
    
    def get_abutment_outline(self, abut_center: Point) -> List[Point]:
        """Get corners of an abutment"""
        return self.get_deck_corners(
            abut_center,
            self.params.abutment_length,
            self.params.abutment_width
        )
    
    def get_bearing_positions(self, support_center: Point) -> List[Point]:
        """Calculate bearing positions at a support (pier or abutment)"""
        bearing_spacing = self.params.carriageway_width / 3  # 3 bearings per support
        positions = []
        
        for i in range(-1, 2):  # -1, 0, 1 for 3 bearings
            offset_y = i * bearing_spacing
            bearing_point = Point(support_center.x, support_center.y + offset_y)
            # Rotate for skew
            rotated = Point(0, offset_y).rotate(self.params.skew_angle)
            positions.append(Point(
                support_center.x + rotated.x,
                support_center.y + rotated.y
            ))
        
        return positions
    
    def get_chainage_points(self, interval: float = 10.0) -> List[Tuple[float, Point]]:
        """Generate chainage points along the bridge centerline"""
        points = []
        num_points = int(self.params.total_length / interval) + 1
        
        for i in range(num_points):
            chainage = i * interval
            if chainage <= self.params.total_length:
                point = Point(chainage, 0)
                points.append((chainage, point))
        
        # Add end point
        if self.params.total_length % interval != 0:
            points.append((self.params.total_length, Point(self.params.total_length, 0)))
        
        return points
    
    def get_dimension_lines(self) -> dict:
        """Calculate positions for dimension lines"""
        # Overall dimension
        deck_corners = self.get_deck_outline()
        min_y = min(p.y for p in deck_corners)
        
        return {
            'overall_dim_y': min_y - 3.0,  # 3m below deck
            'span_dim_y': min_y - 1.5,
            'overall_text_y': min_y - 4.0
        }
```

---

### File 2: `excel_reader.py` - Excel Input Handler

```python
"""
Excel Input Reader
Reads bridge parameters from standardized Excel template
"""

import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Any
from bridge_geometry import BridgeParameters


class ExcelBridgeReader:
    """Read and parse bridge data from Excel file"""
    
    def __init__(self, excel_path: str):
        self.excel_path = excel_path
        self.raw_data = {}
        
    def read_all_sheets(self) -> Dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
        """Read all sheets from Excel file"""
        excel_file = pd.ExcelFile(self.excel_path)
        
        for sheet_name in excel_file.sheet_names:
            self.raw_data[sheet_name] = pd.read_excel(
                self.excel_path, 
                sheet_name=sheet_name
            )
        
        return self.raw_data
    
    def parse_geometry_params(self) -> BridgeParameters:
        """Parse GEOMETRY_PARAMETERS sheet"""
        if 'GEOMETRY_PARAMETERS' not in self.raw_data:
            raise ValueError("GEOMETRY_PARAMETERS sheet not found")
        
        df = self.raw_data['GEOMETRY_PARAMETERS']
        
        # Convert to dictionary for easy access
        params_dict = dict(zip(df['Parameter'], df['Value']))
        
        # Parse span lengths (can be multiple)
        span_lengths = []
        i = 1
        while f'Span_Length_{i}' in params_dict:
            span_lengths.append(float(params_dict[f'Span_Length_{i}']))
            i += 1
        
        # If no numbered spans, check for single Span_Length
        if not span_lengths and 'Span_Length' in params_dict:
            span_lengths = [float(params_dict['Span_Length'])]
        
        return BridgeParameters(
            bridge_name=str(self.raw_data.get('PROJECT_MASTER', {}).get('Bridge_Name', ['Unknown'])[0]),
            chainage=float(params_dict.get('Chainage', 0)),
            span_lengths=span_lengths,
            carriageway_width=float(params_dict.get('Carriageway_Width', 7.5)),
            overall_width=float(params_dict.get('Overall_Width', 9.0)),
            skew_angle=float(params_dict.get('Skew_Angle', 0)),
            pier_width=float(params_dict.get('Pier_Width', 1.5)),
            pier_length=float(params_dict.get('Pier_Length', 8.0)),
            abutment_width=float(params_dict.get('Abutment_Width', 1.2)),
            abutment_length=float(params_dict.get('Abutment_Length', 9.0)),
            bearing_width=float(params_dict.get('Bearing_Width', 0.4)),
            kerb_width=float(params_dict.get('Kerb_Width', 0.75))
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_sample_excel(output_path: str = "bridge_template.xlsx"):
        """Create a sample Excel template for user input"""
        
        # Project Master
        project_data = {
            'Project_Code': ['BRG-2024-001'],
            'Bridge_Name': ['Shiwalay Major Bridge'],
            'Chainage': [1250.500],
            'Road_Level': [185.500],
            'Ground_Level': [175.200],
            'Survey_Date': ['2024-01-15']
        }
        
        # Geometry Parameters
        geometry_data = {
            'Parameter': [
                'Span_Count', 'Span_Length_1', 'Span_Length_2', 'Span_Length_3',
                'Carriageway_Width', 'Overall_Width', 'Skew_Angle',
                'Pier_Width', 'Pier_Length', 'Abutment_Width', 'Abutment_Length',
                'Bearing_Width', 'Kerb_Width'
            ],
            'Value': [
                3, 30.0, 30.0, 30.0,  # 3 spans of 30m each
                12.0, 13.5, 15.0,      # 12m carriageway, 15° skew
                2.0, 10.0, 1.5, 11.0,  # Pier and abutment sizes
                0.4, 0.75               # Bearing and kerb
            ],
            'Unit': [
                'nos', 'm', 'm', 'm',
                'm', 'm', 'degrees',
                'm', 'm', 'm', 'm',
                'm', 'm'
            ],
            'Description': [
                'Number of spans', 'First span length', 'Second span length', 
                'Third span length', 'Clear carriageway width', 'Overall width including kerbs',
                'Skew angle (0 = square)', 'Pier cap width', 'Pier cap length',
                'Abutment width', 'Abutment length', 'Bearing width', 'Kerb width'
            ]
        }
        
        # Superstructure
        superstructure_data = {
            'Parameter': ['Type', 'Deck_Thickness', 'Wearing_Coat', 'Parapet_Type'],
            'Value': ['T-Beam', 250, 65, 'RCC'],
            'Unit': ['-', 'mm', 'mm', '-'],
            'Description': ['Superstructure type', 'Deck slab thickness', 
                          'Wearing coat thickness', 'Parapet type']
        }
        
        with pd.ExcelWriter(output_path, engine='openpyxl') as writer:
            pd.DataFrame(project_data).to_excel(writer, sheet_name='PROJECT_MASTER', index=False)
            pd.DataFrame(geometry_data).to_excel(writer, sheet_name='GEOMETRY_PARAMETERS', index=False)
            pd.DataFrame(superstructure_data).to_excel(writer, sheet_name='SUPERSTRUCTURE', index=False)
        
        print(f"Sample Excel template created: {output_path}")
        return output_path
```

---

### File 3: `plan_view_drawing.py` - DXF Drawing Generator

```python
"""
Plan View Drawing Generator
Creates professional bridge GAD plan view using ezdxf
"""

import ezdxf
from ezdxf.enums import TextEntityAlignment
from ezdxf.gfxattribs import GfxAttribs
from bridge_geometry import BridgeGeometryCalculator, BridgeParameters, Point


class PlanViewDrawing:
    """Generate Bridge Plan View Drawing in DXF format"""
    
    # Layer definitions
    LAYERS = {
        'DECK': {'color': 1, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.30},      # Red
        'CARRIAGEWAY': {'color': 2, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.25}, # Yellow
        'PIER': {'color': 4, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.35},      # Cyan
        'ABUTMENT': {'color': 3, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.35},  # Green
        'BEARING': {'color': 5, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.18},   # Blue
        'CENTERLINE': {'color': 6, 'linetype': 'CENTER', 'lineweight': 0.18},    # Magenta
        'DIMENSION': {'color': 8, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.18}, # Grey
        'TEXT': {'color': 7, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.18},      # White/Black
        'ANNOTATION': {'color': 9, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.18}, # Grey
        'CONSTRUCTION': {'color': 253, 'linetype': 'DASHED', 'lineweight': 0.13} # Light grey
    }
    
    def __init__(self, params: BridgeParameters):
        self.params = params
        self.calc = BridgeGeometryCalculator(params)
        self.doc = ezdxf.new('R2018', setup=True)
        self.msp = self.doc.modelspace()
        self.setup_layers()
        self.setup_styles()
        
    def setup_layers(self):
        """Create all required layers with proper attributes"""
        for name, attrs in self.LAYERS.items():
            self.doc.layers.add(
                name=name,
                color=attrs['color'],
                linetype=attrs['linetype'],
                lineweight=attrs['lineweight']
            )
    
    def setup_styles(self):
        """Setup text and dimension styles"""
        # Text style
        self.doc.styles.add('BRIDGE_FONT', font='Arial.ttf')
        
        # Dimension style
        dimstyle = self.doc.dimstyles.add('BRIDGE_DIM')
        dimstyle.dxf.dimtxt = 0.25  # Text height
        dimstyle.dxf.dimexe = 0.18  # Extension beyond dimension line
        dimstyle.dxf.dimexo = 0.15  # Extension line offset
        dimstyle.dxf.dimtad = 1     # Text above dimension line
        dimstyle.dxf.dimdec = 2     # Decimal places
        
    def add_polyline(self, points: list, layer: str, closed: bool = True):
        """Add a polyline from list of Point objects"""
        if len(points) < 2:
            return
            
        points_3d = [(p.x, p.y, 0) for p in points]
        
        if closed:
            points_3d.append(points_3d[0])  # Close the loop
            
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=points_3d,
            close=closed,
            dxfattribs={'layer': layer}
        )
    
    def add_text(self, text: str, position: Point, height: float = 0.25, 
                 layer: str = 'TEXT', rotation: float = 0):
        """Add text annotation"""
        self.msp.add_text(
            text,
            height=height,
            dxfattribs={
                'layer': layer,
                'style': 'BRIDGE_FONT',
                'rotation': rotation
            }
        ).set_placement(
            (position.x, position.y),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
    
    def add_dimension(self, p1: Point, p2: Point, location: Point, 
                      text: str = None, layer: str = 'DIMENSION'):
        """Add linear dimension"""
        dim = self.msp.add_linear_dim(
            base=(location.x, location.y),
            p1=(p1.x, p1.y),
            p2=(p2.x, p2.y),
            dxfattribs={'layer': layer}
        )
        if text:
            dim.override().set_text(text)
        dim.render()
    
    def draw_deck(self):
        """Draw bridge deck outline"""
        corners = self.calc.get_deck_outline()
        self.add_polyline(corners, 'DECK', closed=True)
        
        # Add hatching for deck (optional)
        hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=1, dxfattribs={'layer': 'DECK'})
        hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(
            [(p.x, p.y) for p in corners],
            is_closed=True
        )
        hatch.set_pattern_fill('ANSI31', scale=0.5)
    
    def draw_carriageway(self):
        """Draw carriageway outline"""
        corners = self.calc.get_carriageway_outline()
        self.add_polyline(corners, 'CARRIAGEWAY', closed=True)
    
    def draw_centerline(self):
        """Draw bridge centerline"""
        start = Point(0, 0)
        end = Point(self.params.total_length, 0)
        
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=(start.x, start.y),
            end=(end.x, end.y),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        
        # Add chainage annotations along centerline
        chainage_points = self.calc.get_chainage_points(interval=10.0)
        for chainage, point in chainage_points:
            # Tick mark perpendicular to centerline
            perp_angle = self.params.skew_angle + 90
            tick_start = point
            tick_vec = Point(0, 0.5).rotate(perp_angle)
            tick_end = point.offset(tick_vec.x, tick_vec.y)
            
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=(tick_start.x, tick_start.y),
                end=(tick_end.x, tick_end.y),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
            )
            
            # Chainage text
            text_pos = tick_end.offset(0, 0.3)
            chainage_text = f"CH: {self.params.chainage + chainage:.3f}"
            self.add_text(chainage_text, text_pos, height=0.20, rotation=self.params.skew_angle)
    
    def draw_piers(self):
        """Draw all piers"""
        pier_centers = self.calc.calculate_pier_positions()
        
        for i, center in enumerate(pier_centers, 1):
            # Draw pier outline
            corners = self.calc.get_pier_outline(center)
            self.add_polyline(corners, 'PIER', closed=True)
            
            # Add hatching
            hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=4, dxfattribs={'layer': 'PIER'})
            hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(
                [(p.x, p.y) for p in corners],
                is_closed=True
            )
            hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.3)
            
            # Pier label
            label_pos = center.offset(0, self.params.pier_length/2 + 1.0)
            self.add_text(f"P-{i}", label_pos, height=0.30, layer='ANNOTATION')
            
            # Draw bearings
            bearing_positions = self.calc.get_bearing_positions(center)
            for j, bearing_pos in enumerate(bearing_positions, 1):
                # Bearing rectangle
                b_half_w = self.params.bearing_width / 2
                b_half_l = 0.3  # 300mm bearing length
                bearing_corners = [
                    bearing_pos.offset(-b_half_l, -b_half_w).rotate(self.params.skew_angle, bearing_pos),
                    bearing_pos.offset(b_half_l, -b_half_w).rotate(self.params.skew_angle, bearing_pos),
                    bearing_pos.offset(b_half_l, b_half_w).rotate(self.params.skew_angle, bearing_pos),
                    bearing_pos.offset(-b_half_l, b_half_w).rotate(self.params.skew_angle, bearing_pos)
                ]
                self.add_polyline(bearing_corners, 'BEARING', closed=True)
    
    def draw_abutments(self):
        """Draw left and right abutments"""
        left_abut, right_abut = self.calc.calculate_abutment_positions()
        
        for abut_name, center in [('A-1', left_abut), ('A-2', right_abut)]:
            # Draw abutment outline
            corners = self.calc.get_abutment_outline(center)
            self.add_polyline(corners, 'ABUTMENT', closed=True)
            
            # Add hatching
            hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=3, dxfattribs={'layer': 'ABUTMENT'})
            hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(
                [(p.x, p.y) for p in corners],
                is_closed=True
            )
            hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.3)
            
            # Abutment label
            label_offset = -self.params.abutment_length/2 - 1.5 if '1' in abut_name else self.params.abutment_length/2 + 1.5
            label_pos = center.offset(0, label_offset)
            self.add_text(abut_name, label_pos, height=0.30, layer='ANNOTATION')
            
            # Draw bearings
            bearing_positions = self.calc.get_bearing_positions(center)
            for bearing_pos in bearing_positions:
                b_half_w = self.params.bearing_width / 2
                b_half_l = 0.3
                bearing_corners = [
                    bearing_pos.offset(-b_half_l, -b_half_w).rotate(self.params.skew_angle, bearing_pos),
                    bearing_pos.offset(b_half_l, -b_half_w).rotate(self.params.skew_angle, bearing_pos),
                    bearing_pos.offset(b_half_l, b_half_w).rotate(self.params.skew_angle, bearing_pos),
                    bearing_pos.offset(-b_half_l, b_half_w).rotate(self.params.skew_angle, bearing_pos)
                ]
                self.add_polyline(bearing_corners, 'BEARING', closed=True)
    
    def draw_dimensions(self):
        """Add all dimensions"""
        deck_corners = self.calc.get_deck_outline()
        dim_positions = self.calc.get_dimension_lines()
        
        # Overall length dimension
        left_point = Point(0, -self.params.overall_width/2 - 1.0).rotate(self.params.skew_angle)
        right_point = Point(self.params.total_length, -self.params.overall_width/2 - 1.0).rotate(self.params.skew_angle)
        dim_loc = Point(
            (left_point.x + right_point.x) / 2,
            min(left_point.y, right_point.y) - 3.0
        )
        
        self.add_dimension(
            Point(0, 0),
            Point(self.params.total_length, 0),
            dim_loc,
            text=f"TOTAL LENGTH = {self.params.total_length:.2f}m"
        )
        
        # Span dimensions
        pier_centers = self.calc.calculate_pier_positions()
        left_abut, right_abut = self.calc.calculate_abutment_positions()
        
        # Dimension from left abutment to first pier
        if pier_centers:
            span_dim_y = -self.params.overall_width/2 - 1.5
            self.add_dimension(
                left_abut,
                pier_centers[0],
                Point((left_abut.x + pier_centers[0].x)/2, span_dim_y),
                text=f"SPAN-1 = {self.params.span_lengths[0]:.2f}m"
            )
            
            # Intermediate spans
            for i in range(len(pier_centers) - 1):
                self.add_dimension(
                    pier_centers[i],
                    pier_centers[i + 1],
                    Point((pier_centers[i].x + pier_centers[i+1].x)/2, span_dim_y),
                    text=f"SPAN-{i+2} = {self.params.span_lengths[i+1]:.2f}m"
                )
            
            # Last span
            self.add_dimension(
                pier_centers[-1],
                right_abut,
                Point((pier_centers[-1].x + right_abut.x)/2, span_dim_y),
                text=f"SPAN-{len(pier_centers)+1} = {self.params.span_lengths[-1]:.2f}m"
            )
        
        # Width dimension
        deck_left = min(p.x for p in deck_corners)
        deck_right = max(p.x for p in deck_corners)
        deck_bottom = min(p.y for p in deck_corners)
        
        self.add_dimension(
            Point(deck_left, deck_bottom),
            Point(deck_left, deck_bottom + self.params.overall_width),
            Point(deck_left - 3.0, deck_bottom + self.params.overall_width/2),
            text=f"WIDTH = {self.params.overall_width:.2f}m"
        )
    
    def draw_title_block(self):
        """Add title block and drawing information"""
        # Get drawing extents
        deck_corners = self.calc.get_deck_outline()
        min_x = min(p.x for p in deck_corners) - 10
        max_x = max(p.x for p in deck_corners) + 10
        min_y = min(p.y for p in deck_corners) - 15
        max_y = max(p.y for p in deck_corners) + 5
        
        # Title block rectangle
        title_block = [
            Point(min_x, min_y),
            Point(max_x, min_y),
            Point(max_x, min_y + 4),
            Point(min_x, min_y + 4)
        ]
        self.add_polyline(title_block, 'TEXT', closed=True)
        
        # Title block lines
        self.msp.add_line((min_x, min_y + 2), (max_x, min_y + 2), dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'})
        self.msp.add_line((min_x + (max_x-min_x)*0.6, min_y), (min_x + (max_x-min_x)*0.6, min_y + 4), dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'})
        
        # Text annotations
        self.add_text(
            f"PLAN OF BRIDGE - {self.params.bridge_name}",
            Point(min_x + 2, min_y + 3),
            height=0.35,
            layer='TEXT'
        )
        self.add_text(
            f"CHAINAGE: {self.params.chainage:.3f} | SPANS: {self.params.span_count} @ {self.params.span_lengths[0]:.1f}m",
            Point(min_x + 2, min_y + 1),
            height=0.25,
            layer='TEXT'
        )
        self.add_text(
            f"SKEW: {self.params.skew_angle}° | WIDTH: {self.params.overall_width}m",
            Point(min_x + 2, min_y + 0.5),
            height=0.20,
            layer='TEXT'
        )
        
        # Scale and date
        self.add_text(
            "SCALE: 1:100",
            Point(max_x - 2, min_y + 3),
            height=0.25,
            layer='TEXT'
        )
        self.add_text(
            "DATE: " + pd.Timestamp.now().strftime('%d-%m-%Y'),
            Point(max_x - 2, min_y + 1),
            height=0.20,
            layer='TEXT'
        )
        
        # North arrow
        north_x = max_x - 3
        north_y = max_y - 2
        arrow_points = [
            Point(north_x, north_y),
            Point(north_x - 0.5, north_y - 1.5),
            Point(north_x, north_y - 1.0),
            Point(north_x + 0.5, north_y - 1.5)
        ]
        self.add_polyline(arrow_points, 'TEXT', closed=True)
        self.add_text("N", Point(north_x, north_y + 0.5), height=0.30, layer='TEXT')
    
    def generate(self, output_path: str = "bridge_plan_view.dxf"):
        """Generate complete plan view drawing"""
        print(f"Generating plan view for: {self.params.bridge_name}")
        print(f"Bridge length: {self.params.total_length}m, Spans: {self.params.span_count}")
        
        # Draw all components
        self.draw_deck()
        self.draw_carriageway()
        self.draw_centerline()
        self.draw_piers()
        self.draw_abutments()
        self.draw_dimensions()
        self.draw_title_block()
        
        # Save file
        self.doc.saveas(output_path)
        print(f"Drawing saved: {output_path}")
        return output_path
```

---

### File 4: `main.py` - Complete Execution Script

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Bridge GAD Plan View Generator - Main Execution Script
Usage: python main.py --excel input.xlsx --output bridge.dxf
"""

import argparse
import os
from excel_reader import ExcelBridgeReader
from plan_view_drawing import PlanViewDrawing


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Generate Bridge Plan View from Excel')
    parser.add_argument('--excel', '-e', type=str, default='bridge_template.xlsx',
                       help='Input Excel file path')
    parser.add_argument('--output', '-o', type=str, default='bridge_plan_view.dxf',
                       help='Output DXF file path')
    parser.add_argument('--create-template', '-t', action='store_true',
                       help='Create sample Excel template')
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    # Create template if requested
    if args.create_template:
        ExcelBridgeReader.create_sample_excel(args.excel)
        print(f"Template created: {args.excel}")
        print("Fill in the template and run again without --create-template")
        return
    
    # Check if input file exists
    if not os.path.exists(args.excel):
        print(f"Error: Input file not found: {args.excel}")
        print("Use --create-template to generate a sample input file")
        return
    
    # Read Excel and generate drawing
    try:
        print(f"Reading Excel file: {args.excel}")
        reader = ExcelBridgeReader(args.excel)
        reader.read_all_sheets()
        params = reader.parse_geometry_params()
        
        print(f"\nBridge Parameters:")
        print(f"  Name: {params.bridge_name}")
        print(f"  Chainage: {params.chainage}")
        print(f"  Spans: {params.span_count} x {params.span_lengths}")
        print(f"  Total Length: {params.total_length}m")
        print(f"  Width: {params.carriageway_width}m (C/W) / {params.overall_width}m (Overall)")
        print(f"  Skew: {params.skew_angle}°")
        
        # Generate drawing
        drawing = PlanViewDrawing(params)
        output_file = drawing.generate(args.output)
        
        print(f"\n✓ Success! Drawing generated: {os.path.abspath(output_file)}")
        print(f"\nOpen in AutoCAD, DraftSight, or any DXF viewer")
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"\n✗ Error: {e}")
        raise


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

- Add project details

The generated DXF includes:

| Feature | Layer | Description |
|---------|-------|-------------|
| **Deck Outline** | DECK | Red polyline with concrete hatch |
| **Carriageway** | CARRIAGEWAY | Yellow inner outline |
| **Piers** | PIER | Cyan with concrete hatch + labels (P-1, P-2...) |
| **Abutments** | ABUTMENT | Green with concrete hatch + labels (A-1, A-2) |
| **Bearings** | BEARING | Blue rectangles at each support |
| **Centerline** | CENTERLINE | Magenta dashed line with chainages |
| **Dimensions** | DIMENSION | All span lengths, total length, widths |
| **Title Block** | TEXT | Project info, scale, date, north arrow |

# COMPREHENSIVE EXCEL TEMPLATE STRUCTURE

## WORKBOOK ARCHITECTURE OVERVIEW

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    BRIDGE_GAD_MASTER.xlsx                      │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  1. PROJECT_MASTER      → Project info, client details        │
│  2. BRIDGE_SELECTION    → Bridge type & configuration          │
│  3. GEOMETRY_INPUT      → All dimensional parameters           │
│  4. SUPERSTRUCTURE      → Deck, girder, wearing coat details   │
│  5. SUBSTRUCTURE        → Piers, abutments, foundations        │
│  6. FOUNDATION_DETAILS  → Pile/Well foundation specifics       │
│  7. APPROACHES          → Approach roads, retaining walls      │
│  8. HYDRAULIC_DATA      → HFL, scour, waterway details         │
│  9. MATERIALS           → Concrete, steel grades               │
│ 10. BEARINGS_JOINTS     → Bearing schedule, expansion joints   │
│ 11. COMPONENTS_LIB      → Standard component library           │
│ 12. DRAWING_CONTROL     → Scale, sheet size, output settings   │
│ 13. CALCULATIONS        → Auto-calculated derived values       │
│ 14. VALIDATION          → Error checking & completeness status │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## DETAILED WORKSHEET DESIGNS

---

### **SHEET 1: PROJECT_MASTER**

**Purpose:** Project identification and administrative data

| Cell | Field Name | Data Type | Validation | Formula/Notes |
|------|-----------|-----------|------------|---------------|
| B2 | **Project Title** | Text | Max 100 chars | Free text |
| B3 | **Project Code** | Text | Format: PRJ-YYYY-NNN | e.g., NH-353C-2024-001 |
| B4 | **Bridge Name** | Text | Required | e.g., "Chambakkara Bridge" |
| B5 | **Chainage (Km)** | Number | Decimal, ≥0 | e.g., 37.730 |
| B6 | **Package No** | Text | - | Contract package number |
| B7 | **Client/Authority** | Dropdown | NHAI, State PWD, Railways, etc. | List in hidden sheet |
| B8 | **Consultant** | Text | - | Design consultant name |
| B9 | **Contractor** | Text | - | Executing agency |
| B10 | **Drawing No** | Auto | - | `=CONCATENATE(B3,"-GAD-001")` |
| B11 | **Drawing Title** | Auto | - | `=CONCATENATE("GAD of ",B4)` |
| B12 | **Revision** | Text | R0, R1, R2... | Current revision |
| B13 | **Date** | Date | ≤TODAY() | Drawing date |
| B14 | **Designed By** | Text | - | Engineer name |
| B15 | **Checked By** | Text | - | Checker name |
| B16 | **Approved By** | Text | - | Approver name |

**Layout:**
```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ A          │ B            │ C (Status) │
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ PROJECT MASTER                          │
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Project Title│ [________________]    │
│ Project Code │ [________________]    │
│ Bridge Name  │ [________________]    │
│ Chainage (Km)│ [____] ▼ (validate)  │
│ ...          │ ...                    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

### **SHEET 2: BRIDGE_SELECTION**

**Purpose:** Bridge type classification and configuration

| Cell | Parameter | Type | Validation | Options/Notes |
|------|-----------|------|------------|---------------|
| B3 | **Bridge Category** | Dropdown | Required | Major Bridge / Minor Bridge / Viaduct / ROB / RUB / FOB / Culvert |
| B4 | **Bridge Type** | Dropdown | Dependent on B3 | See type matrix below |
| B5 | **Span Configuration** | Dropdown | - | Simply Supported / Continuous / Cantilever / Cable Stayed / Suspension |
| B6 | **Total Span Count** | Number | Integer, 1-50 | Auto-calculate span array |
| B7 | **Carriageway Configuration** | Dropdown | - | Single Lane / 2-Lane / 4-Lane / 6-Lane / Multi-level |
| B8 | **Design Code** | Dropdown | - | IRC:5-2015 / IRC:6-2017 / IRC:21-2000 / IRC:24-2010 / MORTH |
| B9 | **Loading Class** | Dropdown | - | IRC Class A / 70R / AA / Special Vehicle |
| B10 | **Seismic Zone** | Dropdown | - | Zone II / III / IV / V |
| B11 | **Wind Zone** | Dropdown | - | Basic wind speed selection |
| B12 | **Foundation Type** | Dropdown | - | Open / Pile / Well / Raft / Combined |

**Bridge Type Matrix (Conditional Dropdown):**
```
IF B3="Major Bridge" → B4 options: PSC Girder, Steel Girder, Cable Stayed, Arch, Truss
IF B3="Minor Bridge" → B4 options: RCC T-Beam, Solid Slab, Box Culvert, Pipe Culvert
IF B3="Viaduct" → B4 options: PSC Box Girder, Segmental, I-Girder, U-Girder
IF B3="ROB/RUB" → B4 options: PSC Girder, Steel Composite, Box Culvert
```

---

### **SHEET 3: GEOMETRY_INPUT**

**Purpose:** All geometric parameters with auto-calculations

#### Section A: Alignment Data

| Cell | Parameter | Input | Unit | Validation | Formula |
|------|-----------|-------|------|------------|---------|
| B4 | Road Width | Input | m | 3.0 - 30.0 | - |
| B5 | Design Speed | Input | kmph | 20 - 150 | - |
| B6 | Horizontal Curve Radius | Input | m | ≥0 | 0 = Straight |
| B7 | Vertical Curve Type | Dropdown | - | - | Summit / Valley / None |
| B8 | Gradient | Input | % | -10 to +10 | + = Upward |

#### Section B: Bridge Geometry

| Cell | Parameter | Input | Unit | Validation | Formula |
|------|-----------|-------|------|------------|---------|
| B12 | Span Count | Input | nos | 1-20 | Triggers span table |
| B13 | **Span-1 Length** | Input | m | 3.0 - 200.0 | - |
| B14 | **Span-2 Length** | Input | m | 3.0 - 200.0 | - |
| B15 | **Span-3 Length** | Input | m | 3.0 - 200.0 | - |
| ... | ... | ... | ... | ... | Auto-extends to match B12 |
| B32 | **Total Bridge Length** | Auto | m | - | `=SUM(B13:B31)` |
| B33 | **Longest Span** | Auto | m | - | `=MAX(B13:B31)` |
| B34 | **Average Span** | Auto | m | - | `=AVERAGE(B13:B31)` |

#### Section C: Cross Section

| Cell | Parameter | Input | Unit | Validation | Notes |
|------|-----------|-------|------|------------|-------|
| B38 | Carriageway Width | Input | m | 3.0 - 20.0 | Clear width |
| B39 | Footpath Width (Left) | Input | m | 0 - 5.0 | 0 if none |
| B40 | Footpath Width (Right) | Input | m | 0 - 5.0 | 0 if none |
| B41 | Crash Barrier Width | Input | m | 0.3 - 0.6 | Each side |
| B42 | Kerb Width | Input | m | 0.15 - 0.75 | Each side |
| B43 | Median Width | Input | m | 0 - 10.0 | 0 if no median |
| B44 | **Overall Width** | Auto | m | - | `=B38+B39+B40+(2*B41)+B43` |
| B45 | **Half Width** | Auto | m | - | `=B44/2` |

#### Section D: Skew and Curvature

| Cell | Parameter | Input | Unit | Validation | Formula |
|------|-----------|-------|------|------------|---------|
| B49 | Skew Angle | Input | degrees | 0 - 60 | 0 = Square bridge |
| B50 | Skew Direction | Dropdown | - | - | Left / Right / None |
| B51 | Curved Bridge | Boolean | - | - | YES/NO |
| B52 | Curve Radius | Input | m | ≥0 | If B51=YES |
| B53 | Degree of Curve | Auto | degrees | - | `=IF(B52>0, 1718.87/B52, 0)` |

**Span Input Table (Dynamic Array):**
```
┌──────────┬─────────────┬──────────┬──────────────┐
│ Span No. │ Length (m)  │ Type     │ Status       │
├──────────┼─────────────┼──────────┼──────────────┤
│ 1        │ [ 30.00 ]   │ ▼        │ ✓            │
│ 2        │ [ 30.00 ]   │ ▼        │ ✓            │
│ 3        │ [ 30.00 ]   │ ▼        │ ✓            │
│ ...      │ ...         │ ...      │ ...          │
│ TOTAL    │ =SUM(C4:C23)│          │              │
└──────────┴─────────────┴──────────┴──────────────┘
```

### **SHEET 10: CALCULATIONS** (Auto-Generated)

**Purpose:** Derived calculations and checks

| Cell | Parameter | Formula | Status |
|------|-----------|---------|--------|
| B4 | Total Bridge Length | `=GEOMETRY_INPUT!B32` | ✓ |
| B5 | Number of Spans | `=GEOMETRY_INPUT!B12` | ✓ |
| B6 | Longest Span | `=GEOMETRY_INPUT!B33` | ✓ |
| B7 | Span/Depth Ratio | `=SUPERSTRUCTURE!B22` | Check |
| B8 | Pier Count | `=SUBSTRUCTURE!B6` | ✓ |
| B9 | Total Pile Count | `=IF(FOUNDATION!B5="PILE", FOUNDATION!B9 * B8, 0)` | ✓ |
| B10 | Concrete Volume (Est.) | Formula based on type | ~ |
| B11 | Steel Weight (Est.) | Formula based on type | ~ |
| B12 | Bearing Count | `=(B8+2)*3` | ✓ |
| B13 | Expansion Joint Count | `=2` | ✓ |
| B14 | Deck Area | `=GEOMETRY_INPUT!B32 * GEOMETRY_INPUT!B44` | ✓ |
| B15 | Cost Estimate (Lakhs) | Rough formula | ~ |

**Status Indicators:**
- ✓ = Valid
- ⚠ = Warning (out of typical range)
- ✗ = Error (fails validation)

---

### **SHEET 11: VALIDATION**

**Purpose:** Comprehensive error checking

| Check ID | Description | Formula | Status |
|----------|-------------|---------|--------|
| V001 | Project code format | `=IF(REGEXMATCH(B3,"^[A-Z]+-\d{4}-\d{3}$"),"✓","✗")` | - |
| V002 | Chainage positive | `=IF(B5>0,"✓","✗")` | - |
| V003 | Span lengths valid | `=IF(AND(B13:B31>3,B13:B31<200),"✓","✗")` | - |
| V004 | Total length reasonable | `=IF(B32<1000,"✓","⚠")` | - |
| V005 | Width consistent | `=IF(B44>B38,"✓","✗")` | - |
| V006 | Skew angle limit | `=IF(B49<=60,"✓","✗")` | - |
| V007 | Span/depth ratio | `=IF(AND(B22>10,B22<25),"✓","⚠")` | - |
| V008 | HFL > LWL | `=IF(B5>B6,"✓","✗")` | - |
| V009 | Scour depth adequate | `=IF(B10>0,"✓","✗")` | - |
| V010 | Soffit above HFL | `=IF(B20>B5,"✓","✗")` | - |
| V011 | Foundation below scour | `=IF(FOUNDATION!B14<B9,"✓","✗")` | - |
| V012 | Material grades valid | `=IF(AND(B4<>"",B5<>""),"✓","✗")` | - |

**Overall Status:**
```
=IF(COUNTIF(D4:D15,"✗")>0,"CRITICAL ERRORS FOUND",
   IF(COUNTIF(D4:D15,"⚠")>0,"WARNINGS - REVIEW REQUIRED",
   "ALL CHECKS PASSED ✓"))
```

## DATA VALIDATION RULES (Excel Implementation)

### Dropdown Lists (Create Named Ranges)

```excel
' Named Range: BRIDGE_TYPES
Major Bridge
Minor Bridge
Viaduct
ROB
RUB
FOB
Culvert

' Named Range: SUPERSTRUCTURE_TYPES
RCC T-Beam

PSC Box Girder

Voided Slab
Steel Composite
Cable Stayed
Arch
Truss

' Named Range: FOUNDATION_TYPES
Open
Pile
Well
Raft
Combined

' Named Range: CONCRETE_GRADES
M20
M25
M30
M35
M40
M45
M50
M55
```

### Conditional Formatting Rules

```excel
' For Validation Column (D):
- Cell Value = "✓" → Green fill
- Cell Value = "⚠" → Yellow fill  
- Cell Value = "✗" → Red fill

' For Input Cells:
- If blank and required → Light red background
- If valid → White background
- If out of range → Yellow background
```

---

## PYTHON INTEGRATION CODE

To read this Excel structure:

```python
import pandas as pd
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Optional, List

class BridgeExcelReader:
    def __init__(self, filepath: str):
        self.filepath = filepath
        self.excel = pd.ExcelFile(filepath)
        
    def read_project_master(self) -> dict:
        df = pd.read_excel(self.filepath, sheet_name='PROJECT_MASTER', header=None)
        return {
            'project_title': df.iloc[1, 1],
            'project_code': df.iloc[2, 1],
            'bridge_name': df.iloc[3, 1],
            'chainage': df.iloc[4, 1],
            'drawing_no': df.iloc[9, 1],
            'revision': df.iloc[11, 1],
            'date': df.iloc[12, 1]
        }
    
    def read_geometry(self) -> dict:
        df = pd.read_excel(self.filepath, sheet_name='GEOMETRY_INPUT', header=None)
        
        # Read span count
        span_count = int(df.iloc[11, 1])
        
        # Read span lengths (rows 12-30, column 1)
        spans = []
        for i in range(span_count):
            span_val = df.iloc[12+i, 1]
            if pd.notna(span_val):
                spans.append(float(span_val))
        
        return {
            'span_count': span_count,
            'span_lengths': spans,
            'total_length': float(df.iloc[31, 1]),
            'carriageway_width': float(df.iloc[37, 1]),
            'overall_width': float(df.iloc[43, 1]),
            'skew_angle': float(df.iloc[48, 1]),
            'half_width': float(df.iloc[44, 1])
        }
    
    def read_all_sheets(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'project': self.read_project_master(),
            'geometry': self.read_geometry(),
            'superstructure': self.read_sheet_data('SUPERSTRUCTURE'),
            'substructure': self.read_sheet_data('SUBSTRUCTURE'),
            'foundation': self.read_sheet_data('FOUNDATION_DETAILS'),
            'hydraulic': self.read_sheet_data('HYDRAULIC_DATA'),
            'materials': self.read_sheet_data('MATERIALS'),
            'drawing_control': self.read_sheet_data('DRAWING_CONTROL')
        }
    
    def read_sheet_data(self, sheet_name: str) -> dict:
        """Generic sheet reader - assumes 2-column key-value format"""
        df = pd.read_excel(self.filepath, sheet_name=sheet_name, header=None)
        data = {}
        for _, row in df.iterrows():
            if pd.notna(row[0]) and pd.notna(row[1]):
                key = str(row[0]).strip().replace(' ', '_').lower()
                data[key] = row[1]
        return data
    
    def validate(self) -> List[str]:
        """Run all validation checks"""
        errors = []
        
        # Check required sheets exist
        required = ['PROJECT_MASTER', 'GEOMETRY_INPUT', 'SUPERSTRUCTURE', 
                   'SUBSTRUCTURE', 'FOUNDATION_DETAILS']
        for sheet in required:
            if sheet not in self.excel.sheet_names:
                errors.append(f"Missing required sheet: {sheet}")
        
        # Check critical values
        geom = self.read_geometry()
        if geom['span_count'] != len(geom['span_lengths']):
            errors.append("Span count doesn't match span length entries")
        
        if geom['overall_width'] <= geom['carriageway_width']:
            errors.append("Overall width must be greater than carriageway width")
        
        return errors
```

---

## USAGE WORKFLOW

### 1. Create New Bridge Project
```bash
python bridge_system.py --create-template --output new_bridge.xlsx
```

### 2. Fill Excel Data
Open `new_bridge.xlsx` and complete:
- **PROJECT_MASTER**: Basic project info
- **BRIDGE_SELECTION**: Select bridge type
- **GEOMETRY_INPUT**: Enter spans, widths, skew
- **SUPERSTRUCTURE**: Select girder type and dimensions
- **SUBSTRUCTURE**: Configure piers and abutments
- **FOUNDATION_DETAILS**: Specify foundation type and sizes
- **HYDRAULIC_DATA**: Enter HFL, scour levels
- **DRAWING_CONTROL**: Set output preferences

### 3. Validate Input
```bash
python bridge_system.py --validate --excel new_bridge.xlsx
```

### 4. Generate Drawings
```bash
python bridge_system.py --excel new_bridge.xlsx --output ./drawings/
```

---

## SAMPLE FILLED TEMPLATE (Chambakkara Bridge Example)

| Sheet | Key Values |
|-------|-----------|
| **PROJECT_MASTER** | Bridge: Chambakkara Bridge, Chainage: 0+600, Code: NH-353C-600 |
| **BRIDGE_SELECTION** | Type: Major Bridge, Spans: 3, Configuration: Simply Supported |
| **GEOMETRY_INPUT** | Spans: 25m, 30m, 25m, Width: 12m, Skew: 15° |
| **SUPERSTRUCTURE** | T-Beam, M35 concrete, 250mm deck, 65mm wearing coat |
| **SUBSTRUCTURE** | Wall piers, Cantilever abutments |
| **FOUNDATION** | Pile foundation, 1.2m diameter, 15m depth |
| **HYDRAULIC** | HFL: 185.50m, Scour: 175.20m, Waterway: 180m |
| **DRAWING_CONTROL** | Scale: 1:100, Sheets: A1, Output: DXF+PDF |
