Would you like me to:
1. **Generate the actual Excel file** with all formulas and validation (Python code to create XLSX)?
2. **Create additional sheets** for Cost Estimation, Construction Sequence, or Bar Bending Schedule?
3. **Design the Cross-Section and Longitudinal Section** input sheets in detail?
4. **Add reinforcement detailing** input tables for beams, piers, and foundations?
Design the geometry calculation engine for skew bridges?
# SKEW BRIDGE GEOMETRY CALCULATION ENGINE

## MATHEMATICAL FOUNDATION

### Key Concepts for Skew Bridges:

```
SQUARE BRIDGE (0° Skew)          SKEW BRIDGE (θ° Skew)
                                    
    ←─── L ───→                  ←───── L' ─────→
    ┌─────────┐                  ╱────────────────╲
    │         │                 ╱                  ╲
 W  │   ▲     │  W            ╱        ▲           ╲  W'
    │   │     │               ╱         │            ╲
    └─────────┘              ╱──────────────────────────╲
    
    L' = L / cos(θ)          W' = W / cos(θ)
    Effective span increases  Effective width increases
```

---

## COMPLETE PYTHON MODULE

### File 1: `skew_geometry_core.py` - Core Mathematics

```python
"""
Skew Bridge Geometry Calculation Engine
Handles all coordinate transformations and geometric calculations for skewed bridges
"""

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Tuple, Optional, Dict
from enum import Enum


class SkewDirection(Enum):
    """Direction of skew relative to centerline"""
    LEFT = -1   # Skew to left (counter-clockwise)
    RIGHT = 1   # Skew to right (clockwise)
    NONE = 0    # No skew (square)


@dataclass
class Point2D:
    """2D Point with x (chainage), y (offset) coordinates"""
    x: float  # Along alignment (chainage direction)
    y: float  # Perpendicular to alignment
    
    def __add__(self, other: 'Point2D') -> 'Point2D':
        return Point2D(self.x + other.x, self.y + other.y)
    
    def __sub__(self, other: 'Point2D') -> 'Point2D':
        return Point2D(self.x - other.x, self.y - other.y)
    
    def __mul__(self, scalar: float) -> 'Point2D':
        return Point2D(self.x * scalar, self.y * scalar)
    
    def distance_to(self, other: 'Point2D') -> float:
        """Euclidean distance to another point"""
        return math.sqrt((self.x - other.x)**2 + (self.y - other.y)**2)
    
    def rotate(self, angle_deg: float, origin: 'Point2D' = None) -> 'Point2D':
        """
        Rotate point around origin by given angle in degrees
        Positive angle = counter-clockwise
        """
        if origin is None:
            origin = Point2D(0, 0)
        
        angle_rad = math.radians(angle_deg)
        dx = self.x - origin.x
        dy = self.y - origin.y
        
        new_x = origin.x + dx * math.cos(angle_rad) - dy * math.sin(angle_rad)
        new_y = origin.y + dx * math.sin(angle_rad) + dy * math.cos(angle_rad)
        
        return Point2D(new_x, new_y)
    
    def offset_perpendicular(self, distance: float, angle_deg: float) -> 'Point2D':
        """
        Offset point perpendicular to a given direction
        angle_deg: Direction of the reference line
        """
        perp_angle = angle_deg + 90
        rad = math.radians(perp_angle)
        return Point2D(
            self.x + distance * math.cos(rad),
            self.y + distance * math.sin(rad)
        )
    
    def to_tuple(self) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        return (self.x, self.y)


@dataclass
class Point3D:
    """3D Point for 3D geometry calculations"""
    x: float
    y: float
    z: float  # RL (Reduced Level)
    
    def to_2d(self) -> Point2D:
        return Point2D(self.x, self.y)


@dataclass
class SkewParameters:
    """Skew bridge specific parameters"""
    skew_angle_deg: float  # Angle between bridge axis and normal to centerline
    skew_direction: SkewDirection = SkewDirection.RIGHT
    is_curved: bool = False
    curve_radius: float = 0  # 0 = straight, >0 = curved
    curve_direction: str = "LEFT"  # or "RIGHT" for curved bridges
    
    @property
    def skew_angle_rad(self) -> float:
        return math.radians(self.skew_angle_deg)
    
    @property
    def effective_skew(self) -> float:
        """Skew angle with direction sign"""
        return self.skew_angle_deg * self.skew_direction.value


@dataclass
class BridgeDeckGeometry:
    """Complete deck geometry for a skew bridge"""
    # Square dimensions (perpendicular to bridge axis)
    square_length: float
    square_width: float
    
    # Skew parameters
    skew: SkewParameters
    
    # Calculated properties
    @property
    def projected_length(self) -> float:
        """Length projected on centerline (increases with skew)"""
        return self.square_length / math.cos(self.skew.skew_angle_rad)
    
    @property
    def projected_width(self) -> float:
        """Width projected perpendicular to centerline"""
        return self.square_width * math.cos(self.skew.skew_angle_rad)
    
    @property
    def acute_corner_angle(self) -> float:
        """Angle at acute corners (for dimensioning)"""
        return 90 - self.skew.skew_angle_deg
    
    @property
    def obtuse_corner_angle(self) -> float:
        """Angle at obtuse corners"""
        return 90 + self.skew.skew_angle_deg


class SkewCoordinateTransformer:
    """
    Handles coordinate transformations between:
    - Bridge local coordinates (along/perpendicular to bridge axis)
    - Global coordinates (along/perpendicular to road centerline)
    - Skew coordinates (accounting for skew angle)
    """
    
    def __init__(self, skew_params: SkewParameters, bridge_center: Point2D = None):
        self.skew = skew_params
        self.center = bridge_center or Point2D(0, 0)
        
    def bridge_to_global(self, point: Point2D) -> Point2D:
        """
        Convert from bridge local coordinates to global coordinates
        Bridge X = along bridge axis
        Bridge Y = perpendicular to bridge axis
        """
        # Rotate by skew angle around bridge center
        return point.rotate(self.skew.effective_skew, self.center)
    
    def global_to_bridge(self, point: Point2D) -> Point2D:
        """Convert from global to bridge local coordinates"""
        return point.rotate(-self.skew.effective_skew, self.center)
    
    def get_bridge_axis_direction(self) -> float:
        """Get the angle of bridge axis in global coordinates"""
        return self.skew.effective_skew
    
    def get_normal_direction(self) -> float:
        """Get direction perpendicular to bridge axis"""
        return self.skew.effective_skew + 90


class SkewIntersectionCalculator:
    """
    Calculates intersections and geometric relationships for skew bridges
    Critical for wing walls, abutments, and approach slabs
    """
    
    def __init__(self, skew_params: SkewParameters):
        self.skew = skew_params
        self.transformer = SkewCoordinateTransformer(skew_params)
    
    def line_intersection(self, p1: Point2D, angle1: float, 
                          p2: Point2D, angle2: float) -> Optional[Point2D]:
        """
        Find intersection of two lines defined by point and angle
        Returns None if lines are parallel
        """
        # Convert to standard form: ax + by = c
        rad1 = math.radians(angle1)
        rad2 = math.radians(angle2)
        
        a1, b1 = math.sin(rad1), -math.cos(rad1)
        a2, b2 = math.sin(rad2), -math.cos(rad2)
        
        c1 = a1 * p1.x + b1 * p1.y
        c2 = a2 * p2.x + b2 * p2.y
        
        determinant = a1 * b2 - a2 * b1
        
        if abs(determinant) < 1e-10:
            return None  # Lines are parallel
        
        x = (b2 * c1 - b1 * c2) / determinant
        y = (a1 * c2 - a2 * c1) / determinant
        
        return Point2D(x, y)
    
    def line_segment_intersection(self, p1: Point2D, p2: Point2D,
                                   p3: Point2D, p4: Point2D) -> Optional[Point2D]:
        """Find intersection of two line segments"""
        # Line 1: p1 to p2
        # Line 2: p3 to p4
        
        x1, y1 = p1.x, p1.y
        x2, y2 = p2.x, p2.y
        x3, y3 = p3.x, p3.y
        x4, y4 = p4.x, p4.y
        
        denom = (x1 - x2) * (y3 - y4) - (y1 - y2) * (x3 - x4)
        
        if abs(denom) < 1e-10:
            return None
        
        t = ((x1 - x3) * (y3 - y4) - (y1 - y3) * (x3 - x4)) / denom
        u = -((x1 - x2) * (y1 - y3) - (y1 - y2) * (x1 - x3)) / denom
        
        if 0 <= t <= 1 and 0 <= u <= 1:
            x = x1 + t * (x2 - x1)
            y = y1 + t * (y2 - y1)
            return Point2D(x, y)
        
        return None
    
    def perpendicular_foot(self, point: Point2D, line_point: Point2D, 
                          line_angle: float) -> Point2D:
        """
        Find foot of perpendicular from point to a line
        """
        perp_angle = line_angle + 90
        return self.line_intersection(point, perp_angle, line_point, line_angle)
    
    def offset_line(self, point: Point2D, angle: float, offset: float) -> Tuple[Point2D, float]:
        """
        Get a line parallel to given line at specified offset distance
        Returns (new_point, same_angle)
        """
        perp_angle = angle + 90
        rad = math.radians(perp_angle)
        new_point = Point2D(
            point.x + offset * math.cos(rad),
            point.y + offset * math.sin(rad)
        )
        return new_point, angle


class SkewDeckGeometryCalculator:
    """
    Calculates complete deck geometry including corners, edges, and internal points
    """
    
    def __init__(self, deck: BridgeDeckGeometry, center: Point2D = None):
        self.deck = deck
        self.center = center or Point2D(deck.projected_length / 2, 0)
        self.skew = deck.skew
        self.transformer = SkewCoordinateTransformer(self.skew, self.center)
        self.intersection = SkewIntersectionCalculator(self.skew)
    
    def get_deck_corners(self) -> Dict[str, Point2D]:
        """
        Calculate all four corners of the skewed deck
        Naming convention (looking along bridge from start to end):
        - LEFT_START: Left side at bridge start
        - RIGHT_START: Right side at bridge start
        - LEFT_END: Left side at bridge end
        - RIGHT_END: Right side at bridge end
        """
        half_length = self.deck.square_length / 2
        half_width = self.deck.square_width / 2
        
        # In bridge local coordinates (square)
        local_corners = {
            'LEFT_START': Point2D(-half_length, -half_width),
            'RIGHT_START': Point2D(-half_length, half_width),
            'LEFT_END': Point2D(half_length, -half_width),
            'RIGHT_END': Point2D(half_length, half_width)
        }
        
        # Transform to global coordinates (skewed)
        global_corners = {}
        for name, point in local_corners.items():
            global_corners[name] = self.transformer.bridge_to_global(point)
        
        return global_corners
    
    def get_deck_edges(self) -> Dict[str, Tuple[Point2D, Point2D]]:
        """Get deck edges as line segments"""
        corners = self.get_deck_corners()
        
        return {
            'START_EDGE': (corners['LEFT_START'], corners['RIGHT_START']),
            'END_EDGE': (corners['LEFT_END'], corners['RIGHT_END']),
            'LEFT_EDGE': (corners['LEFT_START'], corners['LEFT_END']),
            'RIGHT_EDGE': (corners['RIGHT_START'], corners['RIGHT_END'])
        }
    
    def get_centerline_points(self, num_points: int = 100) -> List[Point2D]:
        """Generate points along bridge centerline"""
        points = []
        half_length = self.deck.projected_length / 2
        
        for i in range(num_points + 1):
            t = i / num_points
            local_x = -half_length + t * self.deck.projected_length
            local_point = Point2D(local_x, 0)
            global_point = self.transformer.bridge_to_global(local_point)
            points.append(global_point)
        
        return points
    
    def get_carriageway_edges(self, carriageway_width: float) -> Dict[str, Tuple[Point2D, Point2D]]:
        """Get carriageway edges (inside deck edges)"""
        half_length = self.deck.projected_length / 2
        kerb_offset = (self.deck.square_width - carriageway_width) / 2
        
        # Calculate edges parallel to deck edges at kerb offset
        edges = self.get_deck_edges()
        
        # Left carriageway edge (offset from LEFT_EDGE toward center)
        left_edge = edges['LEFT_EDGE']
        left_cw_edge = self._offset_edge_toward_center(left_edge, kerb_offset)
        
        # Right carriageway edge (offset from RIGHT_EDGE toward center)
        right_edge = edges['RIGHT_EDGE']
        right_cw_edge = self._offset_edge_toward_center(right_edge, -kerb_offset)
        
        return {
            'LEFT_CW': left_cw_edge,
            'RIGHT_CW': right_cw_edge
        }
    
    def _offset_edge_toward_center(self, edge: Tuple[Point2D, Point2D], 
                                    offset: float) -> Tuple[Point2D, Point2D]:
        """Offset an edge toward bridge center"""
        p1, p2 = edge
        
        # Calculate edge angle
        dx = p2.x - p1.x
        dy = p2.y - p1.y
        angle = math.degrees(math.atan2(dy, dx))
        
        # Offset perpendicular to edge
        perp_angle = angle + 90
        rad = math.radians(perp_angle)
        
        new_p1 = Point2D(
            p1.x + offset * math.cos(rad),
            p1.y + offset * math.sin(rad)
        )
        new_p2 = Point2D(
            p2.x + offset * math.cos(rad),
            p2.y + offset * math.sin(rad)
        )
        
        return (new_p1, new_p2)
    
    def get_chainage_at_point(self, point: Point2D) -> float:
        """Get chainage along bridge centerline for any point"""
        # Transform to bridge coordinates
        local = self.transformer.global_to_bridge(point)
        # X coordinate in bridge system is the chainage
        return local.x + self.center.x
    
    def get_offset_from_centerline(self, point: Point2D) -> float:
        """Get perpendicular offset from bridge centerline"""
        local = self.transformer.global_to_bridge(point)
        return local.y


class SkewSupportGeometryCalculator:
    """
    Calculates geometry for piers and abutments on skew bridges
    Critical for bearing alignment and support positioning
    """
    
    def __init__(self, deck_calc: SkewDeckGeometryCalculator):
        self.deck_calc = deck_calc
        self.deck = deck_calc.deck
        self.skew = deck_calc.skew
        self.transformer = deck_calc.transformer
    
    def get_support_center(self, chainage: float) -> Point2D:
        """Get center point of support at given chainage"""
        local_point = Point2D(chainage - self.deck_calc.center.x, 0)
        return self.transformer.bridge_to_global(local_point)
    
    def get_support_outline(self, center: Point2D, width: float, 
                           length: float) -> Dict[str, Point2D]:
        """
        Get corners of a rectangular support (pier/abutment cap)
        Support is aligned perpendicular to bridge axis (normal to skew)
        """
        half_width = width / 2
        half_length = length / 2
        
        # Support is perpendicular to bridge axis
        # So it's aligned with the Y-axis in bridge coordinates
        local_corners = {
            'CORNER_1': Point2D(-half_length, -half_width),
            'CORNER_2': Point2D(half_length, -half_width),
            'CORNER_3': Point2D(half_length, half_width),
            'CORNER_4': Point2D(-half_length, half_width)
        }
        
        # Transform to global, then translate to center
        global_corners = {}
        for name, point in local_corners.items():
            # First rotate by skew angle
            rotated = point.rotate(self.skew.effective_skew)
            # Then translate to center
            global_corners[name] = Point2D(
                rotated.x + center.x,
                rotated.y + center.y
            )
        
        return global_corners
    
    def get_bearing_positions(self, support_center: Point2D, 
                              carriageway_width: float,
                              num_bearings: int = 3,
                              bearing_spacing: float = None) -> List[Point2D]:
        """
        Calculate bearing positions at a support
        Bearings are aligned perpendicular to bridge axis
        """
        if bearing_spacing is None:
            bearing_spacing = carriageway_width / (num_bearings - 1) if num_bearings > 1 else 0
        
        positions = []
        
        # Start from left edge of carriageway
        start_offset = -carriageway_width / 2
        
        for i in range(num_bearings):
            offset = start_offset + i * bearing_spacing
            
            # In bridge coordinates, offset is Y direction
            local_point = Point2D(0, offset)
            
            # Transform to global
            global_point = self.transformer.bridge_to_global(local_point)
            
            # Translate to support center
            final_point = Point2D(
                global_point.x + support_center.x - self.deck_calc.center.x,
                global_point.y + support_center.y
            )
            positions.append(final_point)
        
        return positions
    
    def get_bearing_seats(self, bearing_positions: List[Point2D],
                          seat_width: float = 0.6,
                          seat_length: float = 0.4) -> List[Dict[str, Point2D]]:
        """
        Get bearing seat (pedestal) outlines for each bearing
        Seats are rectangular and aligned with bridge axis
        """
        seats = []
        
        for pos in bearing_positions:
            corners = self.get_support_outline(pos, seat_width, seat_length)
            seats.append(corners)
        
        return seats


class SkewWingWallCalculator:
    """
    Calculates wing wall geometry for skew bridges
    Wing walls are particularly complex due to varying lengths and angles
    """
    
    def __init__(self, deck_calc: SkewDeckGeometryCalculator):
        self.deck_calc = deck_calc
        self.deck = deck_calc.deck
        self.skew = deck_calc.skew
        self.intersection = SkewIntersectionCalculator(self.skew)
    
    def get_wing_wall_geometry(self, abutment_center: Point2D,
                                  abutment_width: float,
                                  abutment_length: float,
                                  wing_length: float,
                                  splay_angle: float = 45,
                                  is_left: bool = True) -> Dict:
        """
        Calculate complete wing wall geometry
        
        For skew bridges, wing walls have:
        - Different lengths on acute vs obtuse sides
        - Complex intersections with abutment
        - Splayed or returned configurations
        """
        # Get abutment outline
        support_calc = SkewSupportGeometryCalculator(self.deck_calc)
        abutment_corners = support_calc.get_support_outline(
            abutment_center, abutment_width, abutment_length
        )
        
        # Determine which corners to use based on left/right and skew direction
        if is_left:
            if self.skew.skew_direction == SkewDirection.RIGHT:
                # Acute corner at start
                abutment_corner = abutment_corners['CORNER_1']
                wall_angle = self.skew.effective_skew - splay_angle
            else:
                # Obtuse corner at start
                abutment_corner = abutment_corners['CORNER_4']
                wall_angle = self.skew.effective_skew + splay_angle
        else:
            if self.skew.skew_direction == SkewDirection.RIGHT:
                abutment_corner = abutment_corners['CORNER_2']
                wall_angle = self.skew.effective_skew + splay_angle
            else:
                abutment_corner = abutment_corners['CORNER_3']
                wall_angle = self.skew.effective_skew - splay_angle
        
        # Calculate wing wall end point
        wing_end = Point2D(
            abutment_corner.x + wing_length * math.cos(math.radians(wall_angle)),
            abutment_corner.y + wing_length * math.sin(math.radians(wall_angle))
        )
        
        # Calculate wing wall width (typically 0.3-0.6m)
        wall_thickness = 0.45
        
        # Calculate second corner (width perpendicular to wall)
        perp_angle = wall_angle + 90
        wall_corner_2 = Point2D(
            abutment_corner.x + wall_thickness * math.cos(math.radians(perp_angle)),
            abutment_corner.y + wall_thickness * math.sin(math.radians(perp_angle))
        )
        
        wall_corner_3 = Point2D(
            wing_end.x + wall_thickness * math.cos(math.radians(perp_angle)),
            wing_end.y + wall_thickness * math.sin(math.radians(perp_angle))
        )
        
        return {
            'abutment_corner': abutment_corner,
            'wall_end': wing_end,
            'corners': [
                abutment_corner,
                wall_corner_2,
                wall_corner_3,
                wing_end
            ],
            'wall_angle': wall_angle,
            'wall_length': wing_length,
            'is_left': is_left
        }
    
    def get_return_wall_geometry(self, abutment_center: Point2D,
                                   abutment_width: float,
                                   abutment_length: float,
                                   return_length: float,
                                   is_left: bool = True) -> Dict:
        """
        Calculate return wall geometry (parallel to centerline)
        """
        support_calc = SkewSupportGeometryCalculator(self.deck_calc)
        abutment_corners = support_calc.get_support_outline(
            abutment_center, abutment_width, abutment_length
        )
        
        # Return wall is parallel to centerline (0 degrees)
        # But offset by abutment width/2
        
        if is_left:
            if self.skew.skew_direction == SkewDirection.RIGHT:
                start_corner = abutment_corners['CORNER_1']
            else:
                start_corner = abutment_corners['CORNER_4']
        else:
            if self.skew.skew_direction == SkewDirection.RIGHT:
                start_corner = abutment_corners['CORNER_2']
            else:
                start_corner = abutment_corners['CORNER_3']
        
        # Return wall extends parallel to centerline
        return_end = Point2D(start_corner.x - return_length, start_corner.y)
        
        return {
            'start': start_corner,
            'end': return_end,
            'length': return_length,
            'direction': 'PARALLEL_TO_CENTERLINE'
        }


class SkewDimensionCalculator:
    """
    Calculates dimension lines and annotation positions for skew bridges
    Dimensioning skew bridges requires special handling
    """
    
    def __init__(self, deck_calc: SkewDeckGeometryCalculator):
        self.deck_calc = deck_calc
        self.deck = deck_calc.deck
        self.skew = deck_calc.skew
    
    def get_span_dimension_line(self, support1_center: Point2D,
                                 support2_center: Point2D,
                                 offset_distance: float = 3.0) -> Dict:
        """
        Get dimension line for span between two supports
        For skew bridges, dimension is perpendicular to bridge axis
        """
        # Calculate midpoint
        mid_x = (support1_center.x + support2_center.x) / 2
        mid_y = (support1_center.y + support2_center.y) / 2
        midpoint = Point2D(mid_x, mid_y)
        
        # Dimension line is perpendicular to bridge axis
        dim_angle = self.skew.effective_skew + 90
        
        # Offset from centerline
        rad = math.radians(dim_angle)
        dim_start = Point2D(
            midpoint.x + offset_distance * math.cos(rad),
            midpoint.y + offset_distance * math.sin(rad)
        )
        
        # Calculate actual span length
        span_length = support1_center.distance_to(support2_center)
        
        return {
            'start_point': dim_start,
            'angle': dim_angle,
            'text_position': dim_start,
            'span_length': span_length,
            'support1': support1_center,
            'support2': support2_center
        }
    
    def get_overall_dimension(self, start_point: Point2D,
                               end_point: Point2D,
                               offset_distance: float = 5.0) -> Dict:
        """Get overall length dimension"""
        # Calculate angle of line between points
        dx = end_point.x - start_point.x
        dy = end_point.y - start_point.y
        angle = math.degrees(math.atan2(dy, dx))
        
        # Perpendicular offset
        perp_angle = angle + 90
        rad = math.radians(perp_angle)
        
        dim_line_start = Point2D(
            start_point.x + offset_distance * math.cos(rad),
            start_point.y + offset_distance * math.sin(rad)
        )
        
        dim_line_end = Point2D(
            end_point.x + offset_distance * math.cos(rad),
            end_point.y + offset_distance * math.sin(rad)
        )
        
        total_length = start_point.distance_to(end_point)
        
        return {
            'line_start': dim_line_start,
            'line_end': dim_line_end,
            'text_position': Point2D(
                (dim_line_start.x + dim_line_end.x) / 2,
                (dim_line_start.y + dim_line_end.y) / 2
            ),
            'total_length': total_length,
            'angle': angle
        }
    
    def get_width_dimensions(self, deck_corners: Dict[str, Point2D],
                              num_sections: int = 3) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        Get width dimensions at multiple sections along bridge
        For skew bridges, width varies (appears wider at acute corners)
        """
        dimensions = []
        
        # Get start and end edges
        start_left = deck_corners['LEFT_START']
        start_right = deck_corners['RIGHT_START']
        end_left = deck_corners['LEFT_END']
        end_right = deck_corners['RIGHT_END']
        
        for i in range(num_sections):
            t = i / (num_sections - 1) if num_sections > 1 else 0.5
            
            # Interpolate along edges
            left_point = Point2D(
                start_left.x + t * (end_left.x - start_left.x),
                start_left.y + t * (end_left.y - start_left.y)
            )
            
            right_point = Point2D(
                start_right.x + t * (end_right.x - start_right.x),
                start_right.y + t * (end_right.y - start_right.y)
            )
            
            width = left_point.distance_to(right_point)
            
            # Dimension offset
            mid_x = (left_point.x + right_point.x) / 2
            mid_y = (left_point.y + right_point.y) / 2
            
            # Perpendicular to edge
            dx = right_point.x - left_point.x
            dy = right_point.y - left_point.y
            angle = math.degrees(math.atan2(dy, dx))
            perp_angle = angle + 90
            
            rad = math.radians(perp_angle)
            offset = 2.0
            
            dim_pos = Point2D(
                mid_x + offset * math.cos(rad),
                mid_y + offset * math.sin(rad)
            )
            
            dimensions.append({
                'chainage_ratio': t,
                'width': width,
                'left_point': left_point,
                'right_point': right_point,
                'dimension_position': dim_pos,
                'angle': angle
            })
        
        return dimensions


class SkewReinforcementCalculator:
    """
    Calculates reinforcement bar lengths and shapes accounting for skew
    Critical for accurate bar bending schedules
    """
    
    def __init__(self, skew_params: SkewParameters):
        self.skew = skew_params
    
    def get_deck_slab_bar_length(self, bar_spacing: float,
                                  bar_diameter: float,
                                  deck_width: float,
                                  is_main_bar: bool = True) -> Dict:
        """
        Calculate main and distribution bar lengths for skew deck slab
        
        Main bars: Perpendicular to bridge axis (longer due to skew)
        Distribution bars: Parallel to bridge axis
        """
        skew_rad = self.skew.skew_angle_rad
        
        if is_main_bar:
            # Main bars perpendicular to bridge axis
            # Length increases by 1/cos(skew)
            actual_length = deck_width / math.cos(skew_rad)
            
            # Add development length at both ends
            dev_length = 40 * bar_diameter  # Typical development length
            total_length = actual_length + 2 * dev_length
            
            # Hook details
            hook_length = 12 * bar_diameter
            
            return {
                'straight_length': actual_length,
                'total_length': total_length,
                'development_length': dev_length,
                'hook_length': hook_length,
                'cutting_length': total_length + 2 * hook_length - 2 * hook_length * 0.5,  # Deduction for bend
                'orientation': 'PERPENDICULAR_TO_AXIS',
                'skew_correction_factor': 1 / math.cos(skew_rad)
            }
        else:
            # Distribution bars parallel to bridge axis
            # No skew correction needed
            return {
                'straight_length': deck_width,
                'total_length': deck_width + 2 * dev_length,
                'orientation': 'PARALLEL_TO_AXIS',
                'skew_correction_factor': 1.0
            }
    
    def get_girder_bar_length(self, span_length: float,
                               girder_depth: float,
                               skew_angle: float,
                               bar_type: str = 'main') -> Dict:
        """
        Calculate girder reinforcement accounting for skew
        """
        skew_rad = math.radians(skew_angle)
        
        if bar_type == 'main':
            # Main longitudinal bars follow skew
            # Length = span / cos(skew)
            actual_length = span_length / math.cos(skew_rad)
            
            # For T-beams, account for varying depth at ends
            end_depth_factor = 1.0  # Adjust based on haunch
            
            return {
                'span_length': span_length,
                'actual_bar_length': actual_length,
                'depth_at_center': girder_depth,
                'depth_variation': 'ACCOUNTED',
                'extra_length_due_to_skew': actual_length - span_length
            }
        
        elif bar_type == 'stirrup':
            # Stirrups are perpendicular to main bars
            # But spacing is measured along the girder
            # So stirrup count increases with skew
            
            standard_spacing = 0.15  # 150mm
            num_stirrups = math.ceil(actual_length / standard_spacing) + 1
            
            return {
                'girder_length_along_axis': span_length,
                'girder_length_along_skew': actual_length,
                'stirrup_spacing': standard_spacing,
                'number_of_stirrups': num_stirrups,
                'extra_stirrups_due_to_skew': num_stirrups - math.ceil(span_length / standard_spacing) - 1
            }
    
    def get_pier_cap_bar_length(self, cap_width: float,
                                 cap_length: float,
                                 skew_angle: float) -> Dict:
        """
        Calculate pier cap reinforcement
        """
        skew_rad = math.radians(skew_angle)
        
        # Top and bottom bars run along cap length (perpendicular to bridge)
        bar_length = cap_length / math.cos(skew_rad)
        
        # Stirrups around cap width
        stirrup_length = 2 * (cap_width + cap_length)  # Perimeter approximation
        
        return {
            'longitudinal_bar_length': bar_length,
            'stirrup_perimeter': stirrup_length,
            'skew_angle': skew_angle,
            'cutting_length_adjustment': bar_length * 0.02  # 2% wastage
        }


class SkewVolumeCalculator:
    """
    Calculates concrete volumes for skew bridge elements
    Accounts for increased volumes due to skew geometry
    """
    
    def __init__(self, skew_params: SkewParameters):
        self.skew = skew_params
    
    def get_deck_slab_volume(self, area: float, thickness: float,
                              skew_angle: float) -> Dict:
        """
        Calculate deck slab concrete volume
        Area increases with skew
        """
        skew_rad = math.radians(skew_angle)
        
        # For skew bridges, plan area is larger
        skew_area_factor = 1 / math.cos(skew_rad)
        actual_area = area * skew_area_factor
        
        volume = actual_area * thickness
        
        # Wastage factor for skew cutting
        wastage = volume * 0.05  # 5% extra for skew
        
        return {
            'square_area': area,
            'actual_area': actual_area,
            'thickness': thickness,
            'concrete_volume': volume,
            'wastage': wastage,
            'total_required': volume + wastage,
            'skew_factor': skew_area_factor
        }
    
    def get_abutment_volume(self, height: float, width: float,
                            length: float, skew_angle: float,
                            wing_walls: bool = True) -> Dict:
        """
        Calculate abutment concrete volume
        """
        # Main abutment body
        main_volume = height * width * length
        
        # Due to skew, one side is longer (acute angle side)
        skew_rad = math.radians(skew_angle)
        length_correction = length / math.cos(skew_rad)
        
        adjusted_volume = height * width * length_correction
        
        # Wing walls (if present)
        wing_volume = 0
        if wing_walls:
            # Wing walls are longer on one side for skew bridges
            avg_wing_length = 5.0  # Typical
            wing_height = height * 0.7  # Typical taper
            wing_thickness = 0.45
            
            # Two wing walls with skew correction
            wing_volume = 2 * avg_wing_length * wing_height * wing_thickness * (1 / math.cos(skew_rad))
        
        return {
            'main_abutment_volume': adjusted_volume,
            'wing_wall_volume': wing_volume,
            'total_volume': adjusted_volume + wing_volume,
            'skew_correction': length_correction / length
        }
```

---

### File 2: `skew_bridge_engine.py` - Complete Bridge Calculator

```python
"""
Complete Skew Bridge Geometry Engine
Integrates all calculators for a complete bridge model
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from skew_geometry_core import (
    SkewParameters, SkewDirection, Point2D, Point3D,
    BridgeDeckGeometry, SkewDeckGeometryCalculator,
    SkewSupportGeometryCalculator, SkewWingWallCalculator,
    SkewDimensionCalculator, SkewReinforcementCalculator,
    SkewVolumeCalculator, SkewIntersectionCalculator
)


@dataclass
class SpanConfiguration:
    """Configuration for a single span"""
    span_number: int
    length: float
    type: str = "Simply Supported"
    bearing_type: str = "Elastomeric"
    expansion_joint: bool = False


@dataclass
class SupportConfiguration:
    """Configuration for a support (pier or abutment)"""
    support_number: int
    support_type: str  # "PIER" or "ABUTMENT"
    chainage: float
    width: float
    length: float
    height: float
    foundation_type: str = "Pile"
    num_bearings: int = 3


@dataclass
class SkewBridgeModel:
    """
    Complete geometric model of a skew bridge
    """
    # Basic parameters
    name: str
    chainage: float
    
    # Geometry
    deck: BridgeDeckGeometry
    spans: List[SpanConfiguration] = field(default_factory=list)
    supports: List[SupportConfiguration] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    # Calculators (initialized after creation)
    deck_calc: Optional[SkewDeckGeometryCalculator] = None
    support_calc: Optional[SkewSupportGeometryCalculator] = None
    wing_calc: Optional[SkewWingWallCalculator] = None
    dim_calc: Optional[SkewDimensionCalculator] = None
    rebar_calc: Optional[SkewReinforcementCalculator] = None
    volume_calc: Optional[SkewVolumeCalculator] = None
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        """Initialize all calculators"""
        self.deck_calc = SkewDeckGeometryCalculator(self.deck)
        self.support_calc = SkewSupportGeometryCalculator(self.deck_calc)
        self.wing_calc = SkewWingWallCalculator(self.deck_calc)
        self.dim_calc = SkewDimensionCalculator(self.deck_calc)
        self.rebar_calc = SkewReinforcementCalculator(self.deck.skew)
        self.volume_calc = SkewVolumeCalculator(self.deck.skew)
    
    def add_span(self, length: float, span_type: str = "Simply Supported"):
        """Add a span to the bridge"""
        span_num = len(self.spans) + 1
        self.spans.append(SpanConfiguration(
            span_number=span_num,
            length=length,
            type=span_type
        ))
    
    def add_support(self, support_type: str, chainage: float,
                    width: float, length: float, height: float):
        """Add a support (pier or abutment)"""
        support_num = len(self.supports) + 1
        self.supports.append(SupportConfiguration(
            support_number=support_num,
            support_type=support_type,
            chainage=chainage,
            width=width,
            length=length,
            height=height
        ))
    
    def get_complete_plan_geometry(self) -> Dict:
        """Get complete plan view geometry"""
        geometry = {
            'deck': self.deck_calc.get_deck_corners(),
            'deck_edges': self.deck_calc.get_deck_edges(),
            'centerline': self.deck_calc.get_centerline_points(),
            'supports': []
        }
        
        # Add all supports
        for support in self.supports:
            center = self.support_calc.get_support_center(support.chainage)
            outline = self.support_calc.get_support_outline(
                center, support.width, support.length
            )
            
            # Get bearings
            bearings = self.support_calc.get_bearing_positions(
                center, self.deck.square_width * 0.9  # 90% of deck width
            )
            
            geometry['supports'].append({
                'number': support.support_number,
                'type': support.support_type,
                'chainage': support.chainage,
                'center': center,
                'outline': outline,
                'bearings': bearings
            })
        
        return geometry
    
    def get_dimensioning_data(self) -> Dict:
        """Get all dimension lines and annotations"""
        dims = {
            'span_dimensions': [],
            'overall_dimension': None,
            'width_dimensions': []
        }
        
        # Span dimensions between supports
        for i in range(len(self.supports) - 1):
            dim = self.dim_calc.get_span_dimension_line(
                self.support_calc.get_support_center(self.supports[i].chainage),
                self.support_calc.get_support_center(self.supports[i+1].chainage),
                offset_distance=3.0 + i * 1.5  # Stagger dimensions
            )
            dims['span_dimensions'].append(dim)
        
        # Overall dimension
        if len(self.supports) >= 2:
            dims['overall_dimension'] = self.dim_calc.get_overall_dimension(
                self.support_calc.get_support_center(self.supports[0].chainage),
                self.support_calc.get_support_center(self.supports[-1].chainage),
                offset_distance=8.0
            )
        
        # Width dimensions
        dims['width_dimensions'] = self.dim_calc.get_width_dimensions(
            self.deck_calc.get_deck_corners(),
            num_sections=3
        )
        
        return dims
    
    def get_reinforcement_schedule(self) -> Dict:
        """Get complete reinforcement schedule accounting for skew"""
        schedule = {
            'deck_slab': [],
            'girders': [],
            'supports': []
        }
        
        # Deck slab bars
        main_bars = self.rebar_calc.get_deck_slab_bar_length(
            bar_spacing=0.15,
            bar_diameter=16,
            deck_width=self.deck.square_width,
            is_main_bar=True
        )
        dist_bars = self.rebar_calc.get_deck_slab_bar_length(
            bar_spacing=0.20,
            bar_diameter=12,
            deck_width=self.deck.square_width,
            is_main_bar=False
        )
        
        schedule['deck_slab'] = {
            'main_bars': main_bars,
            'distribution_bars': dist_bars,
            'extra_length_note': f"Add {main_bars['extra_length_due_to_skew']:.2f}m per bar due to {self.deck.skew.skew_angle_deg}° skew"
        }
        
        # Girder bars for each span
        for span in self.spans:
            girder_bars = self.rebar_calc.get_girder_bar_length(
                span_length=span.length,
                girder_depth=2.0,  # Typical
                skew_angle=self.deck.skew.skew_angle_deg,
                bar_type='main'
            )
            stirrups = self.rebar_calc.get_girder_bar_length(
                span_length=span.length,
                girder_depth=2.0,
                skew_angle=self.deck.skew.skew_angle_deg,
                bar_type='stirrup'
            )
            
            schedule['girders'].append({
                'span': span.span_number,
                'main_bars': girder_bars,
                'stirrups': stirrups
            })
        
        return schedule
    
    def get_volume_calculations(self) -> Dict:
        """Get concrete volumes with skew corrections"""
        volumes = {
            'deck_slab': self.volume_calc.get_deck_slab_volume(
                area=self.deck.square_length * self.deck.square_width,
                thickness=0.25,
                skew_angle=self.deck.skew.skew_angle_deg
            ),
            'supports': []
        }
        
        # Support volumes
        for support in self.supports:
            if support.support_type == "ABUTMENT":
                vol = self.volume_calc.get_abutment_volume(
                    height=support.height,
                    width=support.width,
                    length=support.length,
                    skew_angle=self.deck.skew.skew_angle_deg,
                    wing_walls=True
                )
            else:
                # Pier volume (simpler)
                vol = {
                    'volume': support.height * support.width * support.length,
                    'type': 'PIER'
                }
            
            volumes['supports'].append({
                'number': support.support_number,
                'type': support.support_type,
                'volume_data': vol
            })
        
        return volumes
    
    def get_wing_wall_geometry(self, support_number: int,
                                wing_length: float = 5.0,
                                splay_angle: float = 45) -> Dict:
        """Get wing wall geometry for an abutment"""
        support = next((s for s in self.supports 
                       if s.support_number == support_number), None)
        
        if not support or support.support_type != "ABUTMENT":
            return None
        
        center = self.support_calc.get_support_center(support.chainage)
        
        # Left wing
        left_wing = self.wing_calc.get_wing_wall_geometry(
            center, support.width, support.length,
            wing_length, splay_angle, is_left=True
        )
        
        # Right wing
        right_wing = self.wing_calc.get_wing_wall_geometry(
            center, support.width, support.length,
            wing_length, splay_angle, is_left=False
        )
        
        return {
            'abutment_number': support_number,
            'left_wing': left_wing,
            'right_wing': right_wing
        }
    
    def export_to_dxf_data(self) -> Dict:
        """
        Export all geometry in format suitable for DXF generation
        """
        plan_geom = self.get_complete_plan_geometry()
        dims = self.get_dimensioning_data()
        
        dxf_data = {
            'layers': {
                'DECK': {'color': 1, 'entities': []},
                'CARRIAGEWAY': {'color': 2, 'entities': []},
                'PIER': {'color': 4, 'entities': []},
                'ABUTMENT': {'color': 3, 'entities': []},
                'BEARING': {'color': 5, 'entities': []},
                'CENTERLINE': {'color': 6, 'entities': []},
                'DIMENSION': {'color': 8, 'entities': []},
                'WINGWALL': {'color': 9, 'entities': []},
                'TEXT': {'color': 7, 'entities': []}
            },
            'title_block': {
                'bridge_name': self.name,
                'chainage': self.chainage,
                'skew_angle': self.deck.skew.skew_angle_deg,
                'total_length': sum(s.length for s in self.spans),
                'num_spans': len(self.spans)
            }
        }
        
        # Add deck outline
        deck_corners = plan_geom['deck']
        deck_points = [
            deck_corners['LEFT_START'].to_tuple(),
            deck_corners['RIGHT_START'].to_tuple(),
            deck_corners['RIGHT_END'].to_tuple(),
            deck_corners['LEFT_END'].to_tuple(),
            deck_corners['LEFT_START'].to_tuple()  # Close
        ]
        dxf_data['layers']['DECK']['entities'].append({
            'type': 'LWPOLYLINE',
            'points': deck_points,
            'closed': True
        })
        
        # Add supports
        for support in plan_geom['supports']:
            outline = support['outline']
            support_points = [
                outline['CORNER_1'].to_tuple(),
                outline['CORNER_2'].to_tuple(),
                outline['CORNER_3'].to_tuple(),
                outline['CORNER_4'].to_tuple(),
                outline['CORNER_1'].to_tuple()
            ]
            
            layer = 'PIER' if support['type'] == 'PIER' else 'ABUTMENT'
            dxf_data['layers'][layer]['entities'].append({
                'type': 'LWPOLYLINE',
                'points': support_points,
                'closed': True
            })
            
            # Add bearings
            for bearing in support['bearings']:
                dxf_data['layers']['BEARING']['entities'].append({
                    'type': 'CIRCLE',
                    'center': bearing.to_tuple(),
                    'radius': 0.2
                })
        
        # Add centerline
        centerline_points = [p.to_tuple() for p in plan_geom['centerline']]
        dxf_data['layers']['CENTERLINE']['entities'].append({
            'type': 'LWPOLYLINE',
            'points': centerline_points,
            'closed': False
        })
        
        # Add dimensions
        for span_dim in dims['span_dimensions']:
            dxf_data['layers']['DIMENSION']['entities'].append({
                'type': 'DIMENSION',
                'start': span_dim['support1'].to_tuple(),
                'end': span_dim['support2'].to_tuple(),
                'text': f"SPAN = {span_dim['span_length']:.2f}m"
            })
        
        return dxf_data


# Factory function for common bridge types
def create_skew_bridge(name: str, chainage: float,
                       span_lengths: List[float],
                       deck_width: float,
                       skew_angle: float,
                       skew_direction: str = "RIGHT") -> SkewBridgeModel:
    """
    Factory function to create a complete skew bridge model
    
    Args:
        name: Bridge name
        chainage: Chainage along alignment
        span_lengths: List of span lengths in meters
        deck_width: Width of deck in meters
        skew_angle: Skew angle in degrees
        skew_direction: "LEFT" or "RIGHT"
    """
    # Calculate total length
    total_length = sum(span_lengths)
    
    # Create skew parameters
    direction = SkewDirection.RIGHT if skew_direction == "RIGHT" else SkewDirection.LEFT
    skew = SkewParameters(
        skew_angle_deg=skew_angle,
        skew_direction=direction
    )
    
    # Create deck geometry
    deck = BridgeDeckGeometry(
        square_length=total_length,
        square_width=deck_width,
        skew=skew
    )
    
    # Create bridge model
    bridge = SkewBridgeModel(
        name=name,
        chainage=chainage,
        deck=deck
    )
    
    # Add spans
    for length in span_lengths:
        bridge.add_span(length)
    
    # Add supports (abutments at ends, piers in between)
    current_chainage = chainage
    
    # Left abutment
    bridge.add_support("ABUTMENT", current_chainage, 1.5, deck_width + 1.0, 8.0)
    
    # Piers and intermediate supports
    for i, span in enumerate(span_lengths[:-1]):
        current_chainage += span.length
        bridge.add_support("PIER", current_chainage, 2.0, deck_width + 1.0, 10.0)
    
    # Right abutment
    current_chainage += span_lengths[-1].length if span_lengths else 0
    bridge.add_support("ABUTMENT", current_chainage, 1.5, deck_width + 1.0, 8.0)
    
    return bridge
```

### File 3: `example_usage.py` - Complete Example

```python
"""
Example usage of Skew Bridge Geometry Engine
Demonstrates all capabilities with a real-world example
"""

from skew_bridge_engine import create_skew_bridge, SkewBridgeModel
from skew_geometry_core import SkewParameters, SkewDirection, Point2D


def example_chambakkara_bridge():
    """
    Create Chambakkara Bridge model (from your Scribd reference)
    3-span bridge with 15° skew
    """
    print("=" * 70)
    print("CHAMBAKKARA BRIDGE - SKEW GEOMETRY CALCULATION")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    # Create bridge model
    bridge = create_skew_bridge(
        name="Chambakkara Bridge",
        chainage=600.000,
        span_lengths=[25.0, 30.0, 25.0],  # 3 spans
        deck_width=12.0,  # 12m carriageway
        skew_angle=15.0,
        skew_direction="RIGHT"
    )
    
    print(f"\n1. BRIDGE CONFIGURATION")
    print(f"   Name: {bridge.name}")
    print(f"   Chainage: {bridge.chainage:.3f}")
    print(f"   Skew Angle: {bridge.deck.skew.skew_angle_deg}° {bridge.deck.skew.skew_direction.name}")
    print(f"   Number of Spans: {len(bridge.spans)}")
    print(f"   Span Configuration: {[s.length for s in bridge.spans]}")
    
    print(f"\n2. DECK GEOMETRY (SQUARE vs SKEW)")
    print(f"   Square Length: {bridge.deck.square_length:.2f}m")
    print(f"   Projected Length: {bridge.deck.projected_length:.2f}m")
    print(f"   Length Increase: {(bridge.deck.projected_length/bridge.deck.square_length - 1)*100:.1f}%")
    print(f"   Square Width: {bridge.deck.square_width:.2f}m")
    print(f"   Projected Width: {bridge.deck.projected_width:.2f}m")
    
    print(f"\n3. DECK CORNER COORDINATES")
    corners = bridge.deck_calc.get_deck_corners()
    for name, point in corners.items():
        print(f"   {name:15s}: X={point.x:8.3f}m, Y={point.y:8.3f}m")
    
    print(f"\n4. SUPPORT LOCATIONS")
    plan_geom = bridge.get_complete_plan_geometry()
    for support in plan_geom['supports']:
        print(f"   {support['type']} #{support['number']} at CH {support['chainage']:.3f}")
        print(f"      Center: X={support['center'].x:.3f}, Y={support['center'].y:.3f}")
        print(f"      Bearings: {len(support['bearings'])} positions")
        for i, bearing in enumerate(support['bearings'], 1):
            print(f"         Bearing {i}: X={bearing.x:.3f}, Y={bearing.y:.3f}")
    
    print(f"\n5. DIMENSIONING DATA")
    dims = bridge.get_dimensioning_data()
    
    print(f"   Span Dimensions:")
    for i, span_dim in enumerate(dims['span_dimensions'], 1):
        print(f"      Span {i}: {span_dim['span_length']:.2f}m")
    
    if dims['overall_dimension']:
        od = dims['overall_dimension']
        print(f"   Overall Length: {od['total_length']:.2f}m")
    
    print(f"   Width Dimensions (varies along bridge due to skew):")
    for i, width_dim in enumerate(dims['width_dimensions'], 1):
        print(f"      Section {i} (at {width_dim['chainage_ratio']*100:.0f}%): {width_dim['width']:.2f}m")
    
    print(f"\n6. REINFORCEMENT SCHEDULE (with skew corrections)")
    rebar = bridge.get_reinforcement_schedule()
    
    deck = rebar['deck_slab']
    print(f"   Deck Slab Main Bars:")
    print(f"      Straight Length: {deck['main_bars']['straight_length']:.2f}m")
    print(f"      Actual Length: {deck['main_bars']['actual_bar_length']:.2f}m")
    print(f"      Skew Factor: {deck['main_bars']['skew_correction_factor']:.3f}")
    print(f"      Extra per bar: {deck['main_bars']['actual_bar_length'] - deck['main_bars']['straight_length']:.2f}m")
    
    print(f"\n   Girder Reinforcement:")
    for girder in rebar['girders']:
        print(f"      Span {girder['span']['span_number']}:")
        print(f"         Main bars: {girder['main_bars']['actual_bar_length']:.2f}m")
        print(f"         Stirrups: {girder['stirrups']['number_of_stirrups']} nos")
        print(f"         Extra stirrups due to skew: {girder['stirrups']['extra_stirrups_due_to_skew']}")
    
    print(f"\n7. CONCRETE VOLUMES (with skew corrections)")
    volumes = bridge.get_volume_calculations()
    
    deck_vol = volumes['deck_slab']
    print(f"   Deck Slab:")
    print(f"      Square Area: {deck_vol['square_area']:.2f}m²")
    print(f"      Actual Area: {deck_vol['actual_area']:.2f}m²")
    print(f"      Concrete: {deck_vol['concrete_volume']:.2f}m³")
    print(f"      Wastage (5%): {deck_vol['wastage']:.2f}m³")
    print(f"      Total Required: {deck_vol['total_required']:.2f}m³")
    
    print(f"\n   Supports:")
    for support in volumes['supports']:
        print(f"      {support['type']} #{support['number']}: {support['volume_data']}")
    
    print(f"\n8. WING WALL GEOMETRY")
    wing_geom = bridge.get_wing_wall_geometry(1, wing_length=6.0, splay_angle=45)
    if wing_geom:
        print(f"   Abutment #1 Wing Walls:")
        print(f"      Left Wing: {wing_geom['left_wing']['wall_length']:.2f}m at {wing_geom['left_wing']['wall_angle']:.1f}°")
        print(f"      Right Wing: {wing_geom['right_wing']['wall_length']:.2f}m at {wing_geom['right_wing']['wall_angle']:.1f}°")
    
    print(f"\n9. DXF EXPORT DATA")
    dxf_data = bridge.export_to_dxf_data()
    print(f"   Layers: {list(dxf_data['layers'].keys())}")
    print(f"   Title Block: {dxf_data['title_block']}")
    
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("CALCULATION COMPLETE")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    return bridge


def example_extreme_skew():
    """Demonstrate with extreme 45° skew"""
    print("\n\nEXTREME SKEW EXAMPLE (45°)")
    print("=" * 50)
    
    bridge = create_skew_bridge(
        name="High Skew Bridge",
        chainage=1000.000,
        span_lengths=[20.0, 20.0],
        deck_width=10.0,
        skew_angle=45.0,
        skew_direction="LEFT"
    )
    
    print(f"Square Length: {bridge.deck.square_length:.2f}m")
    print(f"Projected Length: {bridge.deck.projected_length:.2f}m")
    print(f"Length Increase: {(bridge.deck.projected_length/bridge.deck.square_length - 1)*100:.1f}%")
    
    # Show corner coordinates
    corners = bridge.deck_calc.get_deck_corners()
    print("\nCorner Coordinates:")
    for name, point in corners.items():
        print(f"  {name}: ({point.x:.2f}, {point.y:.2f})")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Run main example
    bridge = example_chambakkara_bridge()
    
    # Run extreme example
    example_extreme_skew()
```
