## COMPLETE PYTHON MODULE

### File 1: `longitudinal_section_core.py` - Core Calculations

```python
"""
Longitudinal Section Generator for Bridges
Handles ground profile, deck levels, foundations, and hydraulic data
"""

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Optional
from enum import Enum
import numpy as np
from scipy.interpolate import interp1d


class ProfileType(Enum):
    """Type of ground profile interpolation"""
    LINEAR = "linear"
    CUBIC = "cubic"
    STEP = "step"


@dataclass
class SurveyPoint:
    """Ground survey point with chainage and reduced level"""
    chainage: float
    rl: float
    description: str = ""
    
    def to_tuple(self) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        return (self.chainage, self.rl)


@dataclass
class HydraulicLevels:
    """Water and scour levels"""
    hfl: float  # High Flood Level
    lwl: float  # Low Water Level
    normal_water_level: float
    scour_level: float
    design_scour_level: float
    bed_level: float
    
    @property
    def scour_depth(self) -> float:
        """Calculated scour depth"""
        return self.bed_level - self.scour_level
    
    @property
    def freeboard(self, deck_level: float = None) -> float:
        """Freeboard above HFL"""
        if deck_level:
            return deck_level - self.hfl
        return 0


@dataclass
class DeckLevels:
    """Deck elevation data"""
    road_level: float
    wearing_coat_thickness: float = 0.065  # 65mm typical
    deck_thickness: float = 0.25  # 250mm typical
    girder_depth: float = 2.0  # 2m typical for T-beam
    
    @property
    def top_of_deck(self) -> float:
        """Top of structural deck (below wearing coat)"""
        return self.road_level - self.wearing_coat_thickness
    
    @property
    def soffit_level(self) -> float:
        """Bottom of deck/girder soffit"""
        return self.top_of_deck - self.deck_thickness - self.girder_depth
    
    @property
    def bearing_level(self) -> float:
        """Top of bearing level"""
        return self.soffit_level - 0.05  # 50mm bearing thickness typical


@dataclass
class FoundationLevels:
    """Foundation elevation data"""
    foundation_type: str  # "PILE", "WELL", "OPEN"
    cutoff_level: float
    pile_cap_top: float = None
    pile_cap_bottom: float = None
    pile_toe_level: float = None
    well_curb_level: float = None
    well_steining_bottom: float = None
    well_sinking_depth: float = None
    
    # For open foundation
    foundation_top: float = None
    foundation_bottom: float = None
    
    @property
    def foundation_depth(self) -> float:
        """Depth of foundation below ground"""
        if self.foundation_type == "PILE" and self.pile_toe_level:
            return self.cutoff_level - self.pile_toe_level
        elif self.foundation_type == "WELL" and self.well_curb_level:
            return self.cutoff_level - self.well_curb_level
        elif self.foundation_type == "OPEN" and self.foundation_bottom:
            return self.cutoff_level - self.foundation_bottom
        return 0


@dataclass
class SupportElevation:
    """Complete elevation data for a support"""
    support_number: int
    support_type: str  # "ABUTMENT" or "PIER"
    chainage: float
    ground_level: float
    deck_levels: DeckLevels
    foundation: FoundationLevels
    bearing_type: str = "Elastomeric"
    bearing_height: float = 0.05
    
    @property
    def pier_cap_top(self) -> float:
        """Top of pier/abutment cap"""
        return self.deck_levels.bearing_level - self.bearing_height
    
    @property
    def pier_height(self) -> float:
        """Height from foundation to pier cap"""
        return self.pier_cap_top - self.foundation.cutoff_level


@dataclass
class LongitudinalProfile:
    """Complete longitudinal section data"""
    bridge_name: str
    chainage: float
    total_length: float
    
    # Survey data
    survey_points: List[SurveyPoint] = field(default_factory=list)
    profile_type: ProfileType = ProfileType.CUBIC
    
    # Hydraulic data
    hydraulic: HydraulicLevels = None
    
    # Support elevations
    supports: List[SupportElevation] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    # Ground profile interpolation function
    _ground_interpolator = field(default=None, repr=False)
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        """Create ground profile interpolator"""
        if len(self.survey_points) >= 2:
            points = sorted(self.survey_points, key=lambda p: p.chainage)
            chainages = [p.chainage for p in points]
            rls = [p.rl for p in points]
            
            if self.profile_type == ProfileType.LINEAR:
                self._ground_interpolator = interp1d(
                    chainages, rls, kind='linear', 
                    fill_value='extrapolate'
                )
            elif self.profile_type == ProfileType.CUBIC:
                self._ground_interpolator = interp1d(
                    chainages, rls, kind='cubic',
                    fill_value='extrapolate'
                )
    
    def get_ground_level(self, chainage: float) -> float:
        """Get interpolated ground level at any chainage"""
        if self._ground_interpolator is None:
            # Return average if no survey data
            return sum(p.rl for p in self.survey_points) / len(self.survey_points) if self.survey_points else 0
        return float(self._ground_interpolator(chainage))
    
    def generate_profile_points(self, num_points: int = 200) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
        """Generate smooth ground profile points for drawing"""
        if not self.survey_points:
            return []
        
        start_ch = min(p.chainage for p in self.survey_points)
        end_ch = max(p.chainage for p in self.survey_points)
        
        chainages = np.linspace(start_ch, end_ch, num_points)
        levels = [self.get_ground_level(ch) for ch in chainages]
        
        return list(zip(chainages, levels))
    
    def get_vertical_clearance(self, chainage: float) -> float:
        """Get vertical clearance at any point"""
        ground = self.get_ground_level(chainage)
        
        # Find nearest support to get deck level
        nearest_support = min(self.supports, 
                            key=lambda s: abs(s.chainage - chainage))
        
        # Interpolate deck level between supports
        if len(self.supports) >= 2:
            # Find surrounding supports
            sorted_supports = sorted(self.supports, key=lambda s: s.chainage)
            for i in range(len(sorted_supports) - 1):
                s1, s2 = sorted_supports[i], sorted_supports[i + 1]
                if s1.chainage <= chainage <= s2.chainage:
                    # Linear interpolation
                    t = (chainage - s1.chainage) / (s2.chainage - s1.chainage)
                    deck_level = s1.deck_levels.soffit_level + t * (
                        s2.deck_levels.soffit_level - s1.deck_levels.soffit_level
                    )
                    return deck_level - ground
        
        # Default to nearest support
        return nearest_support.deck_levels.soffit_level - ground


class GroundProfileGenerator:
    """Generate realistic ground profiles from sparse survey data"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def from_sparse_points(points: List[Tuple[float, float]], 
                          interpolation: str = "cubic") -> callable:
        """
        Create interpolator from sparse (chainage, RL) points
        """
        chainages = [p[0] for p in points]
        rls = [p[1] for p in points]
        
        if len(points) < 3:
            interpolation = "linear"
        
        return interp1d(chainages, rls, kind=interpolation, 
                       fill_value='extrapolate')
    
    @staticmethod
    def add_river_channel(profile_func: callable,
                         center_chainage: float,
                         channel_width: float,
                         channel_depth: float,
                         bank_slope: float = 0.5) -> callable:
        """
        Modify profile to include river channel
        """
        def modified_profile(chainage: float) -> float:
            base_rl = float(profile_func(chainage))
            
            # Distance from channel center
            dist = abs(chainage - center_chainage)
            half_width = channel_width / 2
            
            if dist <= half_width:
                # Inside channel - deeper
                depth_factor = math.cos(dist / half_width * math.pi / 2)
                return base_rl - channel_depth * depth_factor
            else:
                # Outside channel - sloping banks
                bank_dist = dist - half_width
                bank_drop = min(bank_dist * bank_slope, channel_depth)
                return base_rl - max(0, channel_depth - bank_drop)
        
        return modified_profile
    
    @staticmethod
    def generate_random_profile(start_ch: float, end_ch: float,
                               base_rl: float = 175.0,
                               variation: float = 5.0,
                                num_points: int = 20) -> List[SurveyPoint]:
        """Generate random ground profile for testing"""
        np.random.seed(42)
        chainages = np.linspace(start_ch, end_ch, num_points)
        
        # Create smooth variation using cumulative sum of random steps
        steps = np.random.normal(0, variation/5, num_points)
        rls = base_rl + np.cumsum(steps)
        
        # Smooth with moving average
        window = 3
        rls_smooth = np.convolve(rls, np.ones(window)/window, mode='same')
        
        points = []
        for ch, rl in zip(chainages, rls_smooth):
            points.append(SurveyPoint(
                chainage=float(ch),
                rl=float(rl),
                description="Survey Point"
            ))
        
        return points


class LevelCalculator:
    """Calculate levels at various bridge components"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def calculate_deck_levels(road_level: float,
                             wearing_coat: float = 0.065,
                             deck_thickness: float = 0.25,
                             girder_type: str = "T-BEAM",
                             span_length: float = 30.0) -> DeckLevels:
        """
        Calculate all deck-related levels
        """
        # Girder depth based on span (typical span/depth ratio 15)
        if girder_type == "T-BEAM":
            girder_depth = span_length / 15
        elif girder_type == "BOX_GIRDER":
            girder_depth = span_length / 20
        else:
            girder_depth = span_length / 12
        
        return DeckLevels(
            road_level=road_level,
            wearing_coat_thickness=wearing_coat,
            deck_thickness=deck_thickness,
            girder_depth=girder_depth
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def calculate_foundation_levels(foundation_type: str,
                                    ground_level: float,
                                    scour_level: float,
                                    hfl: float,
                                    soil_type: str = "NORMAL") -> FoundationLevels:
        """
        Calculate foundation levels based on type and hydraulic conditions
        """
        # Foundation depth below scour
        if foundation_type == "PILE":
            # Pile foundation
            cutoff = scour_level - 0.5  # 0.5m below scour
            pile_cap_thickness = 1.5
            pile_length = 15.0  # Typical
            
            return FoundationLevels(
                foundation_type="PILE",
                cutoff_level=cutoff,
                pile_cap_top=cutoff,
                pile_cap_bottom=cutoff - pile_cap_thickness,
                pile_toe_level=cutoff - pile_cap_thickness - pile_length
            )
        
        elif foundation_type == "WELL":
            # Well foundation
            cutoff = scour_level - 0.5
            curb_height = 2.0
            sinking_depth = 12.0
            
            return FoundationLevels(
                foundation_type="WELL",
                cutoff_level=cutoff,
                well_curb_level=cutoff - curb_height - sinking_depth,
                well_steining_bottom=cutoff - sinking_depth,
                well_sinking_depth=sinking_depth
            )
        
        else:  # OPEN foundation
            # Open foundation (raft or individual footing)
            foundation_depth = max(2.0, abs(ground_level - scour_level) + 1.0)
            
            return FoundationLevels(
                foundation_type="OPEN",
                cutoff_level=ground_level,
                foundation_top=ground_level - 0.5,
                foundation_bottom=ground_level - foundation_depth
            )
    
    @staticmethod
    def calculate_hydraulic_levels(hfl: float,
                                   bed_level: float,
                                   scour_factor: float = 2.0) -> HydraulicLevels:
        """
        Calculate all hydraulic and scour levels
        """
        # Scour depth calculation (simplified Lacey's formula)
        # Normal scour depth = 1.34 * (Q/f)^(1/3)
        # Here using simplified approach
        
        scour_depth = (hfl - bed_level) * scour_factor
        scour_level = bed_level - scour_depth
        
        return HydraulicLevels(
            hfl=hfl,
            lwl=hfl - 3.0,  # Typical 3m below HFL
            normal_water_level=hfl - 1.5,
            scour_level=scour_level,
            design_scour_level=scour_level - 0.5,  # Additional safety
            bed_level=bed_level
        )


class LongitudinalGeometryCalculator:
    """
    Calculate all geometric elements for longitudinal section
    Handles skew bridges by projecting onto bridge centerline
    """
    
    def __init__(self, profile: LongitudinalProfile, skew_angle: float = 0):
        self.profile = profile
        self.skew_angle = skew_angle
        self.skew_rad = math.radians(skew_angle)
    
    def get_support_elevations(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """Get complete elevation data for all supports"""
        elevations = []
        
        for support in self.profile.supports:
            elev_data = {
                'number': support.support_number,
                'type': support.support_type,
                'chainage': support.chainage,
                'ground_level': support.ground_level,
                'road_level': support.deck_levels.road_level,
                'top_of_deck': support.deck_levels.top_of_deck,
                'soffit_level': support.deck_levels.soffit_level,
                'bearing_level': support.deck_levels.bearing_level,
                'pier_cap_top': support.pier_cap_top,
                'foundation_cutoff': support.foundation.cutoff_level,
                'foundation_bottom': self._get_foundation_bottom(support.foundation),
                'pier_height': support.pier_height
            }
            elevations.append(elev_data)
        
        return elevations
    
    def _get_foundation_bottom(self, foundation: FoundationLevels) -> float:
        """Get bottom level of foundation"""
        if foundation.foundation_type == "PILE":
            return foundation.pile_toe_level or foundation.cutoff_level - 15
        elif foundation.foundation_type == "WELL":
            return foundation.well_curb_level or foundation.cutoff_level - 12
        else:
            return foundation.foundation_bottom or foundation.cutoff_level - 3
    
    def get_longitudinal_section_outline(self) -> Dict:
        """
        Get complete outline of bridge in longitudinal section
        Returns polygons for each component
        """
        # Sort supports by chainage
        supports = sorted(self.profile.supports, key=lambda s: s.chainage)
        
        if len(supports) < 2:
            return {}
        
        # Ground profile
        ground_points = self.profile.generate_profile_points(200)
        
        # Deck outline (top and soffit)
        deck_top_points = []
        deck_soffit_points = []
        
        for ch, _ in ground_points:
            # Interpolate deck levels
            deck_level = self._interpolate_deck_level(ch, supports, 'top_of_deck')
            soffit_level = self._interpolate_deck_level(ch, supports, 'soffit_level')
            
            deck_top_points.append((ch, deck_level))
            deck_soffit_points.append((ch, soffit_level))
        
        # Support outlines
        support_outlines = []
        for support in supports:
            outline = self._get_support_outline(support)
            support_outlines.append(outline)
        
        # Hydraulic levels
        hydraulic_lines = self._get_hydraulic_lines(supports[0].chainage, 
                                                    supports[-1].chainage)
        
        return {
            'ground': ground_points,
            'deck_top': deck_top_points,
            'deck_soffit': deck_soffit_points,
            'supports': support_outlines,
            'hydraulic': hydraulic_lines
        }
    
    def _interpolate_deck_level(self, chainage: float, supports: List[SupportElevation],
                                 level_type: str) -> float:
        """Interpolate deck level between supports"""
        # Find surrounding supports
        for i in range(len(supports) - 1):
            s1, s2 = supports[i], supports[i + 1]
            if s1.chainage <= chainage <= s2.chainage:
                t = (chainage - s1.chainage) / (s2.chainage - s1.chainage)
                
                if level_type == 'top_of_deck':
                    l1, l2 = s1.deck_levels.top_of_deck, s2.deck_levels.top_of_deck
                elif level_type == 'soffit_level':
                    l1, l2 = s1.deck_levels.soffit_level, s2.deck_levels.soffit_level
                else:
                    l1, l2 = s1.deck_levels.road_level, s2.deck_levels.road_level
                
                return l1 + t * (l2 - l1)
        
        # Return nearest
        nearest = min(supports, key=lambda s: abs(s.chainage - chainage))
        if level_type == 'top_of_deck':
            return nearest.deck_levels.top_of_deck
        elif level_type == 'soffit_level':
            return nearest.deck_levels.soffit_level
        return nearest.deck_levels.road_level
    
    def _get_support_outline(self, support: SupportElevation) -> Dict:
        """Get polygon outline for a support"""
        width = 2.0 if support.support_type == "PIER" else 1.5
        
        # Support extends from foundation to pier cap
        foundation_bottom = self._get_foundation_bottom(support.foundation)
        
        return {
            'chainage': support.chainage,
            'type': support.support_type,
            'width': width,
            'top': support.pier_cap_top,
            'bottom': foundation_bottom,
            'ground_level': support.ground_level,
            'left': support.chainage - width/2,
            'right': support.chainage + width/2,
            'foundation_type': support.foundation.foundation_type
        }
    
    def _get_hydraulic_lines(self, start_ch: float, end_ch: float) -> Dict:
        """Get horizontal lines for hydraulic levels"""
        if not self.profile.hydraulic:
            return {}
        
        h = self.profile.hydraulic
        
        return {
            'hfl': {'level': h.hfl, 'start': start_ch, 'end': end_ch},
            'lwl': {'level': h.lwl, 'start': start_ch, 'end': end_ch},
            'normal_water': {'level': h.normal_water_level, 'start': start_ch, 'end': end_ch},
            'scour_level': {'level': h.scour_level, 'start': start_ch, 'end': end_ch},
            'bed_level': {'level': h.bed_level, 'start': start_ch, 'end': end_ch}
        }
    
    def get_dimension_data(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """Get vertical dimensions for annotation"""
        dimensions = []
        supports = sorted(self.profile.supports, key=lambda s: s.chainage)
        
        for support in supports:
            ch = support.chainage
            
            # Foundation depth dimension
            foundation_bottom = self._get_foundation_bottom(support.foundation)
            dimensions.append({
                'type': 'FOUNDATION_DEPTH',
                'chainage': ch - 1.0,
                'top': support.foundation.cutoff_level,
                'bottom': foundation_bottom,
                'value': support.foundation.cutoff_level - foundation_bottom,
                'text': f"FD = {support.foundation.cutoff_level - foundation_bottom:.2f}m"
            })
            
            # Pier height dimension
            dimensions.append({
                'type': 'PIER_HEIGHT',
                'chainage': ch + 1.0,
                'top': support.pier_cap_top,
                'bottom': support.foundation.cutoff_level,
                'value': support.pier_height,
                'text': f"H = {support.pier_height:.2f}m"
            })
            
            # Vertical clearance
            ground = support.ground_level
            soffit = support.deck_levels.soffit_level
            dimensions.append({
                'type': 'VERTICAL_CLEARANCE',
                'chainage': ch + 2.5,
                'top': soffit,
                'bottom': ground,
                'value': soffit - ground,
                'text': f"VC = {soffit - ground:.2f}m"
            })
        
        # Span dimensions
        for i in range(len(supports) - 1):
            s1, s2 = supports[i], supports[i + 1]
            mid_ch = (s1.chainage + s2.chainage) / 2
            
            dimensions.append({
                'type': 'SPAN_LENGTH',
                'chainage': mid_ch,
                'start_support': s1.chainage,
                'end_support': s2.chainage,
                'value': s2.chainage - s1.chainage,
                'level': min(s1.deck_levels.soffit_level, s2.deck_levels.soffit_level) - 2.0,
                'text': f"SPAN = {s2.chainage - s1.chainage:.2f}m"
            })
        
        return dimensions
    
    def get_reinforcement_bar_lengths(self) -> Dict:
        """
        Calculate bar lengths for longitudinal section elements
        """
        bars = {
            'pile_bars': [],
            'pier_vertical_bars': [],
            'pier_cap_bars': [],
            'deck_main_bars': []
        }
        
        supports = sorted(self.profile.supports, key=lambda s: s.chainage)
        
        for support in supports:
            # Pile bars (if pile foundation)
            if support.foundation.foundation_type == "PILE":
                pile_length = (support.foundation.cutoff_level - 
                             support.foundation.pile_toe_level)
                bars['pile_bars'].append({
                    'support': support.support_number,
                    'length': pile_length,
                    'diameter': 20,  # mm
                    'number': 12,  # per pile
                    'total_length': pile_length * 12
                })
            
            # Pier vertical bars
            pier_bar_length = support.pier_height + 1.0  # Lap length
            bars['pier_vertical_bars'].append({
                'support': support.support_number,
                'length': pier_bar_length,
                'diameter': 25,
                'number': 24,
                'spacing': 0.15
            })
            
            # Pier cap bars
            cap_length = 12.0  # Typical
            bars['pier_cap_bars'].append({
                'support': support.support_number,
                'length': cap_length,
                'diameter': 20,
                'number': 20
            })
        
        # Deck main bars (continuous through spans)
        total_length = supports[-1].chainage - supports[0].chainage
        bars['deck_main_bars'] = [{
            'total_length': total_length,
            'diameter': 16,
            'number_per_girder': 8,
            'laps': len(supports) - 1
        }]
        
        return bars
```

---

### File 2: `longitudinal_section_drawing.py` - DXF Generator

```python
"""
Longitudinal Section Drawing Generator
Creates professional DXF drawings of bridge longitudinal sections
"""

import ezdxf
from ezdxf.enums import TextEntityAlignment
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
from longitudinal_section_core import (
    LongitudinalProfile, LongitudinalGeometryCalculator,
    SupportElevation, HydraulicLevels
)


class LongitudinalSectionDrawing:
    """
    Generate longitudinal section drawing in DXF format
    """
    
    # Layer definitions
    LAYERS = {
        'GROUND': {'color': 3, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.25},
        'DECK_TOP': {'color': 1, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.35},
        'DECK_SOFFIT': {'color': 1, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.30},
        'GIRDER': {'color': 4, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.25},
        'PIER': {'color': 5, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.35},
        'FOUNDATION': {'color': 6, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.30},
        'PILE': {'color': 6, 'linetype': 'DASHED', 'lineweight': 0.18},
        'HFL': {'color': 131, 'linetype': 'DASHED', 'lineweight': 0.18},  # Cyan
        'LWL': {'color': 141, 'linetype': 'DASHED', 'lineweight': 0.18},  # Blue
        'SCOUR': {'color': 32, 'linetype': 'DASHED', 'lineweight': 0.18},  # Green
        'BED_LEVEL': {'color': 8, 'linetype': 'DASHED', 'lineweight': 0.13},
        'DIMENSION': {'color': 7, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.18},
        'TEXT': {'color': 7, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.18},
        'HATCH': {'color': 253, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.13},
        'CENTERLINE': {'color': 6, 'linetype': 'CENTER', 'lineweight': 0.18}
    }
    
    def __init__(self, profile: LongitudinalProfile, 
                 drawing_scale: float = 1.0,
                 vertical_exaggeration: float = 2.0):
        self.profile = profile
        self.calc = LongitudinalGeometryCalculator(profile)
        self.scale = drawing_scale
        self.v_exag = vertical_exaggeration
        
        self.doc = ezdxf.new('R2018', setup=True)
        self.msp = self.doc.modelspace()
        self.setup_layers()
        self.setup_styles()
        
        # Calculate drawing extents
        self._calculate_extents()
    
    def setup_layers(self):
        """Create all required layers"""
        for name, attrs in self.LAYERS.items():
            self.doc.layers.add(
                name=name,
                color=attrs['color'],
                linetype=attrs['linetype'],
                lineweight=attrs['lineweight']
            )
    
    def setup_styles(self):
        """Setup text and dimension styles"""
        self.doc.styles.add('SECTION_FONT', font='Arial.ttf')
        
        # Dimension style
        dimstyle = self.doc.dimstyles.add('SECTION_DIM')
        dimstyle.dxf.dimtxt = 2.0  # Text height
        dimstyle.dxf.dimexe = 1.0
        dimstyle.dxf.dimexo = 0.5
        dimstyle.dxf.dimtad = 1
        dimstyle.dxf.dimdec = 2
    
    def _calculate_extents(self):
        """Calculate drawing extents for positioning"""
        supports = sorted(self.profile.supports, key=lambda s: s.chainage)
        
        self.start_ch = supports[0].chainage - 10
        self.end_ch = supports[-1].chainage + 10
        
        all_levels = []
        for s in supports:
            all_levels.extend([
                s.deck_levels.road_level,
                s.foundation.cutoff_level - 15  # Approximate foundation bottom
            ])
        
        if self.profile.hydraulic:
            all_levels.extend([
                self.profile.hydraulic.hfl,
                self.profile.hydraulic.scour_level
            ])
        
        self.max_level = max(all_levels) + 2
        self.min_level = min(all_levels) - 2
    
    def _to_dxf_coords(self, chainage: float, level: float) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """Convert real-world coordinates to DXF coordinates"""
        x = (chainage - self.start_ch) * self.scale
        y = (level - self.min_level) * self.scale * self.v_exag
        return (x, y)
    
    def draw_ground_profile(self):
        """Draw ground profile line"""
        ground_points = self.profile.generate_profile_points(200)
        
        dxf_points = [self._to_dxf_coords(ch, rl) for ch, rl in ground_points]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=dxf_points,
            close=False,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'GROUND'}
        )
        
        # Add hatching below ground
        # Create closed polygon from ground to bottom
        bottom_y = self._to_dxf_coords(self.start_ch, self.min_level)[1]
        hatch_points = dxf_points + [
            self._to_dxf_coords(self.end_ch, self.min_level),
            self._to_dxf_coords(self.start_ch, self.min_level)
        ]
        
        hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=3, dxfattribs={'layer': 'HATCH'})
        hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(hatch_points, is_closed=True)
        hatch.set_pattern_fill('EARTH', scale=1.0)
    
    def draw_hydraulic_levels(self):
        """Draw HFL, LWL, Scour, and Bed Level lines"""
        if not self.profile.hydraulic:
            return
        
        h = self.profile.hydraulic
        
        levels = [
            ('HFL', h.hfl, 'HFL'),
            ('LWL', h.lwl, 'LWL'),
            ('SCOUR', h.scour_level, f"SCOUR LVL = {h.scour_level:.2f}"),
            ('BED_LEVEL', h.bed_level, f"BED LVL = {h.bed_level:.2f}")
        ]
        
        for layer, level, label in levels:
            y = self._to_dxf_coords(self.start_ch, level)[1]
            
            # Draw horizontal line
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf_coords(self.start_ch, level),
                end=self._to_dxf_coords(self.end_ch, level),
                dxfattribs={'layer': layer}
            )
            
            # Add label at left side
            text_pos = self._to_dxf_coords(self.start_ch - 5, level)
            self.msp.add_text(
                label,
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(text_pos, align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_RIGHT)
            
            # Add level value at right side
            value_pos = self._to_dxf_coords(self.end_ch + 2, level)
            self.msp.add_text(
                f"{level:.3f}",
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(value_pos, align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT)
    
    def draw_deck(self):
        """Draw deck structure"""
        outline = self.calc.get_longitudinal_section_outline()
        
        if not outline:
            return
        
        # Draw deck top
        deck_top_points = [self._to_dxf_coords(ch, rl) 
                          for ch, rl in outline['deck_top']]
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=deck_top_points,
            close=False,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DECK_TOP'}
        )
        
        # Draw deck soffit
        deck_soffit_points = [self._to_dxf_coords(ch, rl) 
                             for ch, rl in outline['deck_soffit']]
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=deck_soffit_points,
            close=False,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DECK_SOFFIT'}
        )
        
        # Hatch deck area
        deck_polygon = deck_top_points + deck_soffit_points[::-1]
        hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=1, dxfattribs={'layer': 'DECK_TOP'})
        hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(deck_polygon, is_closed=True)
        hatch.set_pattern_fill('ANSI31', scale=0.5)
        
        # Draw road level line (above deck)
        supports = sorted(self.profile.supports, key=lambda s: s.chainage)
        road_points = []
        for s in supports:
            road_points.append(self._to_dxf_coords(s.chainage, s.deck_levels.road_level))
        
        # Interpolate road line
        road_line = []
        for ch, _ in outline['ground']:
            rl = self._interpolate_road_level(ch, supports)
            road_line.append(self._to_dxf_coords(ch, rl))
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=road_line,
            close=False,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DECK_TOP', 'lineweight': 0.50}
        )
    
    def _interpolate_road_level(self, chainage: float, 
                                 supports: List[SupportElevation]) -> float:
        """Interpolate road level at any chainage"""
        for i in range(len(supports) - 1):
            s1, s2 = supports[i], supports[i + 1]
            if s1.chainage <= chainage <= s2.chainage:
                t = (chainage - s1.chainage) / (s2.chainage - s1.chainage)
                return s1.deck_levels.road_level + t * (
                    s2.deck_levels.road_level - s1.deck_levels.road_level
                )
        return supports[0].deck_levels.road_level
    
    def draw_supports(self):
        """Draw piers and abutments with foundations"""
        outline = self.calc.get_longitudinal_section_outline()
        
        for support in outline['supports']:
            self._draw_single_support(support)
    
    def _draw_single_support(self, support: Dict):
        """Draw a single support (pier/abutment) with foundation"""
        ch = support['chainage']
        left = support['left']
        right = support['right']
        
        # Support body (from foundation to pier cap)
        support_points = [
            self._to_dxf_coords(left, support['bottom']),
            self._to_dxf_coords(right, support['bottom']),
            self._to_dxf_coords(right, support['top']),
            self._to_dxf_coords(left, support['top']),
            self._to_dxf_coords(left, support['bottom'])
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=support_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'PIER'}
        )
        
        # Hatch support
        hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=5, dxfattribs={'layer': 'PIER'})
        hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(support_points, is_closed=True)
        hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.3)
        
        # Draw foundation details
        if support['foundation_type'] == 'PILE':
            self._draw_pile_foundation(support)
        elif support['foundation_type'] == 'WELL':
            self._draw_well_foundation(support)
        
        # Add support label
        label_y = support['top'] + 3.0
        label_pos = self._to_dxf_coords(ch, label_y)
        
        label_text = f"{support['type']}-{support['number']}"
        if support['type'] == 'ABUTMENT':
            label_text = f"A-{support['number']}"
        else:
            label_text = f"P-{support['number']}"
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            label_text,
            height=2.5,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(label_pos, align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER)
        
        # Add chainage annotation
        ch_text = f"CH: {ch:.3f}"
        ch_pos = self._to_dxf_coords(ch, support['ground_level'] - 2.0)
        self.msp.add_text(
            ch_text,
            height=2.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(ch_pos, align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER)
    
    def _draw_pile_foundation(self, support: Dict):
        """Draw pile foundation details"""
        ch = support['chainage']
        pile_spacing = 1.5  # Typical
        
        # Draw 3 piles
        for offset in [-pile_spacing, 0, pile_spacing]:
            pile_x = ch + offset
            pile_top = support['bottom']  # Bottom of pile cap
            pile_bottom = pile_top - 15.0  # Typical pile length
            
            # Pile line
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf_coords(pile_x - 0.3, pile_top),
                end=self._to_dxf_coords(pile_x - 0.2, pile_bottom),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE'}
            )
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf_coords(pile_x + 0.3, pile_top),
                end=self._to_dxf_coords(pile_x + 0.2, pile_bottom),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE'}
            )
    
    def _draw_well_foundation(self, support: Dict):
        """Draw well foundation details"""
        ch = support['chainage']
        well_width = 6.0  # Typical
        
        # Well outline
        well_points = [
            self._to_dxf_coords(ch - well_width/2, support['bottom']),
            self._to_dxf_coords(ch + well_width/2, support['bottom']),
            self._to_dxf_coords(ch + well_width/2, support['bottom'] + 2.0),
            self._to_dxf_coords(ch - well_width/2, support['bottom'] + 2.0),
            self._to_dxf_coords(ch - well_width/2, support['bottom'])
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=well_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'FOUNDATION'}
        )
    
    def draw_dimensions(self):
        """Draw all vertical and horizontal dimensions"""
        dims = self.calc.get_dimension_data()
        
        for dim in dims:
            if dim['type'] == 'FOUNDATION_DEPTH':
                self._draw_vertical_dimension(
                    dim['chainage'], dim['top'], dim['bottom'],
                    dim['text'], offset=-3.0
                )
            
            elif dim['type'] == 'PIER_HEIGHT':
                self._draw_vertical_dimension(
                    dim['chainage'], dim['top'], dim['bottom'],
                    dim['text'], offset=3.0
                )
            
            elif dim['type'] == 'VERTICAL_CLEARANCE':
                self._draw_vertical_dimension(
                    dim['chainage'], dim['top'], dim['bottom'],
                    dim['text'], offset=5.0, style='CLEARANCE'
                )
            
            elif dim['type'] == 'SPAN_LENGTH':
                self._draw_span_dimension(dim)
    
    def _draw_vertical_dimension(self, chainage: float, top: float, 
                                  bottom: float, text: str,
                                  offset: float = 3.0, style: str = 'STANDARD'):
        """Draw a vertical dimension line"""
        # Dimension line position
        dim_x = chainage + offset
        top_point = self._to_dxf_coords(dim_x, top)
        bottom_point = self._to_dxf_coords(dim_x, bottom)
        
        # Draw dimension line
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=top_point,
            end=bottom_point,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        )
        
        # Extension lines
        ext_offset = 0.5
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf_coords(chainage, top),
            end=self._to_dxf_coords(dim_x - ext_offset, top),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        )
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf_coords(chainage, bottom),
            end=self._to_dxf_coords(dim_x - ext_offset, bottom),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        )
        
        # Dimension text
        mid_y = (top + bottom) / 2
        text_pos = self._to_dxf_coords(dim_x + 1.0, mid_y)
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            text,
            height=2.0,
            rotation=90,  # Vertical text
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(text_pos, align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER)
    
    def _draw_span_dimension(self, dim: Dict):
        """Draw horizontal span dimension"""
        start = self._to_dxf_coords(dim['start_support'], dim['level'])
        end = self._to_dxf_coords(dim['end_support'], dim['level'])
        
        # Dimension line
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=start,
            end=end,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        )
        
        # Extension lines up to soffit
        for ch in [dim['start_support'], dim['end_support']]:
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf_coords(ch, dim['level'] + 1.0),
                end=self._to_dxf_coords(ch, dim['level']),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
            )
        
        # Text
        mid_x = (dim['start_support'] + dim['end_support']) / 2
        text_pos = self._to_dxf_coords(mid_x, dim['level'] - 1.0)
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            dim['text'],
            height=2.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(text_pos, align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER)
    
    def draw_level_annotations(self):
        """Draw RL annotations at key points"""
        supports = sorted(self.profile.supports, key=lambda s: s.chainage)
        
        for support in supports:
            ch = support.chainage
            
            # Key levels to annotate
            levels = [
                (support.deck_levels.road_level, 'RL', 8.0),
                (support.deck_levels.soffit_level, 'Soffit', 5.0),
                (support.foundation.cutoff_level, 'Cutoff', -8.0)
            ]
            
            for level, label, x_offset in levels:
                # Leader line
                leader_start = self._to_dxf_coords(ch, level)
                leader_end = self._to_dxf_coords(ch + x_offset, level)
                
                self.msp.add_line(
                    start=leader_start,
                    end=leader_end,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
                )
                
                # Text
                text = f"{label} = {level:.3f}"
                text_pos = self._to_dxf_coords(ch + x_offset + 2.0, level)
                
                self.msp.add_text(
                    text,
                    height=2.0,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
                ).set_placement(text_pos, align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT)
    
    def draw_title_block(self):
        """Add title block and drawing information"""
        # Title block at bottom
        title_y = self._to_dxf_coords(self.start_ch, self.min_level)[1] - 15.0
        
        # Border
        border = [
            (0, title_y),
            ((self.end_ch - self.start_ch) * self.scale, title_y),
            ((self.end_ch - self.start_ch) * self.scale, title_y + 10.0),
            (0, title_y + 10.0),
            (0, title_y)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=border,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
        )
        
        # Title text
        title_text = f"LONGITUDINAL SECTION - {self.profile.bridge_name}"
        self.msp.add_text(
            title_text,
            height=3.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            ((self.end_ch - self.start_ch) * self.scale / 2, title_y + 7.0),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        # Details
        details = [
            f"Chainage: {self.profile.chainage:.3f}",
            f"Total Length: {sum(s.length for s in self.profile.supports)}m",
            f"Scale: H 1:{int(1/self.scale)}  V 1:{int(1/(self.scale * self.v_exag))}"
        ]
        
        for i, detail in enumerate(details):
            self.msp.add_text(
                detail,
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(
                (5.0, title_y + 4.0 - i * 2.5),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
            )
    
    def generate(self, output_path: str = "longitudinal_section.dxf"):
        """Generate complete longitudinal section drawing"""
        print(f"Generating longitudinal section for: {self.profile.bridge_name}")
        
        # Draw all components
        self.draw_ground_profile()
        self.draw_hydraulic_levels()
        self.draw_supports()
        self.draw_deck()
        self.draw_dimensions()
        self.draw_level_annotations()
        self.draw_title_block()
        
        # Save
        self.doc.saveas(output_path)
        print(f"Longitudinal section saved: {output_path}")
        return output_path
```

### File 3: `example_longitudinal.py` - Complete Example

```python
"""
Example: Generate longitudinal section for Chambakkara Bridge
"""

from longitudinal_section_core import (
    LongitudinalProfile, SupportElevation, DeckLevels,
    FoundationLevels, HydraulicLevels, LevelCalculator,
    SurveyPoint, GroundProfileGenerator
)
from longitudinal_section_drawing import LongitudinalSectionDrawing


def create_chambakkara_longitudinal_section():
    """Create complete longitudinal section for Chambakkara Bridge"""
    
    print("=" * 70)
    print("CHAMBAKKARA BRIDGE - LONGITUDINAL SECTION")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    # Generate ground profile with river channel
    survey_points = GroundProfileGenerator.generate_random_profile(
        start_ch=590.0,
        end_ch=740.0,
        base_rl=175.0,
        variation=3.0,
        num_points=25
    )
    
    # Modify with river channel
    profile_func = GroundProfileGenerator.from_sparse_points(
        [(p.chainage, p.rl) for p in survey_points]
    )
    
    modified_func = GroundProfileGenerator.add_river_channel(
        profile_func,
        center_chainage=665.0,  # Middle of bridge
        channel_width=80.0,
        channel_depth=8.0,
        bank_slope=0.3
    )
    
    # Update survey points with modified profile
    for p in survey_points:
        p.rl = modified_func(p.chainage)
    
    # Define hydraulic levels
    hydraulic = HydraulicLevels(
        hfl=182.500,
        lwl=178.000,
        normal_water_level=180.000,
        scour_level=172.200,
        design_scour_level=171.700,
        bed_level=175.500
    )
    
    print(f"\nHydraulic Data:")
    print(f"  HFL: {hydraulic.hfl:.3f}m")
    print(f"  LWL: {hydraulic.lwl:.3f}m")
    print(f"  Scour Level: {hydraulic.scour_level:.3f}m")
    print(f"  Scour Depth: {hydraulic.scour_depth:.2f}m")
    
    # Create support elevations
    supports = []
    
    # Left Abutment (A-1)
    gl_a1 = modified_func(600.0)
    deck_a1 = LevelCalculator.calculate_deck_levels(
        road_level=185.500,
        wearing_coat=0.065,
        deck_thickness=0.25,
        girder_type="T-BEAM",
        span_length=25.0
    )
    foundation_a1 = LevelCalculator.calculate_foundation_levels(
        foundation_type="PILE",
        ground_level=gl_a1,
        scour_level=hydraulic.scour_level,
        hfl=hydraulic.hfl
    )
    
    supports.append(SupportElevation(
        support_number=1,
        support_type="ABUTMENT",
        chainage=600.0,
        ground_level=gl_a1,
        deck_levels=deck_a1,
        foundation=foundation_a1
    ))
    
    # Pier 1
    gl_p1 = modified_func(625.0)
    deck_p1 = LevelCalculator.calculate_deck_levels(
        road_level=185.480,  # Slight gradient
        span_length=30.0
    )
    foundation_p1 = LevelCalculator.calculate_foundation_levels(
        foundation_type="PILE",
        ground_level=gl_p1,
        scour_level=hydraulic.scour_level,
        hfl=hydraulic.hfl
    )
    
    supports.append(SupportElevation(
        support_number=1,
        support_type="PIER",
        chainage=625.0,
        ground_level=gl_p1,
        deck_levels=deck_p1,
        foundation=foundation_p1
    ))
    
    # Pier 2
    gl_p2 = modified_func(655.0)
    deck_p2 = LevelCalculator.calculate_deck_levels(
        road_level=185.450,
        span_length=30.0
    )
    foundation_p2 = LevelCalculator.calculate_foundation_levels(
        foundation_type="PILE",
        ground_level=gl_p2,
        scour_level=hydraulic.scour_level,
        hfl=hydraulic.hfl
    )
    
    supports.append(SupportElevation(
        support_number=2,
        support_type="PIER",
        chainage=655.0,
        ground_level=gl_p2,
        deck_levels=deck_p2,
        foundation=foundation_p2
    ))
    
    # Pier 3
    gl_p3 = modified_func(685.0)
    deck_p3 = LevelCalculator.calculate_deck_levels(
        road_level=185.420,
        span_length=25.0
    )
    foundation_p3 = LevelCalculator.calculate_foundation_levels(
        foundation_type="PILE",
        ground_level=gl_p3,
        scour_level=hydraulic.scour_level,
        hfl=hydraulic.hfl
    )
    
    supports.append(SupportElevation(
        support_number=3,
        support_type="PIER",
        chainage=685.0,
        ground_level=gl_p3,
        deck_levels=deck_p3,
        foundation=foundation_p3
    ))
    
    # Right Abutment (A-2)
    gl_a2 = modified_func(710.0)
    deck_a2 = LevelCalculator.calculate_deck_levels(
        road_level=185.400,
        span_length=25.0
    )
    foundation_a2 = LevelCalculator.calculate_foundation_levels(
        foundation_type="PILE",
        ground_level=gl_a2,
        scour_level=hydraulic.scour_level,
        hfl=hydraulic.hfl
    )
    
    supports.append(SupportElevation(
        support_number=2,
        support_type="ABUTMENT",
        chainage=710.0,
        ground_level=gl_a2,
        deck_levels=deck_a2,
        foundation=foundation_a2
    ))
    
    # Create longitudinal profile
    profile = LongitudinalProfile(
        bridge_name="Chambakkara Bridge",
        chainage=600.0,
        total_length=110.0,
        survey_points=survey_points,
        hydraulic=hydraulic,
        supports=supports
    )
    
    print(f"\nSupport Summary:")
    for s in supports:
        print(f"  {s.support_type} #{s.support_number} at CH {s.chainage:.3f}:")
        print(f"    Ground: {s.ground_level:.3f}m")
        print(f"    Road: {s.deck_levels.road_level:.3f}m")
        print(f"    Sofit: {s.deck_levels.soffit_level:.3f}m")
        print(f"    Pier Height: {s.pier_height:.2f}m")
        print(f"    Foundation: {s.foundation.foundation_type}")
    
    # Generate drawing
    drawing = LongitudinalSectionDrawing(
        profile=profile,
        drawing_scale=0.5,  # 1:2 (1 unit = 2m horizontal)
        vertical_exaggeration=2.0  # 2x vertical exaggeration
    )
    
    output_file = drawing.generate("chambakkara_longitudinal_section.dxf")
    
    print(f"\n{'='*70}")
    print(f"OUTPUT: {output_file}")
    print(f"{'='*70}")
    
    return profile, drawing


if __name__ == "__main__":
    profile, drawing = create_chambakkara_longitudinal_section()
```

---

## KEY FEATURES

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **Ground Profile** | Cubic spline interpolation from survey points |
| **River Channel** | Automatic channel generation with sloping banks |
| **Hydraulic Levels** | HFL, LWL, Scour, Bed Level with annotations |
| **Deck Levels** | Road, wearing coat, deck top, soffit, bearing |
| **Foundation Types** | Pile, Well, Open foundation with different detailing |
| **Vertical Dimensions** | Foundation depth, pier height, clearances |
| **Span Dimensions** | Horizontal dimensions between supports |
| **Level Annotations** | RL values at all critical points |
| **Vertical Exaggeration** | Configurable for clarity |

To generate both plan and longitudinal sections:

```python
from skew_bridge_engine import create_skew_bridge
from plan_view_drawing import PlanViewDrawing
from longitudinal_section_drawing import LongitudinalSectionDrawing
from longitudinal_section_core import LongitudinalProfile

# Create bridge model
bridge = create_skew_bridge(
    name="Chambakkara Bridge",
    chainage=600.0,
    span_lengths=[25, 30, 25, 30, 25],
    deck_width=12.0,
    skew_angle=15.0
)

# Generate plan view
plan_drawing = PlanViewDrawing(bridge.to_params())
plan_drawing.generate("plan.dxf")

# Generate longitudinal section
long_profile = LongitudinalProfile.from_bridge_model(bridge)
long_drawing = LongitudinalSectionDrawing(long_profile)
long_drawing.generate("longitudinal.dxf")
```
