# CROSS-SECTION GENERATOR MODULE

## MATHEMATICAL FOUNDATION

```
TYPICAL CROSS-SECTION (Perpendicular to Bridge Axis)
====================================================

     ←────────── Overall Width ──────────→
     ←──── Carriageway ────→
     
     ╔═══════════════════════════════════╗  ← Parapet/Railing
     ║░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░║  ← Wearing Coat (65mm)
     ╠═══════════════════════════════════╣  ← Deck Top
     ║                                   ║
     ║    ╔═══════╗     ╔═══════╗     ║  ← Deck Slab
     ║    ║       ║     ║       ║     ║
     ║    ║  WEB  ║     ║  WEB  ║     ║
     ║    ║       ║     ║       ║     ║
     ║    ╚═══╦═══╝     ╚═══╦═══╝     ║  ← Bottom Flange
     ║        ║             ║         ║
     ║     [BEARING]     [BEARING]     ║  ← Bearings
     ║        ║             ║         ║
     ╚════════╩═════════════╩═════════╝  ← Pier Cap
    
     Cross-fall: 2.5%    Camber: 1 in 1000
     
     Reinforcement:
     Main bars ⊥ to axis (skew correction applied)
     Distribution bars ∥ to axis
```

---

## COMPLETE PYTHON MODULE

### File 1: `cross_section_core.py` - Core Calculations

```python
"""
Cross-Section Generator Core
Calculates geometry for bridge cross-sections including T-beam, I-girder, Box girder, and Solid slab
"""

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Optional, Union
from enum import Enum
import numpy as np


class GirderType(Enum):
    """Types of bridge superstructure"""
    T_BEAM = "T-BEAM"
    I_GIRDER = "I-GIRDER"
    BOX_GIRDER = "BOX-GIRDER"
    SOLID_SLAB = "SOLID-SLAB"
    VOIDED_SLAB = "VOIDED-SLAB"


class ParapetType(Enum):
    """Types of parapets"""
    RCC_CRASH_BARRIER = "RCC_CRASH_BARRIER"
    METAL_RAILING = "METAL_RAILING"
    COMBINATION = "COMBINATION"
    CONCRETE_RAILING = "CONCRETE_RAILING"


@dataclass
class CrossSectionDimensions:
    """Overall cross-section dimensions"""
    carriageway_width: float  # Clear width between kerbs (m)
    overall_width: float  # Total width including parapets (m)
    deck_thickness: float  # Slab thickness (m)
    wearing_coat_thickness: float  # Wearing coat (m)
    left_kerb_width: float = 0.6
    right_kerb_width: float = 0.6
    left_footpath_width: float = 0.0
    right_footpath_width: float = 0.0
    median_width: float = 0.0
    
    @property
    def half_width(self) -> float:
        return self.overall_width / 2
    
    @property
    def effective_width(self) -> float:
        """Width available for girders"""
        return self.carriageway_width + self.left_kerb_width + self.right_kerb_width


@dataclass
class TBeamDimensions:
    """T-beam girder dimensions"""
    web_width: float  # Width of stem/web (m)
    web_depth: float  # Depth of stem below slab (m)
    top_flange_width: float  # Width of top flange (deck slab) (m)
    top_flange_thickness: float  # Thickness of deck slab (m)
    bottom_flange_width: float  # Width of bottom flange (m)
    bottom_flange_thickness: float  # Thickness of bottom flange (m)
    haunch_width: float = 0.15  # Haunch at slab-web junction
    haunch_height: float = 0.10
    
    @property
    def total_depth(self) -> float:
        """Total depth from soffit to top of deck"""
        return self.web_depth + self.top_flange_thickness
    
    @property
    def self_weight(self) -> float:
        """Self weight per meter length (kN/m)"""
        # Concrete density 25 kN/m³
        web_area = self.web_width * self.web_depth
        top_flange_area = (self.top_flange_width - self.web_width) * self.top_flange_thickness
        bottom_flange_area = self.bottom_flange_width * self.bottom_flange_thickness
        haunch_area = self.haunch_width * self.haunch_height / 2
        
        total_area = web_area + top_flange_area + bottom_flange_area + 2 * haunch_area
        return total_area * 25


@dataclass
class IGirderDimensions:
    """Prestressed I-girder dimensions"""
    web_width: float
    total_depth: float
    top_flange_width: float
    top_flange_thickness: float
    bottom_flange_width: float
    bottom_flange_thickness: float
    web_taper: bool = True  # Tapered web or constant width
    
    @property
    def self_weight(self) -> float:
        """Self weight per meter"""
        # Approximate calculation
        avg_web_width = self.web_width * 1.2 if self.web_taper else self.web_width
        web_area = avg_web_width * (self.total_depth - 
                                    self.top_flange_thickness - 
                                    self.bottom_flange_thickness)
        top_area = self.top_flange_width * self.top_flange_thickness
        bottom_area = self.bottom_flange_width * self.bottom_flange_thickness
        
        return (web_area + top_area + bottom_area) * 25


@dataclass
class BoxGirderDimensions:
    """Box girder dimensions"""
    total_depth: float
    top_flange_width: float
    top_flange_thickness: float
    bottom_flange_width: float
    bottom_flange_thickness: float
    web_thickness: float
    number_of_cells: int = 1
    
    @property
    def cell_width(self) -> float:
        """Width of each cell"""
        return (self.top_flange_width - 2 * self.web_thickness) / self.number_of_cells


@dataclass
class ParapetDimensions:
    """Parapet and railing dimensions"""
    parapet_type: ParapetType
    height: float
    base_width: float
    top_width: float
    railing_height: float = 0.0
    railing_offset: float = 0.0
    
    def get_outline(self, position: str = "LEFT") -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
        """Get parapet outline coordinates"""
        x_offset = -self.base_width if position == "LEFT" else 0
        
        if self.parapet_type == ParapetType.RCC_CRASH_BARRIER:
            # New Jersey shape or similar
            return [
                (x_offset, 0),
                (x_offset + self.base_width * 0.3, self.height * 0.3),
                (x_offset + self.base_width * 0.2, self.height * 0.7),
                (x_offset + self.base_width * 0.5, self.height),
                (x_offset + self.base_width, self.height),
                (x_offset + self.base_width, 0),
                (x_offset, 0)
            ]
        else:
            # Simple rectangular
            return [
                (x_offset, 0),
                (x_offset, self.height),
                (x_offset + self.base_width, self.height),
                (x_offset + self.base_width, 0),
                (x_offset, 0)
            ]


@dataclass
class ReinforcementConfig:
    """Reinforcement configuration for cross-section"""
    # Deck slab reinforcement
    deck_main_bar_dia: int = 16  # mm
    deck_main_bar_spacing: float = 0.15  # m
    deck_dist_bar_dia: int = 12  # mm
    deck_dist_bar_spacing: float = 0.20  # m
    
    # Girder reinforcement
    girder_main_bar_dia: int = 25  # mm
    girder_main_bars_top: int = 4
    girder_main_bars_bottom: int = 8
    girder_stirrup_dia: int = 10  # mm
    girder_stirrup_spacing: float = 0.15  # m
    
    # Cover
    deck_cover: float = 0.030  # 30mm
    girder_cover: float = 0.040  # 40mm
    
    def get_deck_main_bar_area_per_meter(self) -> float:
        """Area of main reinforcement per meter width (mm²/m)"""
        bar_area = math.pi * (self.deck_main_bar_dia ** 2) / 4
        return bar_area * (1000 / self.deck_main_bar_spacing)
    
    def get_deck_dist_bar_area_per_meter(self) -> float:
        """Area of distribution reinforcement per meter (mm²/m)"""
        bar_area = math.pi * (self.deck_dist_bar_dia ** 2) / 4
        return bar_area * (1000 / self.deck_dist_bar_spacing)


@dataclass
class CrossFallConfig:
    """Cross-fall/camber configuration"""
    type: str = "PARABOLIC"  # PARABOLIC, LINEAR, FLAT
    cross_fall_percent: float = 2.5  # 2.5% typical
    camber_ratio: float = 1000  # 1 in 1000
    
    def get_level_at(self, x: float, half_width: float, 
                    center_level: float) -> float:
        """Get level at any point across width"""
        if self.type == "FLAT":
            return center_level
        
        # Distance from center
        dist_from_center = abs(x)
        
        if self.type == "LINEAR":
            # Linear cross-fall
            rise = (dist_from_center / half_width) * (half_width * self.cross_fall_percent / 100)
            return center_level + rise
        
        else:  # PARABOLIC
            # Parabolic camber
            ratio = dist_from_center / half_width
            camber_height = half_width / self.camber_ratio
            rise = camber_height * (1 - ratio ** 2)
            cross_fall = (dist_from_center / half_width) * (half_width * self.cross_fall_percent / 100)
            return center_level + rise + cross_fall


@dataclass
class CrossSectionModel:
    """Complete cross-section model"""
    girder_type: GirderType
    section_dimensions: CrossSectionDimensions
    girder_dimensions: Union[TBeamDimensions, IGirderDimensions, BoxGirderDimensions]
    parapet: ParapetDimensions
    reinforcement: ReinforcementConfig
    cross_fall: CrossFallConfig
    
    # Calculated properties
    number_of_girders: int = field(init=False)
    girder_spacing: float = field(init=False)
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        """Calculate girder layout"""
        if self.girder_type == GirderType.SOLID_SLAB:
            self.number_of_girders = 0
            self.girder_spacing = 0
        else:
            # Calculate number of girders based on spacing
            effective_width = self.section_dimensions.effective_width
            # Typical spacing 2.5-3.5m
            typical_spacing = 3.0
            
            self.number_of_girders = max(2, int(effective_width / typical_spacing) + 1)
            self.girder_spacing = effective_width / (self.number_of_girders - 1)
    
    def get_girder_positions(self) -> List[float]:
        """Get X-coordinates of all girders from left edge"""
        if self.number_of_girders == 0:
            return []
        
        # Start from left kerb face
        start_x = self.section_dimensions.left_kerb_width
        positions = []
        
        for i in range(self.number_of_girders):
            x = start_x + i * self.girder_spacing
            positions.append(x)
        
        return positions
    
    def get_section_outline(self) -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[float, float]]]:
        """Get complete section outline for drawing"""
        half_width = self.section_dimensions.half_width
        
        outlines = {
            'deck_top': [],
            'deck_soffit': [],
            'girders': [],
            'parapets': []
        }
        
        # Generate deck outline with cross-fall
        num_points = 50
        deck_top = []
        deck_soffit = []
        
        for i in range(num_points + 1):
            x = -half_width + (2 * half_width * i / num_points)
            
            # Top level with cross-fall
            top_level = self.cross_fall.get_level_at(
                x, half_width, 0
            )
            deck_top.append((x, top_level))
            
            # Soffit level (below top by deck thickness)
            soffit_level = top_level - self.section_dimensions.deck_thickness
            deck_soffit.append((x, soffit_level))
        
        outlines['deck_top'] = deck_top
        outlines['deck_soffit'] = deck_soffit
        
        # Girder outlines
        if self.girder_type != GirderType.SOLID_SLAB:
            girder_positions = self.get_girder_positions()
            
            for pos in girder_positions:
                if self.girder_type == GirderType.T_BEAM:
                    girder_outline = self._get_tbeam_outline(pos)
                elif self.girder_type == GirderType.I_GIRDER:
                    girder_outline = self._get_igirder_outline(pos)
                else:
                    girder_outline = self._get_box_outline(pos)
                
                outlines['girders'].append({
                    'center_x': pos,
                    'outline': girder_outline
                })
        
        # Parapet outlines
        left_parapet = self.parapet.get_outline("LEFT")
        # Offset to left edge
        left_edge = -half_width
        left_parapet = [(x + left_edge, y) for x, y in left_parapet]
        outlines['parapets'].append(left_parapet)
        
        right_parapet = self.parapet.get_outline("RIGHT")
        # Offset to right edge
        right_edge = half_width - self.parapet.base_width
        right_parapet = [(x + right_edge, y) for x, y in right_parapet]
        outlines['parapets'].append(right_parapet)
        
        return outlines
    
    def _get_tbeam_outline(self, center_x: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
        """Get T-beam outline coordinates"""
        d = self.girder_dimensions  # TBeamDimensions
        
        # Reference: soffit level at y=0
        outline = []
        
        # Bottom flange
        bf_left = center_x - d.bottom_flange_width / 2
        bf_right = center_x + d.bottom_flange_width / 2
        bf_top = d.bottom_flange_thickness
        bf_bottom = 0
        
        outline.extend([
            (bf_left, bf_bottom),
            (bf_right, bf_bottom),
            (bf_right, bf_top),
        ])
        
        # Web with haunch
        web_left = center_x - d.web_width / 2
        web_right = center_x + d.web_width / 2
        web_top = d.total_depth
        
        # Haunch transition
        haunch_start = bf_top + d.haunch_height
        
        outline.extend([
            (bf_right, bf_top),
            (web_right + d.haunch_width, haunch_start),
            (web_right, web_top),
            (web_left, web_top),
            (web_left - d.haunch_width, haunch_start),
            (bf_left, bf_top),
            (bf_left, bf_bottom)
        ])
        
        return outline
    
    def _get_igirder_outline(self, center_x: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
        """Get I-girder outline"""
        d = self.girder_dimensions
        
        outline = []
        
        # Bottom flange
        bf_left = center_x - d.bottom_flange_width / 2
        bf_right = center_x + d.bottom_flange_width / 2
        bf_top = d.bottom_flange_thickness
        
        outline.extend([
            (bf_left, 0),
            (bf_right, 0),
            (bf_right, bf_top),
        ])
        
        # Web (tapered or straight)
        if d.web_taper:
            web_bottom_width = d.web_width * 1.5
            web_top_width = d.web_width * 0.8
        else:
            web_bottom_width = d.web_width
            web_top_width = d.web_width
        
        web_bottom = bf_top
        web_top = d.total_depth - d.top_flange_thickness
        
        outline.extend([
            (center_x + web_bottom_width/2, web_bottom),
            (center_x + web_top_width/2, web_top),
        ])
        
        # Top flange
        tf_left = center_x - d.top_flange_width / 2
        tf_right = center_x + d.top_flange_width / 2
        tf_bottom = web_top
        tf_top = d.total_depth
        
        outline.extend([
            (tf_right, tf_bottom),
            (tf_right, tf_top),
            (tf_left, tf_top),
            (tf_left, tf_bottom),
        ])
        
        # Left side down
        outline.extend([
            (center_x - web_top_width/2, web_top),
            (center_x - web_bottom_width/2, web_bottom),
            (bf_left, bf_top),
            (bf_left, 0)
        ])
        
        return outline
    
    def _get_box_outline(self, center_x: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
        """Get box girder outline"""
        d = self.girder_dimensions
        
        # For box girder, return outer and inner outlines
        outer_left = center_x - d.top_flange_width / 2
        outer_right = center_x + d.top_flange_width / 2
        outer_bottom = 0
        outer_top = d.total_depth
        
        outer = [
            (outer_left, outer_bottom),
            (outer_right, outer_bottom),
            (outer_right, outer_top),
            (outer_left, outer_top),
            (outer_left, outer_bottom)
        ]
        
        # Inner void
        inner_left = outer_left + d.web_thickness
        inner_right = outer_right - d.web_thickness
        inner_bottom = outer_bottom + d.bottom_flange_thickness
        inner_top = outer_top - d.top_flange_thickness
        
        inner = [
            (inner_left, inner_bottom),
            (inner_right, inner_bottom),
            (inner_right, inner_top),
            (inner_left, inner_top),
            (inner_left, inner_bottom)
        ]
        
        return {'outer': outer, 'inner': inner}
    
    def get_reinforcement_layout(self) -> Dict:
        """Get reinforcement bar positions"""
        layout = {
            'deck_main_bars': [],
            'deck_dist_bars': [],
            'girder_bars': []
        }
        
        half_width = self.section_dimensions.half_width
        
        # Deck main bars (perpendicular to axis)
        # For skew bridges, these are at skew angle
        num_bars = int(self.section_dimensions.overall_width / 
                      self.reinforcement.deck_main_bar_spacing) + 1
        
        for i in range(num_bars):
            x = -half_width + i * self.reinforcement.deck_main_bar_spacing
            if abs(x) <= half_width:
                # Bar at top of deck
                top_level = self.cross_fall.get_level_at(x, half_width, 0)
                bar_level = top_level - self.reinforcement.deck_cover - \
                           self.reinforcement.deck_main_bar_dia / 2000
                
                layout['deck_main_bars'].append({
                    'x': x,
                    'y': bar_level,
                    'diameter': self.reinforcement.deck_main_bar_dia
                })
        
        # Deck distribution bars (parallel to axis)
        # Shown as short lines in cross-section
        dist_spacing = self.reinforcement.deck_dist_bar_spacing
        num_dist = int(self.section_dimensions.overall_width / dist_spacing) + 1
        
        for i in range(num_dist):
            x = -half_width + i * dist_spacing
            if abs(x) <= half_width:
                top_level = self.cross_fall.get_level_at(x, half_width, 0)
                bar_level = top_level - self.reinforcement.deck_cover - \
                           self.reinforcement.deck_main_bar_dia / 1000 - \
                           self.reinforcement.deck_dist_bar_dia / 2000
                
                layout['deck_dist_bars'].append({
                    'x': x,
                    'y': bar_level,
                    'diameter': self.reinforcement.deck_dist_bar_dia,
                    'length': 0.10  # Short line in cross-section
                })
        
        # Girder reinforcement
        if self.girder_type != GirderType.SOLID_SLAB:
            girder_positions = self.get_girder_positions()
            
            for pos in girder_positions:
                girder_bars = self._get_girder_reinforcement(pos)
                layout['girder_bars'].append(girder_bars)
        
        return layout
    
    def _get_girder_reinforcement(self, center_x: float) -> Dict:
        """Get reinforcement details for a single girder"""
        d = self.girder_dimensions
        r = self.reinforcement
        
        if isinstance(d, TBeamDimensions):
            # T-beam reinforcement
            
            # Bottom bars in bottom flange
            bottom_bars = []
            bf_width = d.bottom_flange_width
            spacing = bf_width / (r.girder_main_bars_bottom + 1)
            
            for i in range(r.girder_main_bars_bottom):
                x = center_x - bf_width/2 + (i + 1) * spacing
                y = r.girder_cover + r.girder_main_bar_dia / 2000
                bottom_bars.append({'x': x, 'y': y})
            
            # Top bars in deck
            top_bars = []
            web_top = d.total_depth
            deck_level = web_top + d.top_flange_thickness - r.deck_cover - \
                        r.girder_main_bar_dia / 2000
            
            spacing = d.web_width / (r.girder_main_bars_top + 1)
            for i in range(r.girder_main_bars_top):
                x = center_x - d.web_width/2 + (i + 1) * spacing
                top_bars.append({'x': x, 'y': deck_level})
            
            # Stirrups
            stirrups = []
            stirrup_width = d.web_width - 2 * r.girder_cover
            stirrup_height = d.total_depth - 2 * r.girder_cover
            
            return {
                'center_x': center_x,
                'bottom_bars': bottom_bars,
                'top_bars': top_bars,
                'stirrups': {
                    'width': stirrup_width,
                    'height': stirrup_height,
                    'cover': r.girder_cover,
                    'diameter': r.girder_stirrup_dia
                }
            }
        
        return {}
    
    def get_dimensions(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """Get dimension lines and annotations"""
        dims = []
        half_width = self.section_dimensions.half_width
        
        # Overall width
        dims.append({
            'type': 'OVERALL_WIDTH',
            'start': (-half_width, -2.0),
            'end': (half_width, -2.0),
            'text': f"O/A WIDTH = {self.section_dimensions.overall_width:.2f}m",
            'offset': -3.0
        })
        
        # Carriageway width
        cw_half = self.section_dimensions.carriageway_width / 2
        dims.append({
            'type': 'CARRIAGEWAY',
            'start': (-cw_half, -1.0),
            'end': (cw_half, -1.0),
            'text': f"C/W = {self.section_dimensions.carriageway_width:.2f}m",
            'offset': -1.5
        })
        
        # Girder spacing
        if self.number_of_girders > 1:
            girder_positions = self.get_girder_positions()
            for i in range(len(girder_positions) - 1):
                dims.append({
                    'type': 'GIRDER_SPACING',
                    'start': (girder_positions[i], -4.0),
                    'end': (girder_positions[i + 1], -4.0),
                    'text': f"GS = {self.girder_spacing:.2f}m",
                    'offset': -4.5
                })
        
        # Depth dimensions
        if self.girder_type == GirderType.T_BEAM:
            d = self.girder_dimensions
            
            # Total depth
            dims.append({
                'type': 'TOTAL_DEPTH',
                'start': (half_width + 1.0, 0),
                'end': (half_width + 1.0, d.total_depth),
                'text': f"D = {d.total_depth:.2f}m",
                'offset': half_width + 2.0
            })
            
            # Web width
            dims.append({
                'type': 'WEB_WIDTH',
                'start': (half_width + 0.5, 0.5),
                'end': (half_width + 0.5, d.web_depth),
                'text': f"Web = {d.web_width*1000:.0f}mm",
                'offset': half_width + 1.5
            })
        
        return dims
    
    def get_material_quantities(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate material quantities per meter length"""
        quantities = {
            'concrete': 0,  # m³/m
            'steel': 0,     # kg/m
            'formwork': 0   # m²/m
        }
        
        # Deck concrete
        deck_area = self.section_dimensions.overall_width * \
                   self.section_dimensions.deck_thickness
        quantities['concrete'] += deck_area
        
        # Wearing coat
        quantities['concrete'] += self.section_dimensions.overall_width * \
                                self.section_dimensions.wearing_coat_thickness
        
        # Girder concrete
        if hasattr(self.girder_dimensions, 'self_weight'):
            quantities['concrete'] += self.girder_dimensions.self_weight / 25
        
        # Parapet
        parapet_area = self.parapet.height * self.parapet.base_width * 2
        quantities['concrete'] += parapet_area * 0.3  # Approximate
        
        # Reinforcement steel (approximate 2% of concrete)
        quantities['steel'] = quantities['concrete'] * 0.02 * 7850  # kg/m³
        
        # Formwork
        quantities['formwork'] = self.section_dimensions.overall_width + \
                                2 * self.section_dimensions.deck_thickness
        
        return quantities
```

---

### File 2: `cross_section_drawing.py` - DXF Generator

```python
"""
Cross-Section Drawing Generator
Creates professional DXF drawings of bridge cross-sections
"""

import ezdxf
from ezdxf.enums import TextEntityAlignment
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
from cross_section_core import (
    CrossSectionModel, GirderType, ParapetType,
    TBeamDimensions, IGirderDimensions, BoxGirderDimensions
)


class CrossSectionDrawing:
    """
    Generate cross-section drawing in DXF format
    """
    
    LAYERS = {
        'DECK': {'color': 1, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.35},
        'GIRDER': {'color': 4, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.30},
        'PARAPET': {'color': 3, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.25},
        'WEARING_COAT': {'color': 30, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.18},
        'REINFORCEMENT': {'color': 2, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.25},
        'STIRRUPS': {'color': 5, 'linetype': 'DASHED', 'lineweight': 0.18},
        'DIMENSION': {'color': 7, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.18},
        'TEXT': {'color': 7, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.18},
        'HATCH': {'color': 253, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.13},
        'CENTERLINE': {'color': 6, 'linetype': 'CENTER', 'lineweight': 0.18}
    }
    
    def __init__(self, model: CrossSectionModel, scale: float = 10.0):
        self.model = model
        self.scale = scale
        
        self.doc = ezdxf.new('R2018', setup=True)
        self.msp = self.doc.modelspace()
        self.setup_layers()
        self.setup_styles()
        
        # Calculate extents
        self._calculate_extents()
    
    def setup_layers(self):
        """Create all required layers"""
        for name, attrs in self.LAYERS.items():
            self.doc.layers.add(
                name=name,
                color=attrs['color'],
                linetype=attrs['linetype'],
                lineweight=attrs['lineweight']
            )
    
    def setup_styles(self):
        """Setup text and dimension styles"""
        self.doc.styles.add('SECTION_FONT', font='Arial.ttf')
        
        dimstyle = self.doc.dimstyles.add('SECTION_DIM')
        dimstyle.dxf.dimtxt = 2.5
        dimstyle.dxf.dimexe = 1.0
        dimstyle.dxf.dimexo = 0.5
        dimstyle.dxf.dimtad = 1
        dimstyle.dxf.dimdec = 2
    
    def _calculate_extents(self):
        """Calculate drawing extents"""
        d = self.model.section_dimensions
        g = self.model.girder_dimensions
        
        self.min_x = -d.half_width - 5
        self.max_x = d.half_width + 5
        
        if hasattr(g, 'total_depth'):
            self.max_y = g.total_depth + 2
        else:
            self.max_y = d.deck_thickness + 2
        
        self.min_y = -5
    
    def _to_dxf(self, x: float, y: float) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """Convert model coordinates to DXF coordinates"""
        return (x * self.scale, y * self.scale)
    
    def draw_centerline(self):
        """Draw vertical centerline"""
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(0, self.min_y),
            end=self._to_dxf(0, self.max_y),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        
        # Centerline label
        self.msp.add_text(
            "CENTERLINE",
            height=2.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(0.5, self.max_y - 1),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
        )
    
    def draw_deck(self):
        """Draw deck slab with wearing coat"""
        outline = self.model.get_section_outline()
        
        # Draw deck soffit
        soffit_points = [self._to_dxf(x, y) for x, y in outline['deck_soffit']]
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=soffit_points,
            close=False,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DECK'}
        )
        
        # Draw deck top
        top_points = [self._to_dxf(x, y) for x, y in outline['deck_top']]
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=top_points,
            close=False,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DECK', 'lineweight': 0.50}
        )
        
        # Close deck polygon
        deck_polygon = top_points + soffit_points[::-1]
        if deck_polygon:
            deck_polygon.append(deck_polygon[0])  # Close
        
        # Hatch deck
        hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=1, dxfattribs={'layer': 'DECK'})
        hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(deck_polygon, is_closed=True)
        hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.2)
        
        # Draw wearing coat (thin line above deck)
        wearing_points = []
        wc_thickness = self.model.section_dimensions.wearing_coat_thickness
        
        for x, y in outline['deck_top']:
            wearing_points.append(self._to_dxf(x, y + wc_thickness))
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=wearing_points,
            close=False,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WEARING_COAT'}
        )
        
        # Hatch wearing coat
        wc_polygon = wearing_points + top_points[::-1]
        if wc_polygon:
            wc_polygon.append(wc_polygon[0])
        
        wc_hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=30, dxfattribs={'layer': 'WEARING_COAT'})
        wc_hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(wc_polygon, is_closed=True)
        wc_hatch.set_pattern_fill('ANSI31', scale=0.1)
    
    def draw_girders(self):
        """Draw girder outlines"""
        if self.model.girder_type == GirderType.SOLID_SLAB:
            return
        
        outline = self.model.get_section_outline()
        
        for girder in outline['girders']:
            center_x = girder['center_x']
            girder_outline = girder['outline']
            
            if isinstance(girder_outline, dict):
                # Box girder - outer and inner
                outer = [self._to_dxf(x, y) for x, y in girder_outline['outer']]
                inner = [self._to_dxf(x, y) for x, y in girder_outline['inner']]
                
                # Outer outline
                self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
                    points=outer,
                    close=True,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'GIRDER'}
                )
                
                # Inner void
                self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
                    points=inner,
                    close=True,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'GIRDER'}
                )
                
                # Hatch concrete
                hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=4, dxfattribs={'layer': 'GIRDER'})
                hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(outer, is_closed=True)
                hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(inner, is_closed=True)
                hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.3)
            
            else:
                # T-beam or I-girder
                points = [self._to_dxf(x, y) for x, y in girder_outline]
                
                self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
                    points=points,
                    close=True,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'GIRDER'}
                )
                
                # Hatch
                hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=4, dxfattribs={'layer': 'GIRDER'})
                hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(points, is_closed=True)
                hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.3)
            
            # Girder label
            label_y = -1.0
            self.msp.add_text(
                f"G-{girder['center_x']:.1f}m",
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(
                self._to_dxf(center_x, label_y),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
    
    def draw_parapets(self):
        """Draw parapets and railings"""
        outline = self.model.get_section_outline()
        
        for i, parapet_outline in enumerate(outline['parapets']):
            points = [self._to_dxf(x, y) for x, y in parapet_outline]
            
            self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
                points=points,
                close=True,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'PARAPET'}
            )
            
            # Hatch
            hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=3, dxfattribs={'layer': 'PARAPET'})
            hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(points, is_closed=True)
            hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.3)
            
            # Label
            label_x = parapet_outline[0][0] + self.model.parapet.base_width / 2
            label_y = self.model.parapet.height + 0.5
            
            side = "LHS" if i == 0 else "RHS"
            self.msp.add_text(
                f"{side} PARAPET",
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(
                self._to_dxf(label_x, label_y),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
    
    def draw_reinforcement(self):
        """Draw reinforcement bars"""
        rebar = self.model.get_reinforcement_layout()
        
        # Deck main bars (shown as circles in cross-section)
        for bar in rebar['deck_main_bars']:
            x, y = self._to_dxf(bar['x'], bar['y'])
            radius = bar['diameter'] / 2000 * self.scale
            
            self.msp.add_circle(
                center=(x, y),
                radius=radius,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT'}
            )
        
        # Deck distribution bars (short lines)
        for bar in rebar['deck_dist_bars']:
            x, y = self._to_dxf(bar['x'], bar['y'])
            half_len = bar['length'] / 2 * self.scale
            
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=(x - half_len, y),
                end=(x + half_len, y),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT'}
            )
        
        # Girder reinforcement
        for girder in rebar['girder_bars']:
            # Bottom bars
            for bar in girder['bottom_bars']:
                x, y = self._to_dxf(bar['x'], bar['y'])
                radius = self.model.reinforcement.girder_main_bar_dia / 2000 * self.scale
                self.msp.add_circle(
                    center=(x, y),
                    radius=radius,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT'}
                )
            
            # Top bars
            for bar in girder['top_bars']:
                x, y = self._to_dxf(bar['x'], bar['y'])
                radius = self.model.reinforcement.girder_main_bar_dia / 2000 * self.scale
                self.msp.add_circle(
                    center=(x, y),
                    radius=radius,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT'}
                )
            
            # Stirrups
            s = girder['stirrups']
            stirrup_left = girder['center_x'] - s['width'] / 2
            stirrup_right = girder['center_x'] + s['width'] / 2
            stirrup_bottom = s['cover']
            stirrup_top = stirrup_bottom + s['height']
            
            stirrup_points = [
                self._to_dxf(stirrup_left, stirrup_bottom),
                self._to_dxf(stirrup_right, stirrup_bottom),
                self._to_dxf(stirrup_right, stirrup_top),
                self._to_dxf(stirrup_left, stirrup_top),
                self._to_dxf(stirrup_left, stirrup_bottom)
            ]
            
            self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
                points=stirrup_points,
                close=False,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'STIRRUPS'}
            )
        
        # Legend
        legend_x = self._to_dxf(self.model.section_dimensions.half_width + 3, 0)[0]
        legend_y = self._to_dxf(0, self.model.girder_dimensions.total_depth / 2)[1]
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"Main Bars: {self.model.reinforcement.deck_main_bar_dia}mm @ {self.model.reinforcement.deck_main_bar_spacing*1000:.0f}mm c/c",
            height=2.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            (legend_x, legend_y),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
        )
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"Dist. Bars: {self.model.reinforcement.deck_dist_bar_dia}mm @ {self.model.reinforcement.deck_dist_bar_spacing*1000:.0f}mm c/c",
            height=2.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            (legend_x, legend_y - 3),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
        )
    
    def draw_dimensions(self):
        """Draw all dimensions"""
        dims = self.model.get_dimensions()
        
        for dim in dims:
            start = self._to_dxf(dim['start'][0], dim['start'][1])
            end = self._to_dxf(dim['end'][0], dim['end'][1])
            
            # Dimension line
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=start,
                end=end,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
            )
            
            # Extension lines
            ext_length = 0.5 * self.scale
            if dim['type'] in ['OVERALL_WIDTH', 'CARRIAGEWAY']:
                # Vertical extension lines
                self.msp.add_line(
                    start=start,
                    end=(start[0], start[1] + ext_length),
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
                )
                self.msp.add_line(
                    start=end,
                    end=(end[0], end[1] + ext_length),
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
                )
            
            # Text
            mid_x = (start[0] + end[0]) / 2
            mid_y = (start[1] + end[1]) / 2
            text_offset = dim.get('offset', -1.5) * self.scale
            
            self.msp.add_text(
                dim['text'],
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(
                (mid_x, mid_y + text_offset),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
    
    def draw_levels(self):
        """Draw level annotations"""
        half_width = self.model.section_dimensions.half_width
        
        # Road level at center
        road_level = self.model.cross_fall.get_level_at(0, half_width, 0)
        self._draw_level_annotation(0, road_level, f"RL {road_level:.3f}", "TOP")
        
        # Soffit level at center
        soffit = road_level - self.model.section_dimensions.deck_thickness
        if hasattr(self.model.girder_dimensions, 'total_depth'):
            soffit -= self.model.girder_dimensions.web_depth
        
        self._draw_level_annotation(0, soffit, f"SOFFIT {soffit:.3f}", "BOTTOM")
    
    def _draw_level_annotation(self, x: float, level: float, 
                               text: str, position: str):
        """Draw a level annotation with leader"""
        point = self._to_dxf(x, level)
        
        # Leader line
        offset_x = 2.0 * self.scale
        offset_y = 1.0 * self.scale if position == "TOP" else -1.0 * self.scale
        
        leader_end = (point[0] + offset_x, point[1] + offset_y)
        
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=point,
            end=leader_end,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
        )
        
        # Text
        text_pos = (leader_end[0] + 1.0, leader_end[1])
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            text,
            height=2.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            text_pos,
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
        )
    
    def draw_title_block(self):
        """Add title block"""
        title_y = self._to_dxf(0, self.min_y)[1] - 10
        
        # Border
        width = (self.max_x - self.min_x) * self.scale
        border = [
            (0, title_y),
            (width, title_y),
            (width, title_y + 8),
            (0, title_y + 8),
            (0, title_y)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=border,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
        )
        
        # Title
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"TYPICAL CROSS-SECTION - {self.model.girder_type.value}",
            height=3.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            (width / 2, title_y + 6),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        # Details
        details = [
            f"Carriageway: {self.model.section_dimensions.carriageway_width:.2f}m",
            f"Girders: {self.model.number_of_girders} @ {self.model.girder_spacing:.2f}m",
            f"Cross-fall: {self.model.cross_fall.cross_fall_percent}%"
        ]
        
        for i, detail in enumerate(details):
            self.msp.add_text(
                detail,
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'SECTION_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(
                (5, title_y + 4 - i * 2),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
            )
    
    def generate(self, output_path: str = "cross_section.dxf"):
        """Generate complete cross-section drawing"""
        print(f"Generating cross-section: {self.model.girder_type.value}")
        
        self.draw_centerline()
        self.draw_girders()
        self.draw_deck()
        self.draw_parapets()
        self.draw_reinforcement()
        self.draw_dimensions()
        self.draw_levels()
        self.draw_title_block()
        
        self.doc.saveas(output_path)
        print(f"Cross-section saved: {output_path}")
        return output_path
```

### File 3: `example_cross_section.py` - Complete Example

```python
"""
Example: Generate cross-sections for different bridge types
"""

from cross_section_core import (
    CrossSectionModel, CrossSectionDimensions, TBeamDimensions,
    IGirderDimensions, BoxGirderDimensions, ParapetDimensions,
    ReinforcementConfig, CrossFallConfig,
    GirderType, ParapetType
)
from cross_section_drawing import CrossSectionDrawing


def create_tbeam_cross_section():
    """Create T-beam bridge cross-section"""
    print("=" * 70)
    print("T-BEAM BRIDGE CROSS-SECTION")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    # Section dimensions
    section = CrossSectionDimensions(
        carriageway_width=12.0,
        overall_width=13.5,
        deck_thickness=0.25,
        wearing_coat_thickness=0.065,
        left_kerb_width=0.75,
        right_kerb_width=0.75
    )
    
    # T-beam dimensions
    girder = TBeamDimensions(
        web_width=0.30,
        web_depth=1.75,
        top_flange_width=2.5,
        top_flange_thickness=0.25,
        bottom_flange_width=0.60,
        bottom_flange_thickness=0.20,
        haunch_width=0.15,
        haunch_height=0.10
    )
    
    # Parapet
    parapet = ParapetDimensions(
        parapet_type=ParapetType.RCC_CRASH_BARRIER,
        height=0.90,
        base_width=0.45,
        top_width=0.20
    )
    
    # Reinforcement
    rebar = ReinforcementConfig(
        deck_main_bar_dia=16,
        deck_main_bar_spacing=0.15,
        deck_dist_bar_dia=12,
        deck_dist_bar_spacing=0.20,
        girder_main_bar_dia=25,
        girder_main_bars_top=4,
        girder_main_bars_bottom=8,
        girder_stirrup_dia=10,
        girder_stirrup_spacing=0.15
    )
    
    # Cross-fall
    crossfall = CrossFallConfig(
        type="PARABOLIC",
        cross_fall_percent=2.5,
        camber_ratio=1000
    )
    
    # Create model
    model = CrossSectionModel(
        girder_type=GirderType.T_BEAM,
        section_dimensions=section,
        girder_dimensions=girder,
        parapet=parapet,
        reinforcement=rebar,
        cross_fall=crossfall
    )
    
    print(f"\nModel Summary:")
    print(f"  Girder Type: {model.girder_type.value}")
    print(f"  Number of Girders: {model.number_of_girders}")
    print(f"  Girder Spacing: {model.girder_spacing:.2f}m")
    print(f"  Girder Depth: {girder.total_depth:.2f}m")
    print(f"  Self Weight: {girder.self_weight:.2f} kN/m")
    
    # Material quantities
    quantities = model.get_material_quantities()
    print(f"\nMaterial Quantities (per meter):")
    print(f"  Concrete: {quantities['concrete']:.3f} m³/m")
    print(f"  Steel: {quantities['steel']:.1f} kg/m")
    print(f"  Formwork: {quantities['formwork']:.2f} m²/m")
    
    # Generate drawing
    drawing = CrossSectionDrawing(model, scale=15.0)
    drawing.generate("tbeam_cross_section.dxf")
    
    return model


def create_box_girder_cross_section():
    """Create box girder bridge cross-section"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("BOX GIRDER BRIDGE CROSS-SECTION")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    section = CrossSectionDimensions(
        carriageway_width=12.0,
        overall_width=13.2,
        deck_thickness=0.25,
        wearing_coat_thickness=0.065
    )
    
    girder = BoxGirderDimensions(
        total_depth=2.5,
        top_flange_width=13.2,
        top_flange_thickness=0.25,
        bottom_flange_width=12.0,
        bottom_flange_thickness=0.25,
        web_thickness=0.30,
        number_of_cells=3
    )
    
    parapet = ParapetDimensions(
        parapet_type=ParapetType.RCC_CRASH_BARRIER,
        height=0.90,
        base_width=0.45,
        top_width=0.20
    )
    
    rebar = ReinforcementConfig(
        deck_main_bar_dia=20,
        deck_main_bar_spacing=0.125
    )
    
    crossfall = CrossFallConfig(
        type="LINEAR",
        cross_fall_percent=2.5
    )
    
    model = CrossSectionModel(
        girder_type=GirderType.BOX_GIRDER,
        section_dimensions=section,
        girder_dimensions=girder,
        parapet=parapet,
        reinforcement=rebar,
        cross_fall=crossfall
    )
    
    print(f"\nBox Girder Summary:")
    print(f"  Number of Cells: {girder.number_of_cells}")
    print(f"  Cell Width: {girder.cell_width:.2f}m")
    print(f"  Total Depth: {girder.total_depth:.2f}m")
    
    drawing = CrossSectionDrawing(model, scale=12.0)
    drawing.generate("box_girder_cross_section.dxf")
    
    return model


def create_solid_slab_cross_section():
    """Create solid slab bridge cross-section"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("SOLID SLAB BRIDGE CROSS-SECTION")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    section = CrossSectionDimensions(
        carriageway_width=7.5,
        overall_width=8.5,
        deck_thickness=0.50,  # Thicker for solid slab
        wearing_coat_thickness=0.065
    )
    
    # For solid slab, use minimal girder dimensions
    girder = TBeamDimensions(
        web_width=0.0,
        web_depth=0.0,
        top_flange_width=0.0,
        top_flange_thickness=0.0,
        bottom_flange_width=0.0,
        bottom_flange_thickness=0.0
    )
    
    parapet = ParapetDimensions(
        parapet_type=ParapetType.METAL_RAILING,
        height=1.0,
        base_width=0.30,
        top_width=0.15
    )
    
    rebar = ReinforcementConfig(
        deck_main_bar_dia=16,
        deck_main_bar_spacing=0.125,
        deck_dist_bar_dia=12,
        deck_dist_bar_spacing=0.15
    )
    
    crossfall = CrossFallConfig(
        type="LINEAR",
        cross_fall_percent=2.5
    )
    
    model = CrossSectionModel(
        girder_type=GirderType.SOLID_SLAB,
        section_dimensions=section,
        girder_dimensions=girder,
        parapet=parapet,
        reinforcement=rebar,
        cross_fall=crossfall
    )
    
    print(f"\nSolid Slab Summary:")
    print(f"  Slab Thickness: {section.deck_thickness:.2f}m")
    print(f"  No girders - solid slab construction")
    
    drawing = CrossSectionDrawing(model, scale=20.0)
    drawing.generate("solid_slab_cross_section.dxf")
    
    return model


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Generate all cross-section types
    tbeam = create_tbeam_cross_section()
    box = create_box_girder_cross_section()
    slab = create_solid_slab_cross_section()
    
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("ALL CROSS-SECTIONS GENERATED")
    print("=" * 70)
```

---

## KEY FEATURES

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **Girder Types** | T-beam, I-girder, Box girder, Solid slab, Voided slab |
| **Parapet Types** | RCC crash barrier, Metal railing, Combination |
| **Cross-fall** | Parabolic camber, Linear slope, or Flat |
| **Reinforcement** | Main bars, distribution bars, stirrups with proper cover |
| **Dimensions** | Automatic dimensioning of all key elements |
| **Material Qty** | Concrete, steel, formwork calculations |
| **Skew Handling** | Bars perpendicular to axis, skew correction applied |

# FOUNDATION DETAIL GENERATOR MODULE

## MATHEMATICAL FOUNDATION

```
PILE FOUNDATION LAYOUT
======================

Plan View (Pile Cap):
┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
│  ○      ○      ○      ○      ○   │  ← Piles (typ. 1.2m dia)
│                                     │
│  ○      ○      ○      ○      ○   │
│           [PIER SHAFT]              │
│  ○      ○      ○      ○      ○   │
│                                     │
│  ○      ○      ○      ○      ○   │
└─────────────────────────────────────┘
     Pile Cap (typ. 2.0m thick)
     
Section A-A:
     ←─ Pile Cap ─→
    ┌──────────────┐  ← Cutoff Level
    │              │
    │   PIER       │  ← 1.5m x 8.0m
    │   SHAFT      │
    │              │
    ├──────────────┤  ← Pile Cap Bottom
    │              │
    │  ╱    ╲      │  ← Piles (15m deep)
    │ │      │     │
    │ │      │     │
    │ │      │     │
    │ ╲      ╱     │
    └──────────────┘
    
    Pile Details:
    - Diameter: 1.2m
    - Length: 15.0m
    - Reinforcement: 12 bars of 20mm dia
    - Spiral: 10mm @ 150mm c/c


WELL FOUNDATION
===============

Plan View:
        ┌─────────────┐
       ╱               ╲
      │    STEINING     │
     │      ╔═════╗      │
     │      ║CELL ║      │  ← Well (6.0m dia)
     │      ╚═════╝      │
      │                   │
       ╲                 ╱
        └─────────────┘
        
Section:
    ┌─────────────────┐  ← Well Cap (2.0m thick)
    │                 │
    │   PIER/ABUT     │
    │                 │
    ├─────────────────┤  ← Cutoff Level
    │    STEINING     │
    │   ┌─────────┐   │
    │   │         │   │  ← 0.6m thick steining
    │   │  SINKING │   │
    │   │   DEPTH  │   │
    │   │  (12.0m) │   │
    │   │         │   │
    │   │  CURB   │   │  ← 2.0m high curb
    │   └─────────┘   │
    └─────────────────┘  ← Well Bottom
```
