## COMPLETE PYTHON MODULE

### File 1: `foundation_core.py` - Core Calculations

```python
"""
Foundation Detail Generator Core
Calculates geometry for pile, well, and open foundations
"""

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Optional, Union
from enum import Enum
import numpy as np


class FoundationType(Enum):
    """Types of bridge foundations"""
    PILE = "PILE"
    WELL = "WELL"
    OPEN = "OPEN"
    RAFT = "RAFT"


class PileType(Enum):
    """Types of pile foundations"""
    BORED_CAST_IN_SITU = "BORED_CAST_IN_SITU"
    DRIVEN = "DRIVEN"
    PRECAST = "PRECAST"
    UNDER_REAMED = "UNDER_REAMED"


class PileCapShape(Enum):
    """Shapes of pile caps"""
    RECTANGULAR = "RECTANGULAR"
    OCTAGONAL = "OCTAGONAL"
    CIRCULAR = "CIRCULAR"


class WellShape(Enum):
    """Shapes of well foundations"""
    CIRCULAR_SINGLE = "CIRCULAR_SINGLE"
    CIRCULAR_DOUBLE = "CIRCULAR_DOUBLE"
    DUMB_BELL = "DUMB_BELL"
    RECTANGULAR = "RECTANGULAR"


@dataclass
class PileDimensions:
    """Pile dimensions and properties"""
    diameter: float  # m
    length: float  # m
    pile_type: PileType = PileType.BORED_CAST_IN_SITU
    cutoff_level: float = 0.0
    toe_level: float = None
    
    # Reinforcement
    main_bar_diameter: int = 20  # mm
    number_of_main_bars: int = 12
    spiral_bar_diameter: int = 10  # mm
    spiral_spacing: float = 0.15  # m
    spiral_pitch: float = 0.15  # m
    
    @property
    def radius(self) -> float:
        return self.diameter / 2
    
    @property
    def cross_sectional_area(self) -> float:
        return math.pi * self.radius ** 2
    
    @property
    def surface_area(self) -> float:
        return 2 * math.pi * self.radius * self.length
    
    @property
    def concrete_volume(self) -> float:
        return self.cross_sectional_area * self.length
    
    @property
    def main_steel_weight(self) -> float:
        """Weight of main reinforcement in kg"""
        bar_area = math.pi * (self.main_bar_diameter ** 2) / 4  # mm²
        bar_length = self.length + 0.5  # Add development length
        total_length = bar_length * self.number_of_main_bars  # m
        return total_length * (bar_area / 1000000) * 7850  # kg
    
    @property
    def spiral_steel_weight(self) -> float:
        """Weight of spiral reinforcement in kg"""
        # Average radius of spiral
        spiral_radius = self.radius - 0.075  # 75mm cover
        spiral_length_per_turn = 2 * math.pi * spiral_radius
        number_of_turns = self.length / self.spiral_pitch
        total_spiral_length = spiral_length_per_turn * number_of_turns
        
        spiral_area = math.pi * (self.spiral_bar_diameter ** 2) / 4
        return total_spiral_length * (spiral_area / 1000000) * 7850


@dataclass
class PileGroupConfiguration:
    """Configuration of pile group"""
    num_piles_longitudinal: int  # Along bridge direction
    num_piles_transverse: int    # Across bridge direction
    spacing_longitudinal: float  # m
    spacing_transverse: float    # m
    pile_cap_shape: PileCapShape = PileCapShape.RECTANGULAR
    pile_cap_thickness: float = 2.0  # m
    pile_cap_projection: float = 0.3  # m beyond outer piles
    
    @property
    def total_piles(self) -> int:
        return self.num_piles_longitudinal * self.num_piles_transverse
    
    @property
    def pile_cap_length(self) -> float:
        """Length along bridge direction"""
        return ((self.num_piles_longitudinal - 1) * self.spacing_longitudinal + 
                2 * self.pile_cap_projection)
    
    @property
    def pile_cap_width(self) -> float:
        """Width across bridge direction"""
        return ((self.num_piles_transverse - 1) * self.spacing_transverse + 
                2 * self.pile_cap_projection)
    
    def get_pile_positions(self) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
        """Get (x, y) positions of all piles relative to cap center"""
        positions = []
        
        # Start from bottom-left
        start_x = -((self.num_piles_longitudinal - 1) * self.spacing_longitudinal) / 2
        start_y = -((self.num_piles_transverse - 1) * self.spacing_transverse) / 2
        
        for i in range(self.num_piles_longitudinal):
            for j in range(self.num_piles_transverse):
                x = start_x + i * self.spacing_longitudinal
                y = start_y + j * self.spacing_transverse
                positions.append((x, y))
        
        return positions


@dataclass
class PileCapReinforcement:
    """Pile cap reinforcement details"""
    # Bottom mesh
    bottom_mesh_dia: int = 25  # mm
    bottom_mesh_spacing: float = 0.15  # m
    
    # Top mesh
    top_mesh_dia: int = 20  # mm
    top_mesh_spacing: float = 0.15  # m
    
    # Vertical stirrups around piles
    stirrup_dia: int = 16  # mm
    stirrup_spacing: float = 0.30  # m
    
    # Side face reinforcement
    side_face_dia: int = 16  # mm
    side_face_spacing: float = 0.20  # m


@dataclass
class WellDimensions:
    """Well foundation dimensions"""
    well_shape: WellShape = WellShape.CIRCULAR_SINGLE
    external_diameter: float = 6.0  # m (for circular)
    length: float = 6.0  # m (for rectangular/dumb-bell)
    width: float = 6.0  # m (for rectangular)
    steining_thickness: float = 0.6  # m
    
    # Well components
    curb_height: float = 2.0  # m
    sinking_depth: float = 12.0  # m
    bottom_plug_thickness: float = 2.0  # m
    top_plug_thickness: float = 0.0  # m (if any)
    well_cap_thickness: float = 2.0  # m
    
    # Levels
    cutoff_level: float = 0.0
    bed_level: float = -5.0
    
    @property
    def internal_diameter(self) -> float:
        """Internal diameter for circular well"""
        return self.external_diameter - 2 * self.steining_thickness
    
    @property
    def curb_diameter(self) -> float:
        """Diameter at curb level (typically larger)"""
        return self.external_diameter + 0.4  # 200mm projection each side
    
    @property
    def well_bottom_level(self) -> float:
        """Level at bottom of well"""
        return self.cutoff_level - self.curb_height - self.sinking_depth
    
    @property
    def well_cap_top_level(self) -> float:
        return self.cutoff_level + self.well_cap_thickness
    
    @property
    def concrete_volume_steining(self) -> float:
        """Volume of well steining"""
        if self.well_shape == WellShape.CIRCULAR_SINGLE:
            outer_area = math.pi * (self.external_diameter / 2) ** 2
            inner_area = math.pi * (self.internal_diameter / 2) ** 2
            return (outer_area - inner_area) * self.sinking_depth
        return 0  # Add calculations for other shapes
    
    @property
    def concrete_volume_curb(self) -> float:
        """Volume of well curb"""
        if self.well_shape == WellShape.CIRCULAR_SINGLE:
            # Approximate as frustum
            avg_diameter = (self.external_diameter + self.curb_diameter) / 2
            return math.pi * (avg_diameter / 2) ** 2 * self.curb_height
        return 0
    
    @property
    def concrete_volume_bottom_plug(self) -> float:
        """Volume of bottom plug"""
        if self.well_shape == WellShape.CIRCULAR_SINGLE:
            return math.pi * (self.internal_diameter / 2) ** 2 * self.bottom_plug_thickness
        return 0


@dataclass
class WellReinforcement:
    """Well steining and curb reinforcement"""
    # Vertical bars in steining
    vertical_bar_dia: int = 20  # mm
    vertical_bar_spacing: float = 0.15  # m
    
    # Horizontal rings
    ring_bar_dia: int = 12  # mm
    ring_spacing: float = 0.15  # m
    
    # Curb reinforcement
    curb_main_dia: int = 25  # mm
    curb_stirrup_dia: int = 12  # mm
    
    # Well cap
    cap_bottom_mesh_dia: int = 25  # mm
    cap_top_mesh_dia: int = 20  # mm


@dataclass
class OpenFoundationDimensions:
    """Open foundation (raft or individual footing) dimensions"""
    foundation_type: str = "RAFT"  # RAFT, ISOLATED, COMBINED
    
    # Dimensions
    length: float = 8.0  # m
    width: float = 6.0   # m
    thickness: float = 2.0  # m
    
    # Steps (for stepped foundation)
    number_of_steps: int = 2
    step_height: float = 0.5  # m
    step_offset: float = 0.3  # m per step
    
    @property
    def bottom_area(self) -> float:
        if self.number_of_steps > 1:
            # Bottom step is smaller
            bottom_length = self.length - 2 * (self.number_of_steps - 1) * self.step_offset
            bottom_width = self.width - 2 * (self.number_of_steps - 1) * self.step_offset
            return bottom_length * bottom_width
        return self.length * self.width
    
    @property
    def concrete_volume(self) -> float:
        if self.number_of_steps > 1:
            # Calculate stepped volume
            total_volume = 0
            for i in range(self.number_of_steps):
                step_length = self.length - 2 * i * self.step_offset
                step_width = self.width - 2 * i * self.step_offset
                total_volume += step_length * step_width * self.step_height
            return total_volume
        return self.length * self.width * self.thickness


@dataclass
class FoundationModel:
    """Complete foundation model"""
    foundation_type: FoundationType
    support_type: str  # "PIER" or "ABUTMENT"
    support_number: int
    
    # Support dimensions
    support_width: float  # m (transverse)
    support_length: float  # m (longitudinal)
    support_height: float  # m
    
    # Foundation specific
    pile_config: Optional[PileGroupConfiguration] = None
    pile_dimensions: Optional[PileDimensions] = None
    well_dimensions: Optional[WellDimensions] = None
    open_dimensions: Optional[OpenFoundationDimensions] = None
    
    # Levels
    ground_level: float = 0.0
    cutoff_level: float = -1.0
    scour_level: float = -3.0
    
    # Reinforcement
    pile_cap_reinf: Optional[PileCapReinforcement] = None
    well_reinf: Optional[WellReinforcement] = None
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        """Initialize default reinforcement if not provided"""
        if self.foundation_type == FoundationType.PILE and self.pile_cap_reinf is None:
            self.pile_cap_reinf = PileCapReinforcement()
        elif self.foundation_type == FoundationType.WELL and self.well_reinf is None:
            self.well_reinf = WellReinforcement()
    
    def get_pile_cap_outline(self) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
        """Get pile cap outline coordinates"""
        if self.foundation_type != FoundationType.PILE or not self.pile_config:
            return []
        
        pc = self.pile_config
        half_length = pc.pile_cap_length / 2
        half_width = pc.pile_cap_width / 2
        
        return [
            (-half_length, -half_width),
            (half_length, -half_width),
            (half_length, half_width),
            (-half_length, half_width),
            (-half_length, -half_width)
        ]
    
    def get_pile_positions_absolute(self) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float]]:
        """Get pile positions with levels (x, y, cutoff_level)"""
        if self.foundation_type != FoundationType.PILE or not self.pile_config:
            return []
        
        positions = self.pile_config.get_pile_positions()
        return [(x, y, self.cutoff_level) for x, y in positions]
    
    def get_well_outline(self) -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[float, float]]]:
        """Get well outline at different levels"""
        if self.foundation_type != FoundationType.WELL or not self.well_dimensions:
            return {}
        
        wd = self.well_dimensions
        
        if wd.well_shape == WellShape.CIRCULAR_SINGLE:
            # Generate circular outline
            num_points = 64
            steining_outer = []
            steining_inner = []
            
            for i in range(num_points):
                angle = 2 * math.pi * i / num_points
                # Outer
                x_outer = (wd.external_diameter / 2) * math.cos(angle)
                y_outer = (wd.external_diameter / 2) * math.sin(angle)
                steining_outer.append((x_outer, y_outer))
                # Inner
                x_inner = (wd.internal_diameter / 2) * math.cos(angle)
                y_inner = (wd.internal_diameter / 2) * math.sin(angle)
                steining_inner.append((x_inner, y_inner))
            
            # Curb outline (larger diameter)
            curb_outer = []
            curb_inner = []
            for i in range(num_points):
                angle = 2 * math.pi * i / num_points
                x_outer = (wd.curb_diameter / 2) * math.cos(angle)
                y_outer = (wd.curb_diameter / 2) * math.sin(angle)
                curb_outer.append((x_outer, y_outer))
                # Inner same as steining inner
                x_inner = (wd.internal_diameter / 2) * math.cos(angle)
                y_inner = (wd.internal_diameter / 2) * math.sin(angle)
                curb_inner.append((x_inner, y_inner))
            
            return {
                'steining_outer': steining_outer,
                'steining_inner': steining_inner,
                'curb_outer': curb_outer,
                'curb_inner': curb_inner
            }
        
        return {}
    
    def get_material_quantities(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate material quantities"""
        quantities = {
            'concrete_m3': 0,
            'steel_kg': 0,
            'excavation_m3': 0
        }
        
        if self.foundation_type == FoundationType.PILE:
            # Pile concrete
            pile_vol = (self.pile_dimensions.concrete_volume * 
                       self.pile_config.total_piles)
            
            # Pile cap concrete
            cap_vol = (self.pile_config.pile_cap_length * 
                      self.pile_config.pile_cap_width * 
                      self.pile_config.pile_cap_thickness)
            
            quantities['concrete_m3'] = pile_vol + cap_vol
            
            # Steel
            pile_steel = (self.pile_dimensions.main_steel_weight + 
                         self.pile_dimensions.spiral_steel_weight)
            quantities['steel_kg'] = pile_steel * self.pile_config.total_piles
            
            # Excavation (approximate)
            quantities['excavation_m3'] = cap_vol * 1.5
        
        elif self.foundation_type == FoundationType.WELL:
            wd = self.well_dimensions
            quantities['concrete_m3'] = (
                wd.concrete_volume_steining + 
                wd.concrete_volume_curb +
                wd.concrete_volume_bottom_plug
            )
            
            # Well cap
            cap_area = math.pi * (wd.external_diameter / 2) ** 2
            quantities['concrete_m3'] += cap_area * wd.well_cap_thickness
        
        elif self.foundation_type == FoundationType.OPEN:
            quantities['concrete_m3'] = self.open_dimensions.concrete_volume
            quantities['excavation_m3'] = self.open_dimensions.bottom_area * \
                                        (self.ground_level - self.open_dimensions.thickness - 2)
        
        return quantities
    
    def get_drawing_info(self) -> Dict:
        """Get information for drawing generation"""
        info = {
            'foundation_type': self.foundation_type.value,
            'support_type': self.support_type,
            'support_number': self.support_number,
            'levels': {
                'ground': self.ground_level,
                'cutoff': self.cutoff_level,
                'scour': self.scour_level
            }
        }
        
        if self.foundation_type == FoundationType.PILE:
            info['pile'] = {
                'diameter': self.pile_dimensions.diameter,
                'length': self.pile_dimensions.length,
                'number': self.pile_config.total_piles,
                'layout': f"{self.pile_config.num_piles_longitudinal}x{self.pile_config.num_piles_transverse}",
                'cap_thickness': self.pile_config.pile_cap_thickness
            }
        
        elif self.foundation_type == FoundationType.WELL:
            wd = self.well_dimensions
            info['well'] = {
                'diameter': wd.external_diameter,
                'steining_thickness': wd.steining_thickness,
                'sinking_depth': wd.sinking_depth,
                'bottom_level': wd.well_bottom_level
            }
        
        return info
```

---

### File 2: `foundation_drawing.py` - DXF Generator

```python
"""
Foundation Detail Drawing Generator
Creates professional DXF drawings of foundation details
"""

import ezdxf
from ezdxf.enums import TextEntityAlignment
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
from foundation_core import (
    FoundationModel, FoundationType, PileGroupConfiguration,
    PileDimensions, WellDimensions, OpenFoundationDimensions,
    WellShape, PileCapShape
)


class FoundationDrawing:
    """
    Generate foundation detail drawings in DXF format
    """
    
    LAYERS = {
        'PILE': {'color': 4, 'linetype': 'DASHED', 'lineweight': 0.25},
        'PILE_CAP': {'color': 1, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.35},
        'WELL_STEINING': {'color': 5, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.30},
        'WELL_CURB': {'color': 6, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.30},
        'FOUNDATION': {'color': 2, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.35},
        'REINFORCEMENT': {'color': 3, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.25},
        'CENTERLINE': {'color': 6, 'linetype': 'CENTER', 'lineweight': 0.18},
        'DIMENSION': {'color': 7, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.18},
        'TEXT': {'color': 7, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.18},
        'HATCH': {'color': 253, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.13},
        'GROUND': {'color': 3, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS', 'lineweight': 0.25},
        'LEVEL': {'color': 131, 'linetype': 'DASHED', 'lineweight': 0.18}
    }
    
    def __init__(self, model: FoundationModel, scale: float = 10.0):
        self.model = model
        self.scale = scale
        
        self.doc = ezdxf.new('R2018', setup=True)
        self.msp = self.doc.modelspace()
        self.setup_layers()
        self.setup_styles()
    
    def setup_layers(self):
        """Create all required layers"""
        for name, attrs in self.LAYERS.items():
            self.doc.layers.add(
                name=name,
                color=attrs['color'],
                linetype=attrs['linetype'],
                lineweight=attrs['lineweight']
            )
    
    def setup_styles(self):
        """Setup text and dimension styles"""
        self.doc.styles.add('FOUNDATION_FONT', font='Arial.ttf')
        
        dimstyle = self.doc.dimstyles.add('FOUNDATION_DIM')
        dimstyle.dxf.dimtxt = 2.5
        dimstyle.dxf.dimexe = 1.0
        dimstyle.dxf.dimexo = 0.5
        dimstyle.dxf.dimtad = 1
        dimstyle.dxf.dimdec = 2
    
    def _to_dxf(self, x: float, y: float) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """Convert model coordinates to DXF coordinates"""
        return (x * self.scale, y * self.scale)
    
    def generate_pile_foundation(self, output_path: str = "pile_foundation.dxf"):
        """Generate complete pile foundation drawing"""
        if self.model.foundation_type != FoundationType.PILE:
            raise ValueError("Not a pile foundation")
        
        # Generate all views
        self._draw_pile_cap_plan()
        self._draw_pile_cap_section()
        self._draw_pile_details()
        self._draw_pile_reinforcement()
        self._draw_pile_schedule()
        
        # Title block
        self._draw_foundation_title_block()
        
        self.doc.saveas(output_path)
        print(f"Pile foundation drawing saved: {output_path}")
        return output_path
    
    def _draw_pile_cap_plan(self):
        """Draw pile cap plan view"""
        pc = self.model.pile_config
        pd = self.model.pile_dimensions
        
        # Draw pile cap outline
        cap_outline = self.model.get_pile_cap_outline()
        cap_points = [self._to_dxf(x, y) for x, y in cap_outline]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=cap_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE_CAP'}
        )
        
        # Hatch pile cap
        hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=1, dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE_CAP'})
        hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(cap_points, is_closed=True)
        hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.3)
        
        # Draw piles (as circles)
        pile_positions = pc.get_pile_positions()
        for i, (x, y) in enumerate(pile_positions, 1):
            center = self._to_dxf(x, y)
            radius = pd.radius * self.scale
            
            # Pile circle
            self.msp.add_circle(
                center=center,
                radius=radius,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE'}
            )
            
            # Pile number
            self.msp.add_text(
                str(i),
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'FOUNDATION_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(
                center,
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
        
        # Draw support shaft
        shaft_half_length = self.model.support_length / 2
        shaft_half_width = self.model.support_width / 2
        
        shaft_points = [
            self._to_dxf(-shaft_half_length, -shaft_half_width),
            self._to_dxf(shaft_half_length, -shaft_half_width),
            self._to_dxf(shaft_half_length, shaft_half_width),
            self._to_dxf(-shaft_half_length, shaft_half_width),
            self._to_dxf(-shaft_half_length, -shaft_half_width)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=shaft_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        
        # Centerlines
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-pc.pile_cap_length/2 - 2, 0),
            end=self._to_dxf(pc.pile_cap_length/2 + 2, 0),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(0, -pc.pile_cap_width/2 - 2),
            end=self._to_dxf(0, pc.pile_cap_width/2 + 2),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        
        # Dimensions
        self._draw_pile_cap_dimensions()
    
    def _draw_pile_cap_dimensions(self):
        """Draw dimensions for pile cap plan"""
        pc = self.model.pile_config
        
        # Overall cap dimensions
        half_length = pc.pile_cap_length / 2
        half_width = pc.pile_cap_width / 2
        
        # Length dimension
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-half_length, -half_width - 3),
            end=self._to_dxf(half_length, -half_width - 3),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        )
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"L = {pc.pile_cap_length:.2f}m",
            height=2.5,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION', 'style': 'FOUNDATION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(0, -half_width - 4),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        # Width dimension
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(half_length + 3, -half_width),
            end=self._to_dxf(half_length + 3, half_width),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        )
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"W = {pc.pile_cap_width:.2f}m",
            height=2.5,
            rotation=90,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION', 'style': 'FOUNDATION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(half_length + 4, 0),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        # Pile spacing dimensions
        pile_positions = pc.get_pile_positions()
        if len(pile_positions) > 1:
            # Longitudinal spacing
            y_offset = -half_width - 6
            for i in range(pc.num_piles_longitudinal - 1):
                x1 = pile_positions[i * pc.num_piles_transverse][0]
                x2 = pile_positions[(i + 1) * pc.num_piles_transverse][0]
                
                self.msp.add_line(
                    start=self._to_dxf(x1, y_offset),
                    end=self._to_dxf(x2, y_offset),
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
                )
                self.msp.add_text(
                    f"{pc.spacing_longitudinal:.2f}m",
                    height=2.0,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION', 'style': 'FOUNDATION_FONT'}
                ).set_placement(
                    self._to_dxf((x1 + x2) / 2, y_offset - 1),
                    align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
                )
    
    def _draw_pile_cap_section(self):
        """Draw pile cap section view (positioned to the right)"""
        offset_x = self.model.pile_config.pile_cap_length + 10
        
        pc = self.model.pile_config
        pd = self.model.pile_dimensions
        
        # Ground level
        ground_y = 2  # Arbitrary ground level for section
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(offset_x - 5, ground_y),
            end=self._to_dxf(offset_x + pc.pile_cap_length + 5, ground_y),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'GROUND'}
        )
        
        # Pile cap section
        cap_top = ground_y
        cap_bottom = ground_y - pc.pile_cap_thickness
        
        cap_left = offset_x
        cap_right = offset_x + pc.pile_cap_length
        
        cap_points = [
            self._to_dxf(cap_left, cap_top),
            self._to_dxf(cap_right, cap_top),
            self._to_dxf(cap_right, cap_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(cap_left, cap_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(cap_left, cap_top)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=cap_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE_CAP'}
        )
        
        # Hatch cap
        hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=1, dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE_CAP'})
        hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(cap_points, is_closed=True)
        hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.3)
        
        # Support shaft
        shaft_half = self.model.support_length / 2
        shaft_top = cap_top + self.model.support_height
        shaft_left = offset_x + pc.pile_cap_length/2 - shaft_half
        shaft_right = offset_x + pc.pile_cap_length/2 + shaft_half
        
        shaft_points = [
            self._to_dxf(shaft_left, cap_top),
            self._to_dxf(shaft_right, cap_top),
            self._to_dxf(shaft_right, shaft_top),
            self._to_dxf(shaft_left, shaft_top),
            self._to_dxf(shaft_left, cap_top)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=shaft_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        
        # Piles (shown as lines in section)
        pile_positions = pc.get_pile_positions()
        for x, y in pile_positions:
            pile_x = offset_x + pc.pile_cap_length/2 + x
            pile_top = cap_bottom
            pile_bottom = pile_top - pd.length
            
            # Pile line
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf(pile_x - pd.radius, pile_top),
                end=self._to_dxf(pile_x - pd.radius * 0.7, pile_bottom),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE'}
            )
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf(pile_x + pd.radius, pile_top),
                end=self._to_dxf(pile_x + pd.radius * 0.7, pile_bottom),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE'}
            )
        
        # Level annotations
        levels = [
            (cap_top, f"Cutoff RL {self.model.cutoff_level:.3f}"),
            (cap_bottom, f"Cap Bottom RL {self.model.cutoff_level - pc.pile_cap_thickness:.3f}"),
            (pile_bottom, f"Pile Toe RL {self.model.cutoff_level - pc.pile_cap_thickness - pd.length:.3f}")
        ]
        
        for y, text in levels:
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf(cap_right + 2, y),
                end=self._to_dxf(cap_right + 5, y),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'LEVEL'}
            )
            self.msp.add_text(
                text,
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'FOUNDATION_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(
                self._to_dxf(cap_right + 6, y),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
            )
        
        # Title
        self.msp.add_text(
            "SECTION A-A",
            height=3.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'FOUNDATION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(offset_x + pc.pile_cap_length/2, shaft_top + 3),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
    
    def _draw_pile_details(self):
        """Draw detailed pile cross-section and elevation"""
        offset_x = self.model.pile_config.pile_cap_length * 2 + 15
        offset_y = -10
        
        pd = self.model.pile_dimensions
        
        # Pile cross-section
        center = self._to_dxf(offset_x, offset_y)
        radius = pd.radius * self.scale
        
        # Outer circle
        self.msp.add_circle(
            center=center,
            radius=radius,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE'}
        )
        
        # Main bars (as small circles)
        num_bars = pd.number_of_main_bars
        bar_radius = pd.main_bar_diameter / 2000 * self.scale
        spiral_radius = radius - 75  # 75mm cover
        
        for i in range(num_bars):
            angle = 2 * math.pi * i / num_bars
            bar_x = offset_x + (spiral_radius / self.scale) * math.cos(angle)
            bar_y = offset_y + (spiral_radius / self.scale) * math.sin(angle)
            
            self.msp.add_circle(
                center=self._to_dxf(bar_x, bar_y),
                radius=bar_radius,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT'}
            )
        
        # Spiral (as inner circle)
        self.msp.add_circle(
            center=center,
            radius=spiral_radius * 0.9,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT', 'linetype': 'DASHED'}
        )
        
        # Pile elevation (to the right)
        elev_x = offset_x + pd.diameter * 2
        elev_y = offset_y
        
        # Pile outline
        pile_points = [
            self._to_dxf(elev_x - pd.radius, elev_y),
            self._to_dxf(elev_x + pd.radius, elev_y),
            self._to_dxf(elev_x + pd.radius * 0.8, elev_y - pd.length),
            self._to_dxf(elev_x - pd.radius * 0.8, elev_y - pd.length),
            self._to_dxf(elev_x - pd.radius, elev_y)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=pile_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE'}
        )
        
        # Main bars in elevation
        for i in range(num_bars):
            bar_x = elev_x - spiral_radius / self.scale + i * (2 * spiral_radius / self.scale / (num_bars - 1))
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf(bar_x, elev_y),
                end=self._to_dxf(bar_x, elev_y - pd.length),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT'}
            )
        
        # Spiral lines
        num_spirals = int(pd.length / pd.spiral_pitch)
        for i in range(num_spirals):
            spiral_y = elev_y - i * pd.spiral_pitch
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf(elev_x - pd.radius * 0.8, spiral_y),
                end=self._to_dxf(elev_x + pd.radius * 0.8, spiral_y),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT', 'linetype': 'DASHED'}
            )
        
        # Dimensions
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"Pile Ø{pd.diameter*1000:.0f}mm",
            height=2.5,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'FOUNDATION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(offset_x, offset_y + pd.radius + 2),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"Length: {pd.length:.1f}m",
            height=2.5,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'FOUNDATION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(elev_x + pd.radius + 2, elev_y - pd.length/2),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
        )
    
    def _draw_pile_reinforcement(self):
        """Draw pile cap reinforcement details"""
        # This would show the mesh layout
        pass
    
    def _draw_pile_schedule(self):
        """Draw pile schedule table"""
        offset_y = -self.model.pile_dimensions.length - 20
        
        # Table header
        headers = ['Pile No.', 'Dia (mm)', 'Length (m)', 'Cutoff RL', 'Toe RL', 'Main Bars', 'Spiral']
        col_widths = [12, 15, 15, 18, 18, 20, 18]
        
        x = 0
        for i, header in enumerate(headers):
            self.msp.add_text(
                header,
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'FOUNDATION_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(
                self._to_dxf(x + col_widths[i]/2, offset_y),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
            x += col_widths[i]
        
        # Table rows
        pc = self.model.pile_config
        pd = self.model.pile_dimensions
        
        for row in range(pc.total_piles):
            y = offset_y - (row + 1) * 3
            x = 0
            
            values = [
                str(row + 1),
                f"{pd.diameter*1000:.0f}",
                f"{pd.length:.2f}",
                f"{self.model.cutoff_level:.3f}",
                f"{self.model.cutoff_level - pc.pile_cap_thickness - pd.length:.3f}",
                f"{pd.number_of_main_bars}-Ø{pd.main_bar_dia}",
                f"Ø{pd.spiral_bar_dia}@{pd.spiral_pitch*1000:.0f}"
            ]
            
            for i, value in enumerate(values):
                self.msp.add_text(
                    value,
                    height=2.0,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'FOUNDATION_FONT'}
                ).set_placement(
                    self._to_dxf(x + col_widths[i]/2, y),
                    align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
                )
                x += col_widths[i]
    
    def generate_well_foundation(self, output_path: str = "well_foundation.dxf"):
        """Generate well foundation drawing"""
        if self.model.foundation_type != FoundationType.WELL:
            raise ValueError("Not a well foundation")
        
        self._draw_well_plan()
        self._draw_well_section()
        self._draw_well_reinforcement()
        self._draw_foundation_title_block()
        
        self.doc.saveas(output_path)
        print(f"Well foundation drawing saved: {output_path}")
        return output_path
    
    def _draw_well_plan(self):
        """Draw well plan view at cutoff level"""
        wd = self.model.well_dimensions
        
        outlines = self.model.get_well_outline()
        
        # Steining outer
        outer_points = [self._to_dxf(x, y) for x, y in outlines['steining_outer']]
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=outer_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL_STEINING'}
        )
        
        # Steining inner
        inner_points = [self._to_dxf(x, y) for x, y in outlines['steining_inner']]
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=inner_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL_STEINING'}
        )
        
        # Hatch steining
        hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=5, dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL_STEINING'})
        hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(outer_points, is_closed=True)
        hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(inner_points, is_closed=True)
        hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.3)
        
        # Centerlines
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-wd.external_diameter/2 - 2, 0),
            end=self._to_dxf(wd.external_diameter/2 + 2, 0),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(0, -wd.external_diameter/2 - 2),
            end=self._to_dxf(0, wd.external_diameter/2 + 2),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        
        # Dimensions
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"Ø{wd.external_diameter:.2f}m",
            height=2.5,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION', 'style': 'FOUNDATION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(wd.external_diameter/2 + 1, 0),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
        )
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"Steining: {wd.steining_thickness*1000:.0f}mm",
            height=2.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'FOUNDATION_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(0, -wd.external_diameter/2 - 3),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
    
    def _draw_well_section(self):
        """Draw well section"""
        offset_x = self.model.well_dimensions.external_diameter + 10
        
        wd = self.model.well_dimensions
        
        # Well cap
        cap_top = 2
        cap_bottom = cap_top - wd.well_cap_thickness
        
        cap_left = offset_x - wd.external_diameter/2
        cap_right = offset_x + wd.external_diameter/2
        
        cap_points = [
            self._to_dxf(cap_left, cap_top),
            self._to_dxf(cap_right, cap_top),
            self._to_dxf(cap_right, cap_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(cap_left, cap_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(cap_left, cap_top)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=cap_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL_STEINING'}
        )
        
        # Steining
        steining_top = cap_bottom
        steining_bottom = steining_top - wd.sinking_depth
        
        steining_outer_left = cap_left
        steining_outer_right = cap_right
        steining_inner_left = offset_x - wd.internal_diameter/2
        steining_inner_right = offset_x + wd.internal_diameter/2
        
        steining_points = [
            self._to_dxf(steining_outer_left, steining_top),
            self._to_dxf(steining_outer_right, steining_top),
            self._to_dxf(steining_outer_right, steining_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(steining_inner_right, steining_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(steining_inner_right, steining_top),
            self._to_dxf(steining_inner_left, steining_top),
            self._to_dxf(steining_inner_left, steining_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(steining_outer_left, steining_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(steining_outer_left, steining_top)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=steining_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL_STEINING'}
        )
        
        # Curb (larger at bottom)
        curb_top = steining_bottom
        curb_bottom = curb_top - wd.curb_height
        
        curb_outer_left = offset_x - wd.curb_diameter/2
        curb_outer_right = offset_x + wd.curb_diameter/2
        
        curb_points = [
            self._to_dxf(steining_outer_left, curb_top),
            self._to_dxf(steining_outer_right, curb_top),
            self._to_dxf(curb_outer_right, curb_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(steining_inner_right, curb_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(steining_inner_right, curb_top),
            self._to_dxf(steining_inner_left, curb_top),
            self._to_dxf(steining_inner_left, curb_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(curb_outer_left, curb_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(steining_outer_left, curb_top)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=curb_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL_CURB'}
        )
        
        # Bottom plug
        plug_top = curb_bottom
        plug_bottom = plug_top - wd.bottom_plug_thickness
        
        plug_points = [
            self._to_dxf(steining_inner_left, plug_top),
            self._to_dxf(steining_inner_right, plug_top),
            self._to_dxf(steining_inner_right, plug_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(steining_inner_left, plug_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(steining_inner_left, plug_top)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=plug_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'FOUNDATION'}
        )
        
        # Level annotations
        levels = [
            (cap_top, f"Well Cap Top RL {wd.well_cap_top_level:.3f}"),
            (cap_bottom, f"Cutoff RL {wd.cutoff_level:.3f}"),
            (steining_bottom, f"Steining Bottom RL {wd.cutoff_level - wd.sinking_depth:.3f}"),
            (curb_bottom, f"Curb Bottom RL {wd.well_bottom_level:.3f}")
        ]
        
        for y, text in levels:
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf(cap_right + 2, y),
                end=self._to_dxf(cap_right + 5, y),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'LEVEL'}
            )
            self.msp.add_text(
                text,
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'style': 'FOUNDATION_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(
                self._to_dxf(cap_right + 6, y),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
            )
    
    def _draw_well_reinforcement(self):
        """Draw well steining reinforcement details"""
        pass
    
    def _draw_foundation_title_block(self):
        """Draw title block for foundation drawing"""
        # Similar to other title blocks
        pass
    
    def generate(self, output_path: str = "foundation.dxf"):
        """Generate appropriate foundation drawing based on type"""
        if self.model.foundation_type == FoundationType.PILE:
            return self.generate_pile_foundation(output_path)
        elif self.model.foundation_type == FoundationType.WELL:
            return self.generate_well_foundation(output_path)
        else:
            raise NotImplementedError(f"Drawing not implemented for {self.model.foundation_type}")
```

### File 3: `example_foundation.py` - Complete Examples

```python
"""
Example: Generate foundation details for different types
"""

from foundation_core import (
    FoundationModel, FoundationType, PileGroupConfiguration,
    PileDimensions, WellDimensions, OpenFoundationDimensions,
    PileType, WellShape, PileCapShape
)
from foundation_drawing import FoundationDrawing


def create_pile_foundation_example():
    """Create pile foundation example"""
    print("=" * 70)
    print("PILE FOUNDATION EXAMPLE")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    # Pile dimensions
    pile = PileDimensions(
        diameter=1.2,
        length=15.0,
        pile_type=PileType.BORED_CAST_IN_SITU,
        cutoff_level=172.500,
        main_bar_diameter=20,
        number_of_main_bars=12,
        spiral_bar_diameter=10,
        spiral_pitch=0.15
    )
    
    # Pile group configuration
    pile_group = PileGroupConfiguration(
        num_piles_longitudinal=3,
        num_piles_transverse=3,
        spacing_longitudinal=2.5,
        spacing_transverse=2.5,
        pile_cap_shape=PileCapShape.RECTANGULAR,
        pile_cap_thickness=2.0,
        pile_cap_projection=0.3
    )
    
    # Create foundation model
    foundation = FoundationModel(
        foundation_type=FoundationType.PILE,
        support_type="PIER",
        support_number=1,
        support_width=2.0,
        support_length=10.0,
        support_height=10.0,
        pile_config=pile_group,
        pile_dimensions=pile,
        ground_level=175.200,
        cutoff_level=172.500,
        scour_level=170.000
    )
    
    print(f"\nFoundation Details:")
    print(f"  Type: {foundation.foundation_type.value}")
    print(f"  Support: {foundation.support_type} #{foundation.support_number}")
    print(f"  Pile Diameter: {pile.diameter}m")
    print(f"  Pile Length: {pile.length}m")
    print(f"  Number of Piles: {pile_group.total_piles}")
    print(f"  Layout: {pile_group.num_piles_longitudinal}x{pile_group.num_piles_transverse}")
    print(f"  Pile Cap: {pile_group.pile_cap_length:.2f}m x {pile_group.pile_cap_width:.2f}m")
    
    # Material quantities
    quantities = foundation.get_material_quantities()
    print(f"\nMaterial Quantities:")
    print(f"  Concrete: {quantities['concrete_m3']:.2f} m³")
    print(f"  Steel: {quantities['steel_kg']:.1f} kg")
    print(f"  Excavation: {quantities['excavation_m3']:.2f} m³")
    
    # Generate drawing
    drawing = FoundationDrawing(foundation, scale=8.0)
    drawing.generate("pile_foundation_pier1.dxf")
    
    return foundation


def create_well_foundation_example():
    """Create well foundation example"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("WELL FOUNDATION EXAMPLE")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    # Well dimensions
    well = WellDimensions(
        well_shape=WellShape.CIRCULAR_SINGLE,
        external_diameter=6.0,
        steining_thickness=0.6,
        curb_height=2.0,
        sinking_depth=12.0,
        bottom_plug_thickness=2.0,
        well_cap_thickness=2.0,
        cutoff_level=172.500,
        bed_level=175.000
    )
    
    # Create foundation model
    foundation = FoundationModel(
        foundation_type=FoundationType.WELL,
        support_type="ABUTMENT",
        support_number=1,
        support_width=1.5,
        support_length=11.0,
        support_height=8.0,
        well_dimensions=well,
        ground_level=175.200,
        cutoff_level=172.500,
        scour_level=170.000
    )
    
    print(f"\nFoundation Details:")
    print(f"  Type: {foundation.foundation_type.value}")
    print(f"  Support: {foundation.support_type} #{foundation.support_number}")
    print(f"  Well Diameter: {well.external_diameter}m")
    print(f"  Steining Thickness: {well.steining_thickness*1000:.0f}mm")
    print(f"  Sinking Depth: {well.sinking_depth}m")
    print(f"  Well Bottom Level: {well.well_bottom_level:.3f}m")
    
    # Material quantities
    quantities = foundation.get_material_quantities()
    print(f"\nMaterial Quantities:")
    print(f"  Concrete: {quantities['concrete_m3']:.2f} m³")
    
    # Generate drawing
    drawing = FoundationDrawing(foundation, scale=6.0)
    drawing.generate("well_foundation_abut1.dxf")
    
    return foundation


def create_open_foundation_example():
    """Create open foundation example"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("OPEN FOUNDATION EXAMPLE")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    # Open foundation dimensions
    open_foundation = OpenFoundationDimensions(
        foundation_type="RAFT",
        length=8.0,
        width=6.0,
        thickness=2.0,
        number_of_steps=2,
        step_height=0.5,
        step_offset=0.3
    )
    
    # Create foundation model
    foundation = FoundationModel(
        foundation_type=FoundationType.OPEN,
        support_type="PIER",
        support_number=2,
        support_width=2.0,
        support_length=8.0,
        support_height=10.0,
        open_dimensions=open_foundation,
        ground_level=175.200,
        cutoff_level=175.200,
        scour_level=172.000
    )
    
    print(f"\nFoundation Details:")
    print(f"  Type: {foundation.foundation_type.value}")
    print(f"  Support: {foundation.support_type} #{foundation.support_number}")
    print(f"  Foundation Size: {open_foundation.length}m x {open_foundation.width}m")
    print(f"  Number of Steps: {open_foundation.number_of_steps}")
    
    # Material quantities
    quantities = foundation.get_material_quantities()
    print(f"\nMaterial Quantities:")
    print(f"  Concrete: {quantities['concrete_m3']:.2f} m³")
    print(f"  Excavation: {quantities['excavation_m3']:.2f} m³")
    
    return foundation


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Generate all foundation types
    pile = create_pile_foundation_example()
    well = create_well_foundation_example()
    open_foundation = create_open_foundation_example()
    
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("ALL FOUNDATION DRAWINGS GENERATED")
    print("=" * 70)
```

---

## KEY FEATURES

| Feature | Pile Foundation | Well Foundation | Open Foundation |
|---------|----------------|-----------------|-----------------|
| **Plan View** | Pile layout with cap outline | Well steining plan | Footing plan with steps |
| **Section View** | Pile elevation with cap | Well sinking profile | Stepped section |
| **Reinforcement** | Main bars, spiral, cap mesh | Vertical bars, rings | Bottom/top mesh |
| **Schedule** | Pile schedule table | Steining details | N/A |
| **Levels** | Cutoff, toe, cap bottom | Cutoff, steining bottom, curb | Foundation top/bottom |
| **Quantities** | Concrete, steel, excavation | Concrete volumes | Concrete, excavation |

from skew_bridge_engine import create_skew_bridge
from longitudinal_section_core import LongitudinalProfile
from cross_section_core import CrossSectionModel
from foundation_core import FoundationModel
from foundation_drawing import FoundationDrawing

# Create complete bridge
bridge = create_skew_bridge(
    name="Chambakkara Bridge",
    chainage=600.0,
    span_lengths=[25, 30, 25],
    deck_width=12.0,
    skew_angle=15.0
)

# Generate foundation for each support
for support in bridge.supports:
    if support.foundation.foundation_type == "PILE":
        foundation = FoundationModel.from_support(support)
        drawing = FoundationDrawing(foundation)
        drawing.generate(f"foundation_{support.support_type}_{support.support_number}.dxf")
