# REINFORCEMENT SCHEDULE GENERATOR MODULE

## MATHEMATICAL FOUNDATION

```
BAR BENDING SCHEDULE FORMAT
===========================

Bar Shape Codes (BS 8666 / IS 2502):
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Shape │ Description              │ Formula                 │
├───────┼──────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────┤
│   A   │ Straight bar             │ A                       │
│   B   │ L-bar (2 legs)           │ A + B - 2r - 2d         │
│   C   │ U-bar (3 legs)           │ A + 2B + C - 4r - 4d    │
│   D   │ Stirrup (closed)         │ 2(A+B) + 20d            │
│   E   │ L-bar with hook          │ A + B + C - 2r - 2d     │
│   F   │ Crank bar (2 bends)      │ A + 2B + C - 0.42r - 4d │
│   G   │ Circular bar             │ πD                      │
│   H   │ Helical/spiral           │ √(πD)² + p² × n         │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

Deductions:
- 45° bend: 1d deduction
- 90° bend: 2d deduction  
- 135° bend: 3d deduction
- 180° hook: 9d (addition)

Where:
d = bar diameter
r = bend radius (typically 2d for mild steel, 4d for HYSD)
```

---

## COMPLETE PYTHON MODULE

### File 1: `reinforcement_core.py` - Core Calculations

```python
"""
Reinforcement Schedule Generator Core
Calculates bar bending schedules, cutting lengths, and material quantities
"""

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Optional
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime


class BarShape(Enum):
    """Standard bar shapes per IS 2502 / BS 8666"""
    STRAIGHT = "A"           # Straight bar
    L_SHAPE = "B"          # L-bar (2 legs)
    U_SHAPE = "C"          # U-bar (3 legs)
    STIRRUP = "D"          # Closed stirrup
    L_WITH_HOOK = "E"       # L-bar with hook
    CRANKED = "F"          # Cranked bar (2 bends)
    CIRCULAR = "G"         # Circular bar
    SPIRAL = "H"           # Helical/spiral
    COLUMN_TIE = "I"       # Column tie with seismic hook
    TRUSS_BAR = "J"        # Truss bar (multiple bends)


class BarGrade(Enum):
    """Steel grades"""
    FE250 = "Fe 250"       # Mild steel
    FE415 = "Fe 415"       # HYSD
    FE500 = "Fe 500"       # HYSD
    FE550 = "Fe 550"       # HYSD


@dataclass
class BarDimension:
    """Dimensions for a bar shape"""
    A: float  # Primary dimension (m)
    B: Optional[float] = None  # Secondary dimension (m)
    C: Optional[float] = None  # Tertiary dimension (m)
    D: Optional[float] = None  # Quaternary dimension (m)
    radius: Optional[float] = None  # Bend radius (m)
    angle: Optional[float] = None  # Bend angle (degrees)


@dataclass
class ReinforcementBar:
    """Single reinforcement bar details"""
    bar_number: int
    bar_mark: str  # Reference mark (e.g., "D1", "B2")
    description: str
    shape: BarShape
    diameter: int  # mm
    grade: BarGrade
    dimensions: BarDimension
    
    # Calculated properties
    cutting_length: float = field(init=False)  # m
    unit_weight: float = field(init=False)   # kg/m
    weight_per_bar: float = field(init=False)  # kg
    number_of_bars: int = 1
    total_weight: float = field(init=False)   # kg
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        """Calculate bar properties"""
        self.unit_weight = self._calculate_unit_weight()
        self.cutting_length = self._calculate_cutting_length()
        self.weight_per_bar = self.cutting_length * self.unit_weight
        self.total_weight = self.weight_per_bar * self.number_of_bars
    
    def _calculate_unit_weight(self) -> float:
        """Calculate unit weight of bar (kg/m)"""
        # Weight = (π/4) × d² × 7850 kg/m³
        # Simplified: d² / 162.28 (for mm to kg/m)
        return (self.diameter ** 2) / 162.28
    
    def _calculate_cutting_length(self) -> float:
        """Calculate cutting length based on shape"""
        d = self.diameter / 1000  # Convert to meters
        r = self.dimensions.radius if self.dimensions.radius else 2 * d
        
        if self.shape == BarShape.STRAIGHT:
            # Shape A: Straight bar
            return self.dimensions.A
        
        elif self.shape == BarShape.L_SHAPE:
            # Shape B: L-bar
            # Formula: A + B - 2r - 2d
            if self.dimensions.B is None:
                raise ValueError("L-shape requires dimension B")
            return self.dimensions.A + self.dimensions.B - 2 * r - 2 * d
        
        elif self.shape == BarShape.U_SHAPE:
            # Shape C: U-bar
            # Formula: A + 2B + C - 4r - 4d
            if self.dimensions.B is None or self.dimensions.C is None:
                raise ValueError("U-shape requires dimensions B and C")
            return (self.dimensions.A + 2 * self.dimensions.B + 
                   self.dimensions.C - 4 * r - 4 * d)
        
        elif self.shape == BarShape.STIRRUP:
            # Shape D: Closed stirrup
            # Formula: 2(A+B) + 20d (hook allowance)
            if self.dimensions.B is None:
                raise ValueError("Stirrup requires dimension B")
            return 2 * (self.dimensions.A + self.dimensions.B) + 20 * d
        
        elif self.shape == BarShape.L_WITH_HOOK:
            # Shape E: L-bar with hook
            # Formula: A + B + C - 2r - 2d + 18d (hook)
            if self.dimensions.B is None or self.dimensions.C is None:
                raise ValueError("L-with-hook requires dimensions B and C")
            return (self.dimensions.A + self.dimensions.B + self.dimensions.C - 
                   2 * r - 2 * d + 18 * d)
        
        elif self.shape == BarShape.CRANKED:
            # Shape F: Cranked bar
            # Formula: A + 2B + C - 0.42r - 4d
            if self.dimensions.B is None or self.dimensions.C is None:
                raise ValueError("Cranked bar requires dimensions B and C")
            return (self.dimensions.A + 2 * self.dimensions.B + self.dimensions.C - 
                   0.42 * r - 4 * d)
        
        elif self.shape == BarShape.CIRCULAR:
            # Shape G: Circular bar
            # Formula: π × D
            return math.pi * self.dimensions.A
        
        elif self.shape == BarShape.SPIRAL:
            # Shape H: Helical/spiral
            # Formula: √(πD)² + p² × n
            if self.dimensions.B is None:  # Pitch
                raise ValueError("Spiral requires pitch dimension B")
            if self.dimensions.C is None:  # Number of turns
                raise ValueError("Spiral requires number of turns C")
            
            D = self.dimensions.A  # Mean diameter
            p = self.dimensions.B  # Pitch
            n = self.dimensions.C  # Number of turns
            
            circumference = math.pi * D
            return math.sqrt(circumference**2 + p**2) * n
        
        elif self.shape == BarShape.COLUMN_TIE:
            # Shape I: Column tie with 135° seismic hook
            # Formula: 2(A+B) + 24d (longer hook for seismic)
            if self.dimensions.B is None:
                raise ValueError("Column tie requires dimension B")
            return 2 * (self.dimensions.A + self.dimensions.B) + 24 * d
        
        else:
            # Default to straight
            return self.dimensions.A
    
    def get_shape_description(self) -> str:
        """Get human-readable shape description"""
        shape_desc = {
            BarShape.STRAIGHT: f"Straight bar L={self.dimensions.A:.3f}m",
            BarShape.L_SHAPE: f"L-bar {self.dimensions.A:.3f} × {self.dimensions.B:.3f}m",
            BarShape.U_SHAPE: f"U-bar {self.dimensions.A:.3f} × {self.dimensions.B:.3f}m",
            BarShape.STIRRUP: f"Stirrup {self.dimensions.A:.3f} × {self.dimensions.B:.3f}m",
            BarShape.CIRCULAR: f"Ring Ø{self.dimensions.A:.3f}m",
            BarShape.SPIRAL: f"Spiral Ø{self.dimensions.A:.3f}m, pitch {self.dimensions.B:.3f}m",
        }
        return shape_desc.get(self.shape, f"Shape {self.shape.value}")
    
    def get_bending_dimensions(self) -> str:
        """Get bending dimensions string for schedule"""
        dims = [f"{self.dimensions.A:.0f}"]
        if self.dimensions.B:
            dims.append(f"{self.dimensions.B:.0f}")
        if self.dimensions.C:
            dims.append(f"{self.dimensions.C:.0f}")
        if self.dimensions.D:
            dims.append(f"{self.dimensions.D:.0f}")
        return "-".join(dims)


@dataclass
class ElementSchedule:
    """Reinforcement schedule for a structural element"""
    element_type: str  # "DECK_SLAB", "GIRDER", "PIER", "ABUTMENT", etc.
    element_number: int
    element_name: str
    
    bars: List[ReinforcementBar] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    @property
    def total_weight(self) -> float:
        """Total weight of all bars in element"""
        return sum(bar.total_weight for bar in self.bars)
    
    @property
    def total_bars(self) -> int:
        """Total number of bars"""
        return sum(bar.number_of_bars for bar in self.bars)
    
    def get_summary_by_diameter(self) -> Dict[int, Dict]:
        """Get summary grouped by bar diameter"""
        summary = {}
        
        for bar in self.bars:
            d = bar.diameter
            if d not in summary:
                summary[d] = {
                    'count': 0,
                    'total_length': 0.0,
                    'total_weight': 0.0
                }
            
            summary[d]['count'] += bar.number_of_bars
            summary[d]['total_length'] += bar.cutting_length * bar.number_of_bars
            summary[d]['total_weight'] += bar.total_weight
        
        return summary


@dataclass
class BridgeReinforcementSchedule:
    """Complete reinforcement schedule for entire bridge"""
    project_name: str
    bridge_name: str
    date: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d"))
    
    element_schedules: List[ElementSchedule] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    # Additional data
    lap_lengths: Dict[int, float] = field(default_factory=dict)  # dia: length
    development_lengths: Dict[int, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    concrete_cover: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        """Initialize standard lap and development lengths"""
        # Lap length = 50d for Fe 415 (typical)
        for d in [10, 12, 16, 20, 25, 32]:
            self.lap_lengths[d] = d * 50 / 1000  # meters
            self.development_lengths[d] = d * 40 / 1000  # meters
        
        # Standard covers
        self.concrete_cover = {
            'DECK_SLAB': 0.030,
            'GIRDER': 0.040,
            'PIER': 0.050,
            'FOUNDATION': 0.075
        }
    
    def add_element_schedule(self, schedule: ElementSchedule):
        """Add an element schedule"""
        self.element_schedules.append(schedule)
    
    def get_grand_total(self) -> Dict:
        """Get grand total for entire bridge"""
        total_weight = sum(es.total_weight for es in self.element_schedules)
        total_bars = sum(es.total_bars for es in self.element_schedules)
        
        # Group by diameter across all elements
        all_bars = []
        for es in self.element_schedules:
            all_bars.extend(es.bars)
        
        by_diameter = {}
        for bar in all_bars:
            d = bar.diameter
            if d not in by_diameter:
                by_diameter[d] = {
                    'count': 0,
                    'total_length': 0.0,
                    'total_weight': 0.0
                }
            
            by_diameter[d]['count'] += bar.number_of_bars
            by_diameter[d]['total_length'] += bar.cutting_length * bar.number_of_bars
            by_diameter[d]['total_weight'] += bar.total_weight
        
        # Add wastage (typically 5%)
        wastage = total_weight * 0.05
        
        return {
            'total_bars': total_bars,
            'total_weight_kg': total_weight,
            'total_weight_mt': total_weight / 1000,
            'wastage_kg': wastage,
            'wastage_mt': wastage / 1000,
            'total_with_wastage_kg': total_weight + wastage,
            'total_with_wastage_mt': (total_weight + wastage) / 1000,
            'by_diameter': by_diameter
        }
    
    def get_procurement_summary(self) -> Dict:
        """Get procurement summary by bar diameter and length"""
        grand_total = self.get_grand_total()
        
        procurement = {}
        
        for d, data in grand_total['by_diameter'].items():
            # Standard bar lengths available (12m typical)
            std_length = 12.0
            
            # Calculate number of standard bars needed
            total_length = data['total_length']
            num_std_bars = math.ceil(total_length / std_length)
            
            procurement[d] = {
                'diameter_mm': d,
                'unit_weight_kg_m': (d ** 2) / 162.28,
                'total_length_m': total_length,
                'number_of_12m_bars': num_std_bars,
                'total_weight_kg': data['total_weight'],
                'total_weight_mt': data['total_weight'] / 1000
            }
        
        return procurement
    
    def export_to_excel(self, filepath: str):
        """Export complete schedule to Excel"""
        import pandas as pd
        
        with pd.ExcelWriter(filepath, engine='openpyxl') as writer:
            # Sheet 1: Summary
            summary_data = {
                'Project': [self.project_name],
                'Bridge': [self.bridge_name],
                'Date': [self.date],
                'Total Elements': [len(self.element_schedules)],
                'Total Weight (kg)': [self.get_grand_total()['total_weight_kg']],
                'Total Weight (MT)': [self.get_grand_total()['total_weight_mt']],
                'Wastage 5% (MT)': [self.get_grand_total()['wastage_mt']],
                'Total with Wastage (MT)': [self.get_grand_total()['total_with_wastage_mt']]
            }
            pd.DataFrame(summary_data).to_excel(writer, sheet_name='SUMMARY', index=False)
            
            # Sheet 2: Bar Bending Schedule
            bbs_data = []
            for es in self.element_schedules:
                for bar in es.bars:
                    bbs_data.append({
                        'Element': f"{es.element_type}-{es.element_number}",
                        'Bar Mark': bar.bar_mark,
                        'Description': bar.description,
                        'Shape': bar.shape.value,
                        'Dia (mm)': bar.diameter,
                        'Grade': bar.grade.value,
                        'Dimensions (mm)': bar.get_bending_dimensions(),
                        'Cutting Length (m)': round(bar.cutting_length, 3),
                        'No. of Bars': bar.number_of_bars,
                        'Total Length (m)': round(bar.cutting_length * bar.number_of_bars, 2),
                        'Unit Wt (kg/m)': round(bar.unit_weight, 3),
                        'Weight per Bar (kg)': round(bar.weight_per_bar, 2),
                        'Total Weight (kg)': round(bar.total_weight, 2)
                    })
            
            pd.DataFrame(bbs_data).to_excel(writer, sheet_name='BBS', index=False)
            
            # Sheet 3: By Diameter Summary
            grand_total = self.get_grand_total()
            dia_summary = []
            for d, data in grand_total['by_diameter'].items():
                dia_summary.append({
                    'Diameter (mm)': d,
                    'Number of Bars': data['count'],
                    'Total Length (m)': round(data['total_length'], 2),
                    'Total Weight (kg)': round(data['total_weight'], 2),
                    'Total Weight (MT)': round(data['total_weight'] / 1000, 3)
                })
            
            pd.DataFrame(dia_summary).to_excel(writer, sheet_name='BY_DIAMETER', index=False)
            
            # Sheet 4: Procurement
            procurement = self.get_procurement_summary()
            proc_data = []
            for d, data in procurement.items():
                proc_data.append({
                    'Diameter (mm)': data['diameter_mm'],
                    'Unit Weight (kg/m)': round(data['unit_weight_kg_m'], 3),
                    'Total Length Required (m)': round(data['total_length_m'], 2),
                    '12m Bars Required': data['number_of_12m_bars'],
                    'Total Weight (kg)': round(data['total_weight_kg'], 2),
                    'Total Weight (MT)': round(data['total_weight_mt'], 3)
                })
            
            pd.DataFrame(proc_data).to_excel(writer, sheet_name='PROCUREMENT', index=False)
            
            # Sheet 5: Element Summary
            elem_summary = []
            for es in self.element_schedules:
                elem_summary.append({
                    'Element Type': es.element_type,
                    'Number': es.element_number,
                    'Name': es.element_name,
                    'Total Bars': es.total_bars,
                    'Total Weight (kg)': round(es.total_weight, 2)
                })
            
            pd.DataFrame(elem_summary).to_excel(writer, sheet_name='ELEMENTS', index=False)
        
        print(f"Reinforcement schedule exported: {filepath}")
        return filepath
    
    def export_to_csv(self, filepath: str):
        """Export bar bending schedule to CSV"""
        import csv
        
        with open(filepath, 'w', newline='') as f:
            writer = csv.writer(f)
            
            # Header
            writer.writerow([
                'Element', 'Bar Mark', 'Description', 'Shape', 
                'Dia (mm)', 'Grade', 'Dimensions (mm)',
                'Cutting Length (m)', 'No. of Bars',
                'Total Length (m)', 'Unit Wt (kg/m)',
                'Weight per Bar (kg)', 'Total Weight (kg)'
            ])
            
            # Data
            for es in self.element_schedules:
                for bar in es.bars:
                    writer.writerow([
                        f"{es.element_type}-{es.element_number}",
                        bar.bar_mark,
                        bar.description,
                        bar.shape.value,
                        bar.diameter,
                        bar.grade.value,
                        bar.get_bending_dimensions(),
                        round(bar.cutting_length, 3),
                        bar.number_of_bars,
                        round(bar.cutting_length * bar.number_of_bars, 2),
                        round(bar.unit_weight, 3),
                        round(bar.weight_per_bar, 2),
                        round(bar.total_weight, 2)
                    ])
        
        print(f"CSV exported: {filepath}")
        return filepath


class ReinforcementCalculator:
    """Calculate reinforcement for various bridge elements"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def calculate_deck_slab_reinforcement(
        length: float,
        width: float,
        thickness: float,
        main_bar_dia: int = 16,
        main_bar_spacing: float = 0.15,
        dist_bar_dia: int = 12,
        dist_bar_spacing: float = 0.20,
        skew_angle: float = 0
    ) -> ElementSchedule:
        """
        Calculate deck slab reinforcement
        For skew bridges, main bars are perpendicular to bridge axis
        """
        schedule = ElementSchedule(
            element_type="DECK_SLAB",
            element_number=1,
            element_name=f"Deck Slab {length:.1f}m × {width:.1f}m"
        )
        
        # Main bars (perpendicular to traffic/perpendicular to bridge axis)
        # For skew bridges, length increases by 1/cos(skew)
        skew_factor = 1 / math.cos(math.radians(skew_angle)) if skew_angle else 1
        
        num_main_bars = int(width / main_bar_spacing) + 1
        main_bar_length = length * skew_factor + 0.5  # Add development length
        
        # Bar 1: Main bars bottom
        bar1 = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=1,
            bar_mark="D1",
            description="Main bars - bottom layer",
            shape=BarShape.STRAIGHT,
            diameter=main_bar_dia,
            grade=BarGrade.FE500,
            dimensions=BarDimension(A=main_bar_length),
            number_of_bars=num_main_bars
        )
        schedule.bars.append(bar1)
        
        # Bar 2: Main bars top (at supports)
        num_top_bars = int(num_main_bars * 0.5)  # 50% at top
        bar2 = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=2,
            bar_mark="D2",
            description="Main bars - top at supports",
            shape=BarShape.STRAIGHT,
            diameter=main_bar_dia,
            grade=BarGrade.FE500,
            dimensions=BarDimension(A=main_bar_length * 0.4),  # 40% length for top bars
            number_of_bars=num_top_bars
        )
        schedule.bars.append(bar2)
        
        # Bar 3: Distribution bars
        num_dist_bars = int(length / dist_bar_spacing) + 1
        dist_bar_length = width + 0.3  # Add for laps
        
        bar3 = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=3,
            bar_mark="D3",
            description="Distribution bars",
            shape=BarShape.STRAIGHT,
            diameter=dist_bar_dia,
            grade=BarGrade.FE500,
            dimensions=BarDimension(A=dist_bar_length),
            number_of_bars=num_dist_bars
        )
        schedule.bars.append(bar3)
        
        return schedule
    
    @staticmethod
    def calculate_tbeam_reinforcement(
        span_length: float,
        girder_spacing: float,
        web_width: float,
        total_depth: float,
        num_girders: int = 5
    ) -> ElementSchedule:
        """Calculate T-beam reinforcement"""
        schedule = ElementSchedule(
            element_type="T_BEAM_GIRDER",
            element_number=1,
            element_name=f"T-Beam Girder {span_length:.1f}m span"
        )
        
        # Main longitudinal bars
        # Bottom bars (sagging moment)
        num_bottom_bars = 8
        bottom_bar_length = span_length + 1.0  # Add development
        
        bar1 = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=1,
            bar_mark="G1",
            description="Main bars - bottom",
            shape=BarShape.STRAIGHT,
            diameter=25,
            grade=BarGrade.FE500,
            dimensions=BarDimension(A=bottom_bar_length),
            number_of_bars=num_bottom_bars * num_girders
        )
        schedule.bars.append(bar1)
        
        # Top bars (hogging at supports)
        num_top_bars = 4
        top_bar_length = span_length * 0.25  # 25% at each support
        
        bar2 = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=2,
            bar_mark="G2",
            description="Main bars - top at supports",
            shape=BarShape.STRAIGHT,
            diameter=20,
            grade=BarGrade.FE500,
            dimensions=BarDimension(A=top_bar_length),
            number_of_bars=num_top_bars * num_girders * 2  # Both ends
        )
        schedule.bars.append(bar2)
        
        # Stirrups
        stirrup_width = web_width - 0.08  # 40mm cover each side
        stirrup_height = total_depth - 0.08
        
        num_stirrups = int(span_length / 0.15) + 1
        
        bar3 = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=3,
            bar_mark="G3",
            description="Stirrups",
            shape=BarShape.STIRRUP,
            diameter=10,
            grade=BarGrade.FE415,
            dimensions=BarDimension(A=stirrup_width, B=stirrup_height),
            number_of_bars=num_stirrups * num_girders
        )
        schedule.bars.append(bar3)
        
        # Side face reinforcement (for deep beams)
        if total_depth > 0.75:
            side_bar_length = span_length + 0.5
            num_side_bars = 2  # Each face
            
            bar4 = ReinforcementBar(
                bar_number=4,
                bar_mark="G4",
                description="Side face reinforcement",
                shape=BarShape.STRAIGHT,
                diameter=12,
                grade=BarGrade.FE500,
                dimensions=BarDimension(A=side_bar_length),
                number_of_bars=num_side_bars * 2 * num_girders  # Both faces
            )
            schedule.bars.append(bar4)
        
        return schedule
    
    @staticmethod
    def calculate_pier_reinforcement(
        pier_height: float,
        pier_width: float,
        pier_length: float,
        num_piers: int = 3
    ) -> ElementSchedule:
        """Calculate pier reinforcement"""
        schedule = ElementSchedule(
            element_type="PIER",
            element_number=1,
            element_name=f"Pier {pier_height:.1f}m high"
        )
        
        # Vertical bars
        num_vertical = 24  # Total around perimeter
        vertical_bar_length = pier_height + 1.0  # Lap with foundation
        
        bar1 = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=1,
            bar_mark="P1",
            description="Vertical bars",
            shape=BarShape.STRAIGHT,
            diameter=25,
            grade=BarGrade.FE500,
            dimensions=BarDimension(A=vertical_bar_length),
            number_of_bars=num_vertical * num_piers
        )
        schedule.bars.append(bar1)
        
        # Horizontal ties
        tie_perimeter = 2 * (pier_width + pier_length) - 0.16  # Inside cover
        
        num_ties = int(pier_height / 0.15) + 1
        
        bar2 = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=2,
            bar_mark="P2",
            description="Horizontal ties",
            shape=BarShape.STIRRUP,
            diameter=12,
            grade=BarGrade.FE415,
            dimensions=BarDimension(A=pier_length-0.08, B=pier_width-0.08),
            number_of_bars=num_ties * num_piers
        )
        schedule.bars.append(bar2)
        
        return schedule
    
    @staticmethod
    def calculate_pile_reinforcement(
        pile_diameter: float,
        pile_length: float,
        num_piles: int = 9
    ) -> ElementSchedule:
        """Calculate pile reinforcement"""
        schedule = ElementSchedule(
            element_type="PILE",
            element_number=1,
            element_name=f"Pile Ø{pile_diameter*1000:.0f}mm"
        )
        
        # Main bars
        num_main_bars = 12
        main_bar_length = pile_length + 0.5  # Projection into cap
        
        bar1 = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=1,
            bar_mark="PL1",
            description="Main vertical bars",
            shape=BarShape.STRAIGHT,
            diameter=20,
            grade=BarGrade.FE500,
            dimensions=BarDimension(A=main_bar_length),
            number_of_bars=num_main_bars * num_piles
        )
        schedule.bars.append(bar1)
        
        # Spiral reinforcement
        spiral_diameter = pile_diameter - 0.15  # 75mm cover
        num_turns = int(pile_length / 0.15)
        
        bar2 = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=2,
            bar_mark="PL2",
            description="Spiral reinforcement",
            shape=BarShape.SPIRAL,
            diameter=10,
            grade=BarGrade.FE415,
            dimensions=BarDimension(A=spiral_diameter, B=0.15, C=num_turns),
            number_of_bars=num_piles
        )
        schedule.bars.append(bar2)
        
        return schedule
    
    @staticmethod
    def calculate_pile_cap_reinforcement(
        cap_length: float,
        cap_width: float,
        cap_thickness: float
    ) -> ElementSchedule:
        """Calculate pile cap reinforcement"""
        schedule = ElementSchedule(
            element_type="PILE_CAP",
            element_number=1,
            element_name=f"Pile Cap {cap_length:.1f}m × {cap_width:.1f}m"
        )
        
        # Bottom mesh
        num_bottom_bars_long = int(cap_width / 0.15) + 1
        bottom_bar_length = cap_length + 0.3
        
        bar1 = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=1,
            bar_mark="PC1",
            description="Bottom mesh - longitudinal",
            shape=BarShape.STRAIGHT,
            diameter=25,
            grade=BarGrade.FE500,
            dimensions=BarDimension(A=bottom_bar_length),
            number_of_bars=num_bottom_bars_long
        )
        schedule.bars.append(bar1)
        
        num_bottom_bars_trans = int(cap_length / 0.15) + 1
        bottom_bar_length_trans = cap_width + 0.3
        
        bar2 = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=2,
            bar_mark="PC2",
            description="Bottom mesh - transverse",
            shape=BarShape.STRAIGHT,
            diameter=25,
            grade=BarGrade.FE500,
            dimensions=BarDimension(A=bottom_bar_length_trans),
            number_of_bars=num_bottom_bars_trans
        )
        schedule.bars.append(bar2)
        
        # Top mesh
        num_top_bars = int(cap_width / 0.20) + 1
        
        bar3 = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=3,
            bar_mark="PC3",
            description="Top mesh",
            shape=BarShape.STRAIGHT,
            diameter=20,
            grade=BarGrade.FE500,
            dimensions=BarDimension(A=cap_length + 0.3),
            number_of_bars=num_top_bars
        )
        schedule.bars.append(bar3)
        
        return schedule
```

---

### File 2: `reinforcement_report.py` - Report Generation

```python
"""
Reinforcement Report Generator
Creates formatted reports and drawings of reinforcement schedules
"""

import ezdxf
from ezdxf.enums import TextEntityAlignment
from typing import List, Dict
from reinforcement_core import (
    BridgeReinforcementSchedule, ReinforcementBar, ElementSchedule,
    BarShape
)


class ReinforcementReportDrawing:
    """
    Generate reinforcement schedule drawing in DXF format
    """
    
    def __init__(self, schedule: BridgeReinforcementSchedule):
        self.schedule = schedule
        
        self.doc = ezdxf.new('R2018', setup=True)
        self.msp = self.doc.modelspace()
        self.setup_styles()
    
    def setup_styles(self):
        """Setup text styles"""
        self.doc.styles.add('REPORT_FONT', font='Arial.ttf')
    
    def generate_schedule_drawing(self, output_path: str = "reinforcement_schedule.dxf"):
        """Generate bar bending schedule drawing"""
        y_position = 0
        row_height = 8
        
        # Title
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"BAR BENDING SCHEDULE - {self.schedule.bridge_name}",
            height=5.0,
            dxfattribs={'style': 'REPORT_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            (100, y_position + 10),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        y_position += 20
        
        # Headers
        headers = ['Bar\nMark', 'Description', 'Shape', 'Dia\n(mm)', 
                  'Dimensions\n(mm)', 'Cutting\nLength (m)', 'No. of\nBars',
                  'Total\nLength (m)', 'Unit Wt\n(kg/m)', 'Total Wt\n(kg)']
        
        col_widths = [12, 35, 15, 10, 20, 15, 12, 15, 12, 15]
        x_positions = [0]
        for w in col_widths[:-1]:
            x_positions.append(x_positions[-1] + w)
        
        # Draw header row
        self._draw_table_row(y_position, headers, x_positions, col_widths, 
                           is_header=True)
        
        y_position += row_height
        
        # Draw data rows
        for es in self.schedule.element_schedules:
            # Element header
            self.msp.add_text(
                f"=== {es.element_type} #{es.element_number}: {es.element_name} ===",
                height=3.0,
                dxfattribs={'style': 'REPORT_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(
                (sum(col_widths)/2, y_position + 3),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
            y_position += row_height
            
            for bar in es.bars:
                row_data = [
                    bar.bar_mark,
                    bar.description[:30],
                    bar.shape.value,
                    str(bar.diameter),
                    bar.get_bending_dimensions(),
                    f"{bar.cutting_length:.3f}",
                    str(bar.number_of_bars),
                    f"{bar.cutting_length * bar.number_of_bars:.2f}",
                    f"{bar.unit_weight:.3f}",
                    f"{bar.total_weight:.2f}"
                ]
                
                self._draw_table_row(y_position, row_data, x_positions, 
                                   col_widths, is_header=False)
                y_position += row_height
        
        # Summary
        y_position += 10
        grand_total = self.schedule.get_grand_total()
        
        summary_text = [
            f"TOTAL WEIGHT: {grand_total['total_weight_mt']:.3f} MT",
            f"WASTAGE (5%): {grand_total['wastage_mt']:.3f} MT",
            f"TOTAL WITH WASTAGE: {grand_total['total_with_wastage_mt']:.3f} MT"
        ]
        
        for i, text in enumerate(summary_text):
            self.msp.add_text(
                text,
                height=4.0,
                dxfattribs={'style': 'REPORT_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(
                (100, y_position - i * 6),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
        
        self.doc.saveas(output_path)
        print(f"Reinforcement schedule drawing saved: {output_path}")
        return output_path
    
    def _draw_table_row(self, y: float, data: List[str], x_positions: List[float],
                       col_widths: List[float], is_header: bool = False):
        """Draw a table row"""
        height = 3.0 if is_header else 2.5
        
        # Draw cells
        for i, (text, x, w) in enumerate(zip(data, x_positions, col_widths)):
            self.msp.add_text(
                text,
                height=height,
                dxfattribs={'style': 'REPORT_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(
                (x + w/2, y),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
            
            # Cell border
            border = [
                (x, y - 3),
                (x + w, y - 3),
                (x + w, y + 3),
                (x, y + 3),
                (x, y - 3)
            ]
            self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
                points=border,
                close=True,
                dxfattribs={'color': 7}
            )
    
    def generate_bar_shapes_drawing(self, output_path: str = "bar_shapes.dxf"):
        """Generate drawing showing all bar shapes"""
        x_offset = 0
        y_offset = 0
        spacing_x = 50
        spacing_y = 40
        
        shapes_to_draw = set()
        for es in self.schedule.element_schedules:
            for bar in es.bars:
                shapes_to_draw.add((bar.shape, bar.diameter, bar.dimensions))
        
        for i, (shape, dia, dims) in enumerate(shapes_to_draw):
            x = x_offset + (i % 4) * spacing_x
            y = y_offset - (i // 4) * spacing_y
            
            self._draw_bar_shape(x, y, shape, dia, dims)
        
        self.doc.saveas(output_path)
        print(f"Bar shapes drawing saved: {output_path}")
        return output_path
    
    def _draw_bar_shape(self, x: float, y: float, shape: BarShape, 
                       diameter: int, dims):
        """Draw a single bar shape"""
        scale = 5.0
        
        # Draw shape
        if shape == BarShape.STRAIGHT:
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=(x, y),
                end=(x + dims.A * scale, y),
                dxfattribs={'color': 2, 'lineweight': 0.35}
            )
            # Dimension
            self.msp.add_text(
                f"{dims.A*1000:.0f}",
                height=2.5,
                dxfattribs={'style': 'REPORT_FONT'}
            ).set_placement(
                (x + dims.A * scale / 2, y + 3),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
        
        elif shape == BarShape.L_SHAPE:
            self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
                points=[(x, y), (x, y + dims.B * scale), 
                       (x + dims.A * scale, y + dims.B * scale)],
                close=False,
                dxfattribs={'color': 2, 'lineweight': 0.35}
            )
        
        elif shape == BarShape.STIRRUP:
            width = dims.A * scale
            height = dims.B * scale
            self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
                points=[(x, y), (x + width, y), (x + width, y + height),
                       (x, y + height), (x, y)],
                close=True,
                dxfattribs={'color': 2, 'lineweight': 0.35}
            )
        
        # Shape label
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"Shape {shape.value} - Ø{diameter}",
            height=2.5,
            dxfattribs={'style': 'REPORT_FONT'}
        ).set_placement(
            (x + dims.A * scale / 2 if hasattr(dims, 'A') else x, y - 5),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )


def print_text_report(schedule: BridgeReinforcementSchedule):
    """Print formatted text report to console"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 100)
    print(f"REINFORCEMENT SCHEDULE - {schedule.bridge_name}")
    print(f"Project: {schedule.project_name}")
    print(f"Date: {schedule.date}")
    print("=" * 100)
    
    for es in schedule.element_schedules:
        print(f"\n{'─' * 100}")
        print(f"ELEMENT: {es.element_type} #{es.element_number} - {es.element_name}")
        print(f"{'─' * 100}")
        
        print(f"{'Bar':<6} {'Description':<30} {'Shape':<8} {'Dia':<6} "
              f"{'Dimensions':<15} {'Length':<10} {'Qty':<8} {'Weight (kg)':<12}")
        print("-" * 100)
        
        for bar in es.bars:
            print(f"{bar.bar_mark:<6} {bar.description:<30} {bar.shape.value:<8} "
                  f"{bar.diameter:<6} {bar.get_bending_dimensions():<15} "
                  f"{bar.cutting_length:<10.3f} {bar.number_of_bars:<8} "
                  f"{bar.total_weight:<12.2f}")
        
        print(f"\nElement Total: {es.total_bars} bars, {es.total_weight:.2f} kg "
              f"({es.total_weight/1000:.3f} MT)")
    
    print(f"\n{'=' * 100}")
    grand_total = schedule.get_grand_total()
    print("GRAND TOTAL")
    print(f"{'=' * 100}")
    print(f"Total Bars: {grand_total['total_bars']}")
    print(f"Total Weight: {grand_total['total_weight_kg']:.2f} kg "
          f"({grand_total['total_weight_mt']:.3f} MT)")
    print(f"Wastage (5%): {grand_total['wastage_mt']:.3f} MT")
    print(f"Total with Wastage: {grand_total['total_with_wastage_mt']:.3f} MT")
    
    print(f"\n{'─' * 100}")
    print("BY DIAMETER SUMMARY")
    print(f"{'─' * 100}")
    for d, data in sorted(grand_total['by_diameter'].items()):
        print(f"Ø{d}mm: {data['count']} bars, "
              f"{data['total_length']:.2f}m, "
              f"{data['total_weight']/1000:.3f} MT")
    
    print(f"\n{'─' * 100}")
    print("PROCUREMENT SUMMARY")
    print(f"{'─' * 100}")
    procurement = schedule.get_procurement_summary()
    for d, data in sorted(procurement.items()):
        print(f"Ø{d}mm: {data['number_of_12m_bars']} bars of 12m, "
              f"{data['total_weight_mt']:.3f} MT")
    
    print("=" * 100)
```

### File 3: `example_reinforcement.py` - Complete Example

```python
"""
Example: Generate complete reinforcement schedule for Chambakkara Bridge
"""

from reinforcement_core import (
    BridgeReinforcementSchedule, ReinforcementCalculator,
    BarGrade, BarShape, BarDimension, ReinforcementBar, ElementSchedule
)
from reinforcement_report import ReinforcementReportDrawing, print_text_report


def create_chambakkara_reinforcement_schedule():
    """Create complete reinforcement schedule for Chambakkara Bridge"""
    
    print("=" * 100)
    print("CHAMBAKKARA BRIDGE - REINFORCEMENT SCHEDULE")
    print("=" * 100)
    
    # Create schedule
    schedule = BridgeReinforcementSchedule(
        project_name="NH-353C Bridge Project",
        bridge_name="Chambakkara Bridge"
    )
    
    # 1. Deck Slab Reinforcement
    print("\n1. Calculating Deck Slab Reinforcement...")
    deck_schedule = ReinforcementCalculator.calculate_deck_slab_reinforcement(
        length=80.0,  # Total bridge length
        width=12.0,   # Deck width
        thickness=0.25,
        main_bar_dia=16,
        main_bar_spacing=0.15,
        dist_bar_dia=12,
        dist_bar_spacing=0.20,
        skew_angle=15.0
    )
    schedule.add_element_schedule(deck_schedule)
    print(f"   Deck: {deck_schedule.total_weight:.2f} kg")
    
    # 2. T-Beam Girder Reinforcement
    print("\n2. Calculating T-Beam Reinforcement...")
    girder_schedule = ReinforcementCalculator.calculate_tbeam_reinforcement(
        span_length=30.0,  # Longest span
        girder_spacing=3.0,
        web_width=0.30,
        total_depth=2.0,
        num_girders=5
    )
    # Multiply by number of spans (3)
    for bar in girder_schedule.bars:
        bar.number_of_bars *= 3
    
    girder_schedule.element_name = "T-Beam Girders (3 spans)"
    schedule.add_element_schedule(girder_schedule)
    print(f"   Girders: {girder_schedule.total_weight:.2f} kg")
    
    # 3. Pier Reinforcement
    print("\n3. Calculating Pier Reinforcement...")
    pier_schedule = ReinforcementCalculator.calculate_pier_reinforcement(
        pier_height=10.0,
        pier_width=2.0,
        pier_length=10.0,
        num_piers=2  # 2 intermediate piers
    )
    schedule.add_element_schedule(pier_schedule)
    print(f"   Piers: {pier_schedule.total_weight:.2f} kg")
    
    # 4. Abutment Reinforcement
    print("\n4. Calculating Abutment Reinforcement...")
    abut_schedule = ReinforcementCalculator.calculate_pier_reinforcement(
        pier_height=8.0,
        pier_width=1.5,
        pier_length=11.0,
        num_piers=2  # 2 abutments
    )
    abut_schedule.element_type = "ABUTMENT"
    abut_schedule.element_name = "Abutments (2 nos)"
    schedule.add_element_schedule(abut_schedule)
    print(f"   Abutments: {abut_schedule.total_weight:.2f} kg")
    
    # 5. Pile Foundation Reinforcement
    print("\n5. Calculating Pile Foundation Reinforcement...")
    pile_schedule = ReinforcementCalculator.calculate_pile_reinforcement(
        pile_diameter=1.2,
        pile_length=15.0,
        num_piles=27  # 3 piers × 9 piles each
    )
    schedule.add_element_schedule(pile_schedule)
    print(f"   Piles: {pile_schedule.total_weight:.2f} kg")
    
    # 6. Pile Cap Reinforcement
    print("\n6. Calculating Pile Cap Reinforcement...")
    cap_schedule = ReinforcementCalculator.calculate_pile_cap_reinforcement(
        cap_length=8.0,
        cap_width=8.0,
        cap_thickness=2.0
    )
    # 3 pile caps
    for bar in cap_schedule.bars:
        bar.number_of_bars *= 3
    
    cap_schedule.element_name = "Pile Caps (3 nos)"
    schedule.add_element_schedule(cap_schedule)
    print(f"   Pile Caps: {cap_schedule.total_weight:.2f} kg")
    
    # Print text report
    print_text_report(schedule)
    
    # Export to Excel
    print("\nExporting to Excel...")
    schedule.export_to_excel("chambakkara_reinforcement_schedule.xlsx")
    
    # Export to CSV
    print("Exporting to CSV...")
    schedule.export_to_csv("chambakkara_reinforcement_schedule.csv")
    
    # Generate DXF drawings
    print("Generating DXF drawings...")
    report_drawing = ReinforcementReportDrawing(schedule)
    report_drawing.generate_schedule_drawing("chambakkara_reinforcement_schedule.dxf")
    report_drawing.generate_bar_shapes_drawing("chambakkara_bar_shapes.dxf")
    
    print("\n" + "=" * 100)
    print("REINFORCEMENT SCHEDULE COMPLETE")
    print("=" * 100)
    print("\nGenerated files:")
    print("  - chambakkara_reinforcement_schedule.xlsx")
    print("  - chambakkara_reinforcement_schedule.csv")
    print("  - chambakkara_reinforcement_schedule.dxf")
    print("  - chambakkara_bar_shapes.dxf")
    
    return schedule


def create_sample_bar_shapes():
    """Create sample showing all bar shapes"""
    schedule = BridgeReinforcementSchedule(
        project_name="Sample",
        bridge_name="Bar Shapes Reference"
    )
    
    es = ElementSchedule(
        element_type="REFERENCE",
        element_number=1,
        element_name="Standard Bar Shapes"
    )
    
    # All standard shapes
    shapes = [
        (BarShape.STRAIGHT, 16, BarDimension(A=6.0), "Straight"),
        (BarShape.L_SHAPE, 16, BarDimension(A=3.0, B=2.0), "L-Shape"),
        (BarShape.U_SHAPE, 16, BarDimension(A=2.0, B=1.0, C=2.0), "U-Shape"),
        (BarShape.STIRRUP, 10, BarDimension(A=0.35, B=1.5), "Stirrup"),
        (BarShape.CIRCULAR, 12, BarDimension(A=1.5), "Ring"),
        (BarShape.CRANKED, 20, BarDimension(A=4.0, B=0.3, C=4.0), "Cranked"),
    ]
    
    for i, (shape, dia, dims, desc) in enumerate(shapes, 1):
        bar = ReinforcementBar(
            bar_number=i,
            bar_mark=f"S{i}",
            description=desc,
            shape=shape,
            diameter=dia,
            grade=BarGrade.FE500,
            dimensions=dims,
            number_of_bars=1
        )
        es.bars.append(bar)
    
    schedule.add_element_schedule(es)
    
    # Generate drawing
    report = ReinforcementReportDrawing(schedule)
    report.generate_bar_shapes_drawing("bar_shapes_reference.dxf")
    
    return schedule


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Main schedule
    schedule = create_chambakkara_reinforcement_schedule()
    
    # Bar shapes reference
    print("\n" + "=" * 100)
    print("Creating bar shapes reference...")
    print("=" * 100)
    shapes = create_sample_bar_shapes()
```

|---------|-------------|
| **Bar Shapes** | 10 standard shapes per IS 2502/BS 8666 |
| **Cutting Length** | Automatic deduction for bends |
| **Lap Lengths** | Configurable by diameter |
| **Weight Calculation** | d²/162.28 formula |
| **Wastage** | Automatic 5% addition |
| **Excel Export** | Multi-sheet workbook |
| **CSV Export** | For import into other systems |
| **DXF Drawing** | Visual schedule and bar shapes |
| **Procurement** | 12m bar quantities |

## INTEGRATION WITH FULL SYSTEM

```python
from skew_bridge_engine import create_skew_bridge
from reinforcement_core import (
    BridgeReinforcementSchedule, 
    ReinforcementCalculator
)

# Create bridge
bridge = create_skew_bridge(
    name="Chambakkara Bridge",
    chainage=600.0,
    span_lengths=[25, 30, 25],
    deck_width=12.0,
    skew_angle=15.0
)

# Generate complete reinforcement schedule
schedule = BridgeReinforcementSchedule(
    project_name="NH-353C",
    bridge_name=bridge.name
)

# Calculate reinforcement for each element
for span in bridge.spans:
    # Deck slab
    deck = ReinforcementCalculator.calculate_deck_slab_reinforcement(
        length=span.length,
        width=bridge.deck.square_width,
        skew_angle=bridge.skew.skew_angle_deg
    )
    schedule.add_element_schedule(deck)
    
    # Girders
    girders = ReinforcementCalculator.calculate_tbeam_reinforcement(
        span_length=span.length,
        num_girders=bridge.cross_section.number_of_girders
    )
    schedule.add_element_schedule(girders)

# Export
schedule.export_to_excel("reinforcement.xlsx")
```

# MULTI-BRIDGE BATCH PROCESSOR MODULE

## SYSTEM ARCHITECTURE

```
BATCH PROCESSING SYSTEM
=======================

Input: bridges_master.xlsx
├── Sheet: BRIDGE_LIST (100+ bridges)
├── Sheet: STANDARD_COMPONENTS (reusable elements)
├── Sheet: TEMPLATES (typical configurations)
└── Sheet: OUTPUT_SETTINGS

Processing Pipeline:
┌─────────────┐    ┌─────────────┐    ┌─────────────┐    ┌─────────────┐
│   READ      │ →  │  VALIDATE   │ →  │  GENERATE   │ →  │   OUTPUT    │
│   EXCEL     │    │   BRIDGE    │    │   DRAWINGS  │    │   REPORT    │
└─────────────┘    └─────────────┘    └─────────────┘    └─────────────┘
       │                  │                  │                  │
       ↓                  ↓                  ↓                  ↓
   Parse rows        Check errors      Parallel workers    Organized files
   Create objects    Skip/fix bad      (4-8 threads)       Summary Excel
                                          ↓
                                    ┌─────────────┐
                                    │   TYPES     │
                                    │  Plan View  │
                                    │  Long. Sec. │
                                    │  Cross Sec. │
                                    │  Foundation │
                                    │  Reinf.     │
                                    └─────────────┘
```

## COMPLETE PYTHON MODULE

### File 1: `batch_processor_core.py` - Core Processing Engine

```python
"""
Multi-Bridge Batch Processor Core
Handles batch processing of multiple bridges from Excel input
"""

import os
import sys
import time
import json
import logging
import pandas as pd
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Tuple, Callable, Any
from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
import traceback
import shutil


class ProcessingStatus(Enum):
    """Status of bridge processing"""
    PENDING = "PENDING"
    VALIDATING = "VALIDATING"
    PROCESSING = "PROCESSING"
    COMPLETED = "COMPLETED"
    FAILED = "FAILED"
    SKIPPED = "SKIPPED"


@dataclass
class BridgeInputData:
    """Bridge data from Excel row"""
    # Identification
    bridge_id: str
    bridge_name: str
    chainage: float
    
    # Geometry
    span_lengths: List[float]
    deck_width: float
    skew_angle: float
    skew_direction: str = "RIGHT"
    
    # Superstructure
    superstructure_type: str = "T-BEAM"
    deck_thickness: float = 0.25
    wearing_coat: float = 0.065
    
    # Substructure
    pier_type: str = "WALL"
    pier_width: float = 2.0
    pier_height: float = 10.0
    abutment_type: str = "CANTILEVER"
    
    # Foundation
    foundation_type: str = "PILE"
    pile_diameter: float = 1.2
    pile_length: float = 15.0
    num_piles_long: int = 3
    num_piles_trans: int = 3
    
    # Output control
    generate_plan: bool = True
    generate_long_section: bool = True
    generate_cross_section: bool = True
    generate_foundation: bool = True
    generate_reinforcement: bool = True
    
    # Status
    status: ProcessingStatus = ProcessingStatus.PENDING
    error_message: str = ""
    processing_time: float = 0.0
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
        """Convert to dictionary"""
        data = asdict(self)
        data['status'] = self.status.value
        data['span_lengths'] = json.dumps(self.span_lengths)
        return data
    
    @classmethod
    def from_excel_row(cls, row: pd.Series, column_mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> 'BridgeInputData':
        """Create from Excel row with column mapping"""
        def get_value(col_name, default=None):
            mapped_col = column_mapping.get(col_name, col_name)
            return row.get(mapped_col, default)
        
        # Parse span lengths (can be comma-separated or multiple columns)
        span_lengths_str = get_value('span_lengths', '30')
        if isinstance(span_lengths_str, str):
            span_lengths = [float(x.strip()) for x in span_lengths_str.split(',')]
        else:
            span_lengths = [float(span_lengths_str)]
        
        return cls(
            bridge_id=str(get_value('bridge_id', 'BRG-001')),
            bridge_name=str(get_value('bridge_name', 'Bridge')),
            chainage=float(get_value('chainage', 0)),
            span_lengths=span_lengths,
            deck_width=float(get_value('deck_width', 12.0)),
            skew_angle=float(get_value('skew_angle', 0)),
            skew_direction=str(get_value('skew_direction', 'RIGHT')),
            superstructure_type=str(get_value('superstructure_type', 'T-BEAM')),
            deck_thickness=float(get_value('deck_thickness', 0.25)),
            wearing_coat=float(get_value('wearing_coat', 0.065)),
            pier_type=str(get_value('pier_type', 'WALL')),
            pier_width=float(get_value('pier_width', 2.0)),
            pier_height=float(get_value('pier_height', 10.0)),
            abutment_type=str(get_value('abutment_type', 'CANTILEVER')),
            foundation_type=str(get_value('foundation_type', 'PILE')),
            pile_diameter=float(get_value('pile_diameter', 1.2)),
            pile_length=float(get_value('pile_length', 15.0)),
            num_piles_long=int(get_value('num_piles_long', 3)),
            num_piles_trans=int(get_value('num_piles_trans', 3)),
            generate_plan=bool(get_value('generate_plan', True)),
            generate_long_section=bool(get_value('generate_long_section', True)),
            generate_cross_section=bool(get_value('generate_cross_section', True)),
            generate_foundation=bool(get_value('generate_foundation', True)),
            generate_reinforcement=bool(get_value('generate_reinforcement', True))
        )


@dataclass
class ProcessingResult:
    """Result of processing a single bridge"""
    bridge_id: str
    bridge_name: str
    status: ProcessingStatus
    output_files: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    error_message: str = ""
    processing_time: float = 0.0
    warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
        return {
            'bridge_id': self.bridge_id,
            'bridge_name': self.bridge_name,
            'status': self.status.value,
            'output_files': json.dumps(self.output_files),
            'error_message': self.error_message,
            'processing_time': self.processing_time,
            'warnings': json.dumps(self.warnings)
        }


@dataclass
class BatchConfig:
    """Configuration for batch processing"""
    # Input/Output
    input_excel_path: str
    output_base_directory: str
    
    # Processing
    max_workers: int = 4
    use_parallel: bool = True
    continue_on_error: bool = True
    
    # Validation
    validate_before_process: bool = True
    skip_invalid: bool = True
    
    # Output organization
    organize_by_chainage: bool = True
    create_subfolders: bool = True
    copy_to_master: bool = True
    
    # Logging
    log_level: str = "INFO"
    save_detailed_log: bool = True
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        """Create output directory"""
        Path(self.output_base_directory).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


class BridgeValidator:
    """Validate bridge input data"""
    
    VALIDATION_RULES = {
        'bridge_id': {
            'required': True,
            'type': str,
            'min_length': 3,
            'max_length': 20
        },
        'bridge_name': {
            'required': True,
            'type': str,
            'min_length': 3
        },
        'chainage': {
            'required': True,
            'type': (int, float),
            'min': 0,
            'max': 9999
        },
        'span_lengths': {
            'required': True,
            'type': list,
            'min_items': 1,
            'max_items': 20,
            'item_min': 3.0,
            'item_max': 200.0
        },
        'deck_width': {
            'required': True,
            'type': (int, float),
            'min': 3.0,
            'max': 30.0
        },
        'skew_angle': {
            'required': False,
            'type': (int, float),
            'min': 0,
            'max': 60
        }
    }
    
    @classmethod
    def validate(cls, bridge: BridgeInputData) -> Tuple[bool, List[str]]:
        """
        Validate bridge data
        Returns: (is_valid, list_of_errors)
        """
        errors = []
        
        # Check required fields
        if not bridge.bridge_id:
            errors.append("Bridge ID is required")
        
        if not bridge.bridge_name:
            errors.append("Bridge name is required")
        
        if bridge.chainage < 0:
            errors.append("Chainage must be positive")
        
        # Validate span lengths
        if not bridge.span_lengths:
            errors.append("At least one span length is required")
        else:
            for i, span in enumerate(bridge.span_lengths):
                if span < 3.0:
                    errors.append(f"Span {i+1} length {span}m is too short (min 3m)")
                if span > 200.0:
                    errors.append(f"Span {i+1} length {span}m is too long (max 200m)")
        
        # Validate deck width
        if bridge.deck_width < 3.0:
            errors.append(f"Deck width {bridge.deck_width}m is too narrow")
        if bridge.deck_width > 30.0:
            errors.append(f"Deck width {bridge.deck_width}m is too wide")
        
        # Validate skew angle
        if bridge.skew_angle < 0 or bridge.skew_angle > 60:
            errors.append(f"Skew angle {bridge.skew_angle}° is out of range (0-60°)")
        
        # Check total length
        total_length = sum(bridge.span_lengths)
        if total_length > 1000:
            errors.append(f"Total length {total_length}m exceeds maximum (1000m)")
        
        # Warnings (non-fatal)
        warnings = []
        if bridge.skew_angle > 45:
            warnings.append(f"High skew angle ({bridge.skew_angle}°) - check bearing details")
        
        if len(bridge.span_lengths) > 10:
            warnings.append(f"Many spans ({len(bridge.span_lengths)}) - consider expansion joints")
        
        return len(errors) == 0, errors + warnings


class BatchProcessor:
    """
    Main batch processor for multiple bridges
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: BatchConfig):
        self.config = config
        self.results: List[ProcessingResult] = []
        self.logger = self._setup_logging()
        
        # Statistics
        self.stats = {
            'total': 0,
            'completed': 0,
            'failed': 0,
            'skipped': 0,
            'total_time': 0.0
        }
    
    def _setup_logging(self) -> logging.Logger:
        """Setup logging"""
        logger = logging.getLogger('BatchProcessor')
        logger.setLevel(getattr(logging, self.config.log_level))
        
        # Console handler
        console = logging.StreamHandler()
        console.setLevel(logging.INFO)
        formatter = logging.Formatter(
            '%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s',
            datefmt='%H:%M:%S'
        )
        console.setFormatter(formatter)
        logger.addHandler(console)
        
        # File handler
        if self.config.save_detailed_log:
            log_file = os.path.join(
                self.config.output_base_directory,
                'batch_processing.log'
            )
            file_handler = logging.FileHandler(log_file)
            file_handler.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)
            file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
            logger.addHandler(file_handler)
        
        return logger
    
    def read_excel_input(self) -> List[BridgeInputData]:
        """Read bridge data from Excel file"""
        self.logger.info(f"Reading Excel file: {self.config.input_excel_path}")
        
        try:
            # Read bridge list sheet
            df = pd.read_excel(
                self.config.input_excel_path,
                sheet_name='BRIDGE_LIST'
            )
            
            self.logger.info(f"Found {len(df)} bridges in Excel")
            
            # Define column mapping (flexible column names)
            column_mapping = self._detect_columns(df.columns)
            
            bridges = []
            for idx, row in df.iterrows():
                try:
                    bridge = BridgeInputData.from_excel_row(row, column_mapping)
                    bridge.bridge_id = f"{bridge.bridge_id}_{idx+1:03d}"
                    bridges.append(bridge)
                except Exception as e:
                    self.logger.error(f"Error parsing row {idx+2}: {str(e)}")
            
            self.stats['total'] = len(bridges)
            return bridges
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Failed to read Excel: {str(e)}")
            raise
    
    def _detect_columns(self, columns: pd.Index) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """Detect column names with flexible matching"""
        column_mapping = {}
        
        # Common variations
        mappings = {
            'bridge_id': ['bridge_id', 'bridge id', 'id', 'bridge_no', 'bridge number'],
            'bridge_name': ['bridge_name', 'bridge name', 'name', 'bridge'],
            'chainage': ['chainage', 'chainage_km', 'ch', 'km'],
            'span_lengths': ['span_lengths', 'span lengths', 'spans', 'span'],
            'deck_width': ['deck_width', 'deck width', 'width', 'carriageway'],
            'skew_angle': ['skew_angle', 'skew', 'skew angle', 'angle']
        }
        
        for standard, variations in mappings.items():
            for col in columns:
                col_lower = str(col).lower().strip()
                if col_lower in [v.lower() for v in variations]:
                    column_mapping[standard] = col
                    break
        
        self.logger.info(f"Detected columns: {column_mapping}")
        return column_mapping
    
    def validate_all(self, bridges: List[BridgeInputData]) -> List[BridgeInputData]:
        """Validate all bridges"""
        self.logger.info("Validating bridges...")
        
        valid_bridges = []
        
        for bridge in bridges:
            is_valid, messages = BridgeValidator.validate(bridge)
            
            if is_valid:
                bridge.status = ProcessingStatus.PENDING
                valid_bridges.append(bridge)
                self.logger.info(f"✓ {bridge.bridge_id}: Valid")
            else:
                bridge.status = ProcessingStatus.SKIPPED
                bridge.error_message = "; ".join(messages)
                
                if self.config.skip_invalid:
                    self.logger.warning(f"✗ {bridge.bridge_id}: Invalid - {messages[0]}")
                    self.stats['skipped'] += 1
                else:
                    valid_bridges.append(bridge)
                    self.logger.warning(f"⚠ {bridge.bridge_id}: Invalid but including")
        
        self.logger.info(f"Validation complete: {len(valid_bridges)} valid, "
                        f"{self.stats['skipped']} skipped")
        
        return valid_bridges
    
    def process_single_bridge(self, bridge: BridgeInputData) -> ProcessingResult:
        """
        Process a single bridge
        This is the worker function for parallel processing
        """
        start_time = time.time()
        result = ProcessingResult(
            bridge_id=bridge.bridge_id,
            bridge_name=bridge.bridge_name,
            status=ProcessingStatus.PROCESSING
        )
        
        try:
            # Import here to avoid circular imports
            from skew_bridge_engine import create_skew_bridge
            from plan_view_drawing import PlanViewDrawing
            from longitudinal_section_drawing import LongitudinalSectionDrawing
            from cross_section_drawing import CrossSectionDrawing
            from foundation_drawing import FoundationDrawing
            from reinforcement_core import BridgeReinforcementSchedule
            
            # Create bridge model
            bridge_model = create_skew_bridge(
                name=bridge.bridge_name,
                chainage=bridge.chainage,
                span_lengths=bridge.span_lengths,
                deck_width=bridge.deck_width,
                skew_angle=bridge.skew_angle,
                skew_direction=bridge.skew_direction
            )
            
            # Create output directory for this bridge
            if self.config.organize_by_chainage:
                folder_name = f"CH_{bridge.chainage:06.3f}_{bridge.bridge_id}"
            else:
                folder_name = bridge.bridge_id
            
            bridge_output_dir = os.path.join(
                self.config.output_base_directory,
                folder_name
            )
            os.makedirs(bridge_output_dir, exist_ok=True)
            
            output_files = []
            
            # Generate Plan View
            if bridge.generate_plan:
                plan_drawing = PlanViewDrawing(bridge_model.to_params())
                plan_file = os.path.join(bridge_output_dir, "01_PLAN_VIEW.dxf")
                plan_drawing.generate(plan_file)
                output_files.append(plan_file)
            
            # Generate Longitudinal Section
            if bridge.generate_long_section:
                # Create longitudinal profile from bridge
                from longitudinal_section_core import LongitudinalProfile
                long_profile = LongitudinalProfile.from_bridge_model(bridge_model)
                long_drawing = LongitudinalSectionDrawing(long_profile)
                long_file = os.path.join(bridge_output_dir, "02_LONGITUDINAL_SECTION.dxf")
                long_drawing.generate(long_file)
                output_files.append(long_file)
            
            # Generate Cross Section
            if bridge.generate_cross_section:
                from cross_section_core import CrossSectionModel
                cross_model = CrossSectionModel.from_bridge_model(bridge_model)
                cross_drawing = CrossSectionDrawing(cross_model)
                cross_file = os.path.join(bridge_output_dir, "03_CROSS_SECTION.dxf")
                cross_drawing.generate(cross_file)
                output_files.append(cross_file)
            
            # Generate Foundation Details
            if bridge.generate_foundation:
                from foundation_core import FoundationModel
                for support in bridge_model.supports:
                    foundation = FoundationModel.from_support(support)
                    foundation_drawing = FoundationDrawing(foundation)
                    found_file = os.path.join(
                        bridge_output_dir,
                        f"04_FOUNDATION_{support.support_type}_{support.support_number}.dxf"
                    )
                    foundation_drawing.generate(found_file)
                    output_files.append(found_file)
            
            # Generate Reinforcement Schedule
            if bridge.generate_reinforcement:
                schedule = BridgeReinforcementSchedule.from_bridge_model(bridge_model)
                reinf_file = os.path.join(bridge_output_dir, "05_REINFORCEMENT.xlsx")
                schedule.export_to_excel(reinf_file)
                output_files.append(reinf_file)
            
            # Create bridge info JSON
            info = {
                'bridge_id': bridge.bridge_id,
                'bridge_name': bridge.bridge_name,
                'chainage': bridge.chainage,
                'span_lengths': bridge.span_lengths,
                'deck_width': bridge.deck_width,
                'skew_angle': bridge.skew_angle,
                'output_files': output_files,
                'generated_at': datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
            
            info_file = os.path.join(bridge_output_dir, "bridge_info.json")
            with open(info_file, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(info, f, indent=2)
            
            result.status = ProcessingStatus.COMPLETED
            result.output_files = output_files
            
        except Exception as e:
            result.status = ProcessingStatus.FAILED
            result.error_message = str(e)
            result.warnings.append(traceback.format_exc())
        
        finally:
            result.processing_time = time.time() - start_time
        
        return result
    
    def process_all(self, bridges: List[BridgeInputData]) -> List[ProcessingResult]:
        """Process all bridges"""
        self.logger.info(f"Processing {len(bridges)} bridges...")
        
        start_time = time.time()
        
        if self.config.use_parallel and len(bridges) > 1:
            # Parallel processing
            results = self._process_parallel(bridges)
        else:
            # Sequential processing
            results = self._process_sequential(bridges)
        
        self.stats['total_time'] = time.time() - start_time
        
        # Update statistics
        for result in results:
            if result.status == ProcessingStatus.COMPLETED:
                self.stats['completed'] += 1
            elif result.status == ProcessingStatus.FAILED:
                self.stats['failed'] += 1
        
        self.results = results
        return results
    
    def _process_sequential(self, bridges: List[BridgeInputData]) -> List[ProcessingResult]:
        """Process bridges sequentially"""
        results = []
        
        for i, bridge in enumerate(bridges, 1):
            self.logger.info(f"[{i}/{len(bridges)}] Processing {bridge.bridge_id}...")
            
            result = self.process_single_bridge(bridge)
            results.append(result)
            
            status_icon = "✓" if result.status == ProcessingStatus.COMPLETED else "✗"
            self.logger.info(f"{status_icon} {bridge.bridge_id}: "
                           f"{result.status.value} "
                           f"({result.processing_time:.1f}s)")
        
        return results
    
    def _process_parallel(self, bridges: List[BridgeInputData]) -> List[ProcessingResult]:
        """Process bridges in parallel using process pool"""
        results = [None] * len(bridges)
        
        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=self.config.max_workers) as executor:
            # Submit all tasks
            future_to_index = {
                executor.submit(self.process_single_bridge, bridge): i 
                for i, bridge in enumerate(bridges)
            }
            
            # Collect results as they complete
            completed = 0
            for future in as_completed(future_to_index):
                idx = future_to_index[future]
                bridge = bridges[idx]
                
                try:
                    result = future.result()
                    results[idx] = result
                except Exception as e:
                    results[idx] = ProcessingResult(
                        bridge_id=bridge.bridge_id,
                        bridge_name=bridge.bridge_name,
                        status=ProcessingStatus.FAILED,
                        error_message=str(e)
                    )
                
                completed += 1
                status_icon = "✓" if results[idx].status == ProcessingStatus.COMPLETED else "✗"
                self.logger.info(f"[{completed}/{len(bridges)}] {status_icon} "
                               f"{bridge.bridge_id}: {results[idx].status.value}")
        
        return results
    
    def generate_summary_report(self):
        """Generate summary Excel report"""
        self.logger.info("Generating summary report...")
        
        # Create summary DataFrame
        summary_data = []
        for result in self.results:
            summary_data.append({
                'Bridge ID': result.bridge_id,
                'Bridge Name': result.bridge_name,
                'Status': result.status.value,
                'Processing Time (s)': round(result.processing_time, 2),
                'Output Files': len(result.output_files),
                'Error Message': result.error_message[:100] if result.error_message else ""
            })
        
        df_summary = pd.DataFrame(summary_data)
        
        # Statistics
        stats_data = {
            'Metric': ['Total Bridges', 'Completed', 'Failed', 'Skipped', 
                      'Total Time (s)', 'Average Time (s)', 'Output Directory'],
            'Value': [
                self.stats['total'],
                self.stats['completed'],
                self.stats['failed'],
                self.stats['skipped'],
                round(self.stats['total_time'], 2),
                round(self.stats['total_time'] / max(self.stats['total'], 1), 2),
                self.config.output_base_directory
            ]
        }
        df_stats = pd.DataFrame(stats_data)
        
        # Export to Excel
        summary_file = os.path.join(
            self.config.output_base_directory,
            '00_BATCH_SUMMARY.xlsx'
        )
        
        with pd.ExcelWriter(summary_file, engine='openpyxl') as writer:
            df_stats.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='STATISTICS', index=False)
            df_summary.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='BRIDGE_STATUS', index=False)
            
            # Add pivot table
            pivot = df_summary.pivot_table(
                index='Status',
                values='Bridge ID',
                aggfunc='count'
            )
            pivot.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='STATUS_SUMMARY')
        
        self.logger.info(f"Summary report saved: {summary_file}")
        return summary_file
    
    def generate_processing_log(self):
        """Generate detailed processing log"""
        log_file = os.path.join(
            self.config.output_base_directory,
            'processing_log.json'
        )
        
        log_data = {
            'config': {
                'input_excel': self.config.input_excel_path,
                'output_directory': self.config.output_base_directory,
                'max_workers': self.config.max_workers,
                'use_parallel': self.config.use_parallel
            },
            'statistics': self.stats,
            'results': [r.to_dict() for r in self.results],
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        with open(log_file, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(log_data, f, indent=2)
        
        return log_file
    
    def run(self):
        """Run complete batch processing"""
        self.logger.info("=" * 80)
        self.logger.info("BRIDGE BATCH PROCESSING STARTED")
        self.logger.info("=" * 80)
        
        try:
            # Step 1: Read input
            bridges = self.read_excel_input()
            
            if not bridges:
                self.logger.error("No bridges found in Excel file")
                return
            
            # Step 2: Validate
            if self.config.validate_before_process:
                bridges = self.validate_all(bridges)
            
            if not bridges:
                self.logger.error("No valid bridges to process")
                return
            
            # Step 3: Process
            results = self.process_all(bridges)
            
            # Step 4: Generate reports
            self.generate_summary_report()
            self.generate_processing_log()
            
            # Final summary
            self.logger.info("=" * 80)
            self.logger.info("BATCH PROCESSING COMPLETE")
            self.logger.info("=" * 80)
            self.logger.info(f"Total: {self.stats['total']}")
            self.logger.info(f"Completed: {self.stats['completed']} ✓")
            self.logger.info(f"Failed: {self.stats['failed']} ✗")
            self.logger.info(f"Skipped: {self.stats['skipped']} ⚠")
            self.logger.info(f"Total Time: {self.stats['total_time']:.1f}s")
            self.logger.info(f"Output: {self.config.output_base_directory}")
            self.logger.info("=" * 80)
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Batch processing failed: {str(e)}")
            raise


class BatchProcessorCLI:
    """Command-line interface for batch processor"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def main():
        """Main entry point"""
        import argparse
        
        parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
            description='Batch Process Bridge Drawings from Excel'
        )
        
        parser.add_argument(
            '--input', '-i',
            required=True,
            help='Input Excel file path'
        )
        
        parser.add_argument(
            '--output', '-o',
            required=True,
            help='Output directory path'
        )
        
        parser.add_argument(
            '--workers', '-w',
            type=int,
            default=4,
            help='Number of parallel workers (default: 4)'
        )
        
        parser.add_argument(
            '--sequential', '-s',
            action='store_true',
            help='Process sequentially (no parallelism)'
        )
        
        parser.add_argument(
            '--continue-on-error',
            action='store_true',
            default=True,
            help='Continue processing if one bridge fails'
        )
        
        args = parser.parse_args()
        
        # Create config
        config = BatchConfig(
            input_excel_path=args.input,
            output_base_directory=args.output,
            max_workers=args.workers,
            use_parallel=not args.sequential,
            continue_on_error=args.continue_on_error
        )
        
        # Run processor
        processor = BatchProcessor(config)
        processor.run()


# For direct execution
if __name__ == "__main__":
    BatchProcessorCLI.main()
```
